




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

khuddakanikāyai


thairagāthāpāḷi

nidānagāthā

sīhānaṃva  nadantānaṃ, dāṭhīnaṃ girigabbharai|

suṇātha bhāvitattānaṃ, gāthā atthūpanāyikā attūpanāyikā (sī॰ ka॰)||

yathānāmā yathāgauttā, yathādhammavihārinau|

yathādhimuttā sappañ‍ñā, vihariṃsu atanditā||

tattha tattha vipassitvā, phusitvā ac‍cutaṃ padaṃ|

katantaṃ pac‍cavaikkhantā, imamatthamabhāsisuṃ||


1. aikakanipātau

1. paṭhamavaggau

1. subhūtitthairagāthā

1.

 ‘‘chan‍nā  mai kuṭikā sukhā nivātā, vassa daiva yathāsukhaṃ|

cittaṃ mai susamāhitaṃ vimuttaṃ, ātāpī viharāmi vassa daivā’’ti||

itthaṃ sudaṃ itthaṃ sumaṃ (ka॰ aṭṭha॰) āyasmā subhūtitthairau gāthaṃ abhāsitthāti|

2. mahākauṭṭhikatthairagāthā

2.

 ‘‘upasantau  uparatau, mantabhāṇī anuddhatau|

dhunāti pāpakai dhammai, dumapattaṃva mālutau’’ti||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau mahākauṭṭhitau (sī॰ syā॰) thairau gāthaṃ abhāsitthāti|

3. kaṅkhāraivatatthairagāthā

3.

 ‘‘pañ‍ñaṃ  imaṃ passa tathāgatānaṃ, aggi yathā paj‍jalitau nisīthai|

ālaukadā cakkhudadā bhavanti, yai āgatānaṃ vinayanti kaṅkha’’nti||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kaṅkhāraivatau thairau gāthaṃ abhāsitthāti|

4. puṇṇatthairagāthā

4.

 ‘‘sambhiraiva samāsaitha, paṇḍitaihatthadassibhi|

atthaṃ mahantaṃ gambhīraṃ, duddasaṃ nipuṇaṃ aṇuṃ|

dhīrā samadhigacchanti, appamattā vicakkhaṇā’’ti||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā puṇṇau mantāṇiputtau mantāniputtau (syā॰ ka॰) thairau gāthaṃ abhāsitthāti|

5. dabbatthairagāthā

5.

 ‘‘yau  duddamiyau damaina dantau, dabbau santusitau vitiṇṇakaṅkhau|

vijitāvī apaitabhairavau hi, dabbau sau parinibbutau ṭhitattau’’ti||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dabbau thairau gāthaṃ abhāsitthāti|

6. sītavaniyatthairagāthā

6.

 ‘‘yau  sītavanaṃ upagā bhikkhu, aikau santusitau samāhitattau|

vijitāvī apaitalaumahaṃsau, rakkhaṃ kāyagatāsatiṃ dhitimā’’ti||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sītavaniyau thairau gāthaṃ abhāsitthāti|

7. bhal‍liyatthairagāthā

7.

 ‘‘yaupānudī  mac‍curājassa sainaṃ, naḷasaituṃva sudubbalaṃ mahaughau|

vijitāvī apaitabhairavau hi, dantau sau parinibbutau ṭhitattau’’ti||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bhal‍liyau thairau gāthaṃ abhāsitthāti|

8. vīratthairagāthā

8.

 ‘‘yau duddamiyau damaina dantau, vīrau santusitau vitiṇṇakaṅkhau|

vijitāvī apaitalaumahaṃsau, vīrau sau parinibbutau ṭhitattau’’ti||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vīrau thairau gāthaṃ abhāsitthāti|

9. pilindavacchatthairagāthā

9.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ na durāgataṃ nāpagataṃ (sī॰ syā॰), nayidaṃ dumantitaṃ mama|

saṃvibhattaisu dhammaisu, yaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ tadupāgami’’nti||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pilindavacchau pilindivacchau (sī॰) thairau gāthaṃ abhāsitthāti|

10. puṇṇamāsatthairagāthā

10.

 ‘‘vihari  apaikkhaṃ idha vā huraṃ vā, yau vaidagū samitau yatattau|

sabbaisu dhammaisu anūpalittau, laukassa jañ‍ñā udayabbayañ‍cā’’ti||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā puṇṇamāsau thairau gāthaṃ abhāsitthāti|

vaggau paṭhamau niṭṭhitau|

tassuddānaṃ –

subhūti  kauṭṭhikau thairau, kaṅkhāraivatasammatau|

mantāṇiputtau dabbau ca, sītavaniyau ca bhal‍liyau|

vīrau pilindavacchau ca, puṇṇamāsau tamaunudauti||

2. dutiyavaggau

1. cūḷavacchatthairagāthā

11.

 ‘‘pāmauj‍jabahulau bhikkhu, dhammai buddhappavaiditai|

adhigacchai padaṃ santaṃ, saṅkhārūpasamaṃ sukha’’nti||

… cūḷavacchau cūlagavacchau (sī॰) thairau…|

2. mahāvacchatthairagāthā

12.

 ‘‘pañ‍ñābalī sīlavatūpapan‍nau, samāhitau jhānaratau satīmā|

yadatthiyaṃ bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jamānau, kaṅkhaitha kālaṃ idha vītarāgau’’ti||

… mahāvacchau mahāgavacchau (sī॰) thairau…|

3. vanavacchatthairagāthā

13.

 ‘‘nīlabbhavaṇṇā  rucirā, sītavārī sucindharā|

indagaupakasañchan‍nā, tai sailā ramayanti ma’’nti||

… vanavacchau thairau…|

4. sivakasāmaṇairagāthā

14.

 ‘‘upajjhāyau  maṃ avaca, itau gacchāma sīvaka|

gāmai  mai vasati kāyau, arañ‍ñaṃ mai gatau manau|

saimānakaupi gacchāmi, natthi saṅgau vijānata’’nti||

… sivakau sāmaṇairau…|

5. kuṇḍadhānatthairagāthā

15.

 ‘‘pañ‍ca chindai pañ‍ca jahai, pañ‍ca cuttari bhāvayai|

pañ‍casaṅgātigau bhikkhu, aughatiṇṇauti vuc‍catī’’ti||

… kuṇḍadhānau thairau…|

6. bailaṭṭhasīsatthairagāthā

16.

 ‘‘yathāpi bhaddau ājañ‍ñau, naṅgalāvattanī sikhī|

gacchati appakasiraina, aivaṃ rattindivā mama|

gacchanti appakasiraina, sukhai laddhai nirāmisai’’ti||

… bailaṭṭhasīsau thairau…|

7. dāsakatthairagāthā

17.

 ‘‘middhī  yadā hauti mahagghasau ca, niddāyitā samparivattasāyī|

mahāvarāhauva nivāpapuṭṭhau, punappunaṃ gabbhamupaiti mandau’’ti||

… dāsakau thairau…|

8. siṅgālapitutthairagāthā

18.

 ‘‘ahu  buddhassa dāyādau, bhikkhu bhaisakaḷāvanai|

kaivalaṃ aṭṭhisañ‍ñāya, apharī pathaviṃ paṭhaviṃ (sī॰ syā॰) imaṃ|

mañ‍ñaihaṃ kāmarāgaṃ sau, khippamaiva pahissatī’’ti pahīyabhi (sabbattha pāḷiyaṃ)||

… siṅgālapitā sīgālapitā (sī॰) thairau…|

9. kulatthairagāthā

19.

 dha॰ pa॰ 80, 145 dhammapadaipi ‘‘udakaṃ  hi nayanti naittikā, usukārā namayanti damayanti (ka॰) taijanaṃ|

dāruṃ  namayanti tacchakā, attānaṃ damayanti subbatā’’ti||

… kulau kuṇḍalau (sī॰), kuḷau (syā॰ ka॰) thairau…|

10. ajitatthairagāthā

20.

 ‘‘maraṇai mai bhayaṃ natthi, nikanti natthi jīvitai|

sandaihaṃ nikkhipissāmi, sampajānau paṭissatau’’ti patissatauti (sī॰ syā॰)||

… ajitau thairau …|

vaggau dutiyau niṭṭhitau|

tassuddānaṃ –

cūḷavacchau mahāvacchau, vanavacchau ca sīvakau|

kuṇḍadhānau ca bailaṭṭhi, dāsakau ca tataupari|

siṅgālapitikau thairau, kulau ca ajitau dasāti||

3. tatiyavaggau

1. nigraudhatthairagāthā

21.

 ‘‘nāhaṃ bhayassa bhāyāmi, satthā nau amatassa kauvidau|

yattha bhayaṃ nāvatiṭṭhati, taina maggaina vajanti bhikkhavau’’ti||

… nigraudhau thairau…|

2. cittakatthairagāthā

22.

 ‘‘nīlā  sugīvā sikhinau, maurā kārambhiyaṃ kāraṃviyaṃ (sī॰), kāraviyaṃ (syā॰) abhinadanti|

tai sītavātakīḷitā sītavātakadditakalitā (sī॰), sītavātakalitā (syā॰), suttaṃ jhāyaṃ jhānaṃ (syā॰), jhāyiṃ (?) nibaudhaintī’’ti||

… cittakau thairau…|

3. gausālatthairagāthā

23.

 ‘‘ahaṃ  khau vaiḷugumbasmiṃ, bhutvāna madhupāyasaṃ|

padakkhiṇaṃ sammasantau, khandhānaṃ udayabbayaṃ|

sānuṃ paṭigamissāmi, vivaikamanubrūhaya’’nti||

… gausālau thairau…|

4. sugandhatthairagāthā

24.

 ‘‘anuvassikau pabbajitau, passa dhammasudhammataṃ|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

… sugandhau thairau…|

5. nandiyatthairagāthā

25.

 ‘‘aubhāsajātaṃ phalagaṃ, cittaṃ yassa abhiṇhasau|

tādisaṃ bhikkhumāsaj‍ja, kaṇha dukkhaṃ nigacchasī’’ti||

… nandiyau thairau…|

6. abhayatthairagāthā

26.

 ‘‘sutvā subhāsitaṃ vācaṃ, buddhassādic‍cabandhunau|

pac‍cabyadhiṃ hi nipuṇaṃ, vālaggaṃ usunā yathā’’ti||

… abhayau thairau…|

7. laumasakaṅgiyatthairagāthā

27.

 ‘‘dabbaṃ  kusaṃ pauṭakilaṃ, usīraṃ muñ‍japabbajaṃ|

urasā panudissāmi, vivaikamanubrūhaya’’nti||

… laumasakaṅgiyau thairau…|

8. jambugāmikaputtatthairagāthā

28.

 ‘‘kac‍ci  nau vatthapasutau, kac‍ci nau bhūsanāratau|

kac‍ci sīlamayaṃ gandhaṃ, kiṃ tvaṃ vāyasi kac‍ci sīlamayaṃ gandhaṃ, tvaṃ vāsi (syā॰) naitarā pajā’’ti||

… jambugāmikaputtau thairau…|

9. hāritatthairagāthā

29.

 ‘‘samun‍namayamattānaṃ, usukārauva taijanaṃ|

cittaṃ  ujuṃ karitvāna, avij‍jaṃ bhinda hāritā’’ti||

… hāritau thairau…|

10. uttiyatthairagāthā

30.

 ‘‘ābādhai mai samuppan‍nai, sati mai udapaj‍jatha|

ābādhau mai samuppan‍nau, kālau mai nappamaj‍jitu’’nti||

… uttiyau thairau…|

vaggau tatiyau niṭṭhitau|

tassuddānaṃ –

nigraudhau cittakau thairau, gausālathairau sugandhau|

nandiyau abhayau thairau, thairau laumasakaṅgiyau|

jambugāmikaputtau ca, hāritau uttiyau isīti||

4. catutthavaggau

1. gahvaratīriyatthairagāthā

31.

 ‘‘phuṭṭhau  ḍaṃsaihi makasaihi, arañ‍ñasmiṃ brahāvanai|

nāgau saṃgāmasīsaiva, satau tatrādhivāsayai’’ti||

… gahvaratīriyau thairau…|

2. suppiyatthairagāthā

32.

 ‘‘ajaraṃ  jīramānaina, tappamānaina nibbutiṃ|

nimiyaṃ nimmissaṃ (sī॰), nirāmisaṃ (syā॰), niminaiyyaṃ (?) paramaṃ santiṃ, yaugakkhaimaṃ anuttara’’nti||

… suppiyau thairau…|

3. saupākatthairagāthā

33.

 ‘‘yathāpi  aikaputtasmiṃ, piyasmiṃ kusalī siyā|

aivaṃ sabbaisu pāṇaisu, sabbattha kusalau siyā’’ti||

… saupākau thairau…|

4. pausiyatthairagāthā

34.

 ‘‘anāsan‍navarā aitā, nic‍camaiva vijānatā|

gāmā arañ‍ñamāgamma, tatau gaihaṃ upāvisi upāvisiṃ (sī॰)|

tatau uṭṭhāya pak‍kāmi, anāmantaitvā anāmantiya (sī॰) pausiyau’’ti||

… pausiyau thairau…|

5. sāmañ‍ñakānitthairagāthā

35.

 ‘‘sukhaṃ sukhatthau labhatai tadācaraṃ, kittiñ‍ca pappauti yasassa vaḍḍhati|

yau ariyamaṭṭhaṅgikamañ‍jasaṃ ujuṃ, bhāvaiti maggaṃ amatassa pattiyā’’ti||

… sāmañ‍ñakānitthairau…|

6. kumāputtatthairagāthā

36.

 ‘‘sādhu  sutaṃ sādhu caritakaṃ, sādhu sadā anikaitavihārau|

atthapucchanaṃ padakkhiṇakammaṃ, aitaṃ sāmañ‍ñamakiñ‍canassā’’ti||

… kumāputtau thairau…|

7. kumāputtasahāyakatthairagāthā

37.

 ‘‘nānājanapadaṃ yanti, vicarantā asañ‍ñatā|

samādhiñ‍ca virādhainti, kiṃsu raṭṭhacariyā karissati|

tasmā vinaiyya sārambhaṃ, jhāyaiyya apurakkhatau’’ti||

… kumāputtatthairassa sahāyakau thairau…|

8. gavampatitthairagāthā

38.

 ‘‘yau  iddhiyā sarabhuṃ aṭṭhapaisi, sau gavampati asitau anaijau|

taṃ sabbasaṅgātigataṃ mahāmuniṃ, daivā namassanti bhavassa pāragu’’nti||

… gavampatitthairau…|

9. tissatthairagāthā

39.

 saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.21, 97‘‘sattiyā viya aumaṭṭhau, ḍayhamānauva ḍayhamānaiva (sabbattha) matthakai|

kāmarāgappahānāya, satau bhikkhu paribbajai’’ti||

… tissau thairau…|

10. vaḍḍhamānatthairagāthā

40.

 ‘‘sattiyā  viya aumaṭṭhau, ḍayhamānauva matthakai|

bhavarāgappahānāya, satau bhikkhu paribbajai’’ti||

… vaḍḍhamānau thairau…|

vaggau catutthau niṭṭhitau|

tassuddānaṃ –

gahvaratīriyau  suppiyau, saupākau caiva pausiyau|

sāmañ‍ñakāni kumāputtau, kumāputtasahāyakau|

gavampati tissatthairau, vaḍḍhamānau mahāyasauti||

5. pañ‍camavaggau

1. sirivaḍḍhatthairagāthā

41.

 ‘‘vivaramanupatanti  vij‍jutā, vaibhārassa ca paṇḍavassa ca|

nagavivaragatau ca jhāyati, puttau appaṭimassa tādinau’’ti||

… sirivaḍḍhau thairau…|

2. khadiravaniyatthairagāthā

42.

 ‘‘cālai  upacālai sīsūpacālai ( ) (cālā upacālā, sīsūpacālā) (ka॰) patissatā paṭissatikā (syā॰ ka॰) nu khau viharatha|

āgatau vau vālaṃ viya vaidhī’’ti||

… khadiravaniyau thairau…|

3. sumaṅgalatthairagāthā

43.

 ‘‘sumuttikau sumuttikau sāhu, sumuttikaumhi tīhi khuj‍jakaihi|

asitāsu mayā naṅgalāsu, mayā khuddakuddālāsu mayā||

yadipi idhamaiva idhamaiva, atha vāpi alamaiva alamaiva|

jhāya sumaṅgala jhāya sumaṅgala, appamattau vihara sumaṅgalā’’ti||

… sumaṅgalau thairau…|

4. sānutthairagāthā

44.

 saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.239 ‘‘mataṃ vā amma raudanti, yau vā jīvaṃ na dissati|

jīvantaṃ maṃ amma passantī, kasmā maṃ amma raudasī’’ti||

… sānutthairau…|

5. ramaṇīyavihāritthairagāthā

45.

 ‘‘yathāpi  bhaddau ājañ‍ñau, khalitvā patitiṭṭhati|

aivaṃ  dassanasampan‍naṃ, sammāsambuddhasāvaka’’nti||

… ramaṇīyavihāritthairau…|

6. samiddhitthairagāthā

46.

 ‘‘saddhāyāhaṃ pabbajitau, agārasmānagāriyaṃ|

sati pañ‍ñā ca mai vuḍḍhā, cittañ‍ca susamāhitaṃ|

kāmaṃ karassu rūpāni, naiva maṃ byādhayissasī’’ti bādhayissasīti (sī॰), byāthayissasīti (?)||

… samiddhitthairau…|

7. uj‍jayatthairagāthā

47.

 ‘‘namau  tai buddha vīratthu, vippamuttausi sabbadhi|

tuyhāpadānai viharaṃ, viharāmi anāsavau’’ti||

… uj‍jayau thairau…|

8. sañ‍jayatthairagāthā

48.

 ‘‘yatau ahaṃ pabbajitau, agārasmānagāriyaṃ|

nābhijānāmi saṅkappaṃ, anariyaṃ dausasaṃhita’’nti||

… sañ‍jayau thairau…|

9. rāmaṇaiyyakatthairagāthā

49.

 ‘‘cihacihābhinaditai vihavihābhinaditai (sī॰ syā॰), sippikābhirutaihi ca|

na mai taṃ phandati cittaṃ, aikattanirataṃ hi mai’’ti||

… rāmaṇaiyyakau thairau…|

10. vimalatthairagāthā

50.

 ‘‘dharaṇī  ca siñ‍cati vāti, mālutau vij‍jutā carati nabhai|

upasamanti vitak‍kā, cittaṃ susamāhitaṃ mamā’’ti||

… vimalau thairau…|

vaggau pañ‍camau niṭṭhitau|

tassuddānaṃ –

sirīvaḍḍhau raivatau thairau, sumaṅgalau sānusavhayau|

ramaṇīyavihārī ca, samiddhiuj‍jayasañ‍jayā|

rāmaṇaiyyau ca sau thairau, vimalau ca raṇañ‍jahauti||

6. chaṭṭhavaggau

1. gaudhikatthairagāthā

51.

 ‘‘vassati  daivau yathā sugītaṃ, chan‍nā mai kuṭikā sukhā nivātā|

cittaṃ susamāhitañ‍ca mayhaṃ, atha cai patthayasi pavassa daivā’’ti||

… gaudhikau thairau…|

2. subāhutthairagāthā

52.

 ‘‘vassati daivau yathā sugītaṃ, chan‍nā mai kuṭikā sukhā nivātā|

cittaṃ susamāhitañ‍ca kāyai, atha cai patthayasi pavassa daivā’’ti||

… subāhutthairau…|

3. val‍liyatthairagāthā

53.

 ‘‘vassati daivau yathā sugītaṃ, chan‍nā mai kuṭikā sukhā nivātā|

tassaṃ viharāmi appamattau, atha cai patthayasi pavassa daivā’’ti||

… val‍liyau thairau…|

4. uttiyatthairagāthā

54.

 ‘‘vassati  daivau yathā sugītaṃ, chan‍nā mai kuṭikā sukhā nivātā|

tassaṃ viharāmi adutiyau, atha cai patthayasi pavassa daivā’’ti||

… uttiyau thairau…|

5. añ‍janavaniyatthairagāthā

55.

 ‘‘āsandiṃ  kuṭikaṃ katvā, augayha añ‍janaṃ vanaṃ|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

… añ‍janavaniyau thairau…|

6. kuṭivihāritthairagāthā

56.

 ‘‘kau  kuṭikāyaṃ bhikkhu kuṭikāyaṃ, vītarāgau susamāhitacittau|

aivaṃ jānāhi āvusau, amaughā tai kuṭikā katā’’ti||

… kuṭivihāritthairau…|

7. dutiyakuṭivihāritthairagāthā

57.

 ‘‘ayamāhu purāṇiyā kuṭi, añ‍ñaṃ patthayasai navaṃ kuṭiṃ|

āsaṃ kuṭiyā virājaya, dukkhā bhikkhu puna navā kuṭī’’ti||

… dutiyakuṭivihāritthairau…|

8. ramaṇīyakuṭikatthairagāthā

58.

 ‘‘ramaṇīyā mai kuṭikā, saddhādaiyyā manauramā|

na mai atthau kumārīhi, yaisaṃ atthau tahiṃ gacchatha nāriyau’’ti||

… ramaṇīyakuṭikau thairau…|

9. kausalavihāritthairagāthā

59.

 ‘‘saddhāyāhaṃ  pabbajitau, arañ‍ñai mai kuṭikā katā|

appamattau ca ātāpī, sampajānau patissatau’’ti paṭissatauti (ka॰)||

… kausalavihāritthairau…|

10. sīvalitthairagāthā

60.

 ‘‘tai mai ijjhiṃsu saṅkappā, yadatthau pāvisiṃ kuṭiṃ|

vij‍jāvimuttiṃ pac‍caisaṃ, mānānusayamuj‍jaha’’nti||

… sīvalitthairau…|

vaggau chaṭṭhau niṭṭhitau|

tassuddānaṃ –

gaudhikau  ca subāhu ca, val‍liyau uttiyau isi|

añ‍janavaniyau thairau, duvai kuṭivihārinau|

ramaṇīyakuṭikau ca, kausalavhayasīvalīti||

7. sattamavaggau

1. vappatthairagāthā

61.

 ‘‘passati  passau passantaṃ, apassantañ‍ca passati|

apassantau apassantaṃ, passantañ‍ca na passatī’’ti||

… vappau thairau…|

2. vaj‍jiputtatthairagāthā

62.

 ‘‘aikakā  mayaṃ arañ‍ñai viharāma, apaviddhaṃva vanasmiṃ dārukaṃ|

tassa mai bahukā pihayanti, nairayikā viya saggagāmina’’nti||

… vaj‍jiputtau thairau…|

3. pakkhatthairagāthā

63.

 ‘‘cutā  patanti patitā, giddhā ca punarāgatā|

kataṃ kic‍caṃ rataṃ rammaṃ, sukhainanvāgataṃ sukha’’nti||

… pakkhau thairau…|

4. vimalakauṇḍañ‍ñatthairagāthā

64.

 ‘‘dumavhayāya uppan‍nau, jātau paṇḍarakaitunā|

kaituhā  kaitunāyaiva, mahākaituṃ padhaṃsayī’’ti||

… vimalakauṇḍañ‍ñau thairau…|

5. ukkhaipakatavacchatthairagāthā

65.

 ‘‘ukkhaipakatavacchassa, saṅkalitaṃ bahūhi vassaihi|

taṃ bhāsati gahaṭṭhānaṃ, sunisin‍nau uḷārapāmauj‍jau’’ti||

… ukkhaipakatavacchau thairau…|

6. maighiyatthairagāthā

66.

 ‘‘anusāsi  mahāvīrau, sabbadhammāna pāragū|

tassāhaṃ dhammaṃ sutvāna, vihāsiṃ santikai satau|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

… maighiyau thairau…|

7. aikadhammasavanīyatthairagāthā

67.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

vikkhīṇau jātisaṃsārau, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti||

… aikadhammasavanīyau thairau…|

8. aikudāniyatthairagāthā

68.

 udā॰ 37; pāci॰ 153 ‘‘adhicaitasau appamaj‍jatau, muninau maunapathaisu sikkhatau|

saukā na bhavanti tādinau, upasantassa sadā satīmatau’’ti||

… aikudāniyau thairau…|

9. chan‍natthairagāthā

69.

 ‘‘sutvāna  dhammaṃ mahatau mahārasaṃ, sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇavaraina daisitaṃ|

maggaṃ papaj‍jiṃ papaj‍jaṃ (ka॰) amatassa pattiyā, sau yaugakkhaimassa pathassa kauvidau’’ti||

… chan‍nau thairau…|

10. puṇṇatthairagāthā

70.

 ‘‘sīlamaiva  idha aggaṃ, pañ‍ñavā pana uttamau|

manussaisu ca daivaisu, sīlapañ‍ñāṇatau jaya’’nti||

… puṇṇau thairau…|

vaggau sattamau niṭṭhitau|

tassuddānaṃ  –

vappau ca vaj‍jiputtau ca, pakkhau vimalakauṇḍañ‍ñau|

ukkhaipakatavacchau ca, maighiyau aikadhammikau|

aikudāniyachan‍nā ca, puṇṇatthairau mahabbalauti||

8. aṭṭhamavaggau

1. vacchapālatthairagāthā

71.

 ‘‘susukhumanipuṇatthadassinā, matikusalaina nivātavuttinā|

saṃsaivitavuddhasīlinā saṃsaivitabuddhasīlinā (ka॰), nibbānaṃ na hi taina dul‍labha’’nti||

… vacchapālau thairau…|

2. ātumatthairagāthā

72.

 ‘‘yathā  kaḷīrau susu vaḍḍhitaggau, dun‍nikkhamau hauti pasākhajātau|

aivaṃ ahaṃ bhariyāyānitāya, anumañ‍ñaṃ  maṃ pabbajitaumhi dānī’’ti||

… ātumau thairau…|

3. māṇavatthairagāthā

73.

 ‘‘jiṇṇañ‍ca disvā dukhitañ‍ca byādhitaṃ, matañ‍ca disvā gatamāyusaṅkhayaṃ|

tatau ahaṃ nikkhamitūna pabbajiṃ, pahāya kāmāni manauramānī’’ti||

… māṇavau thairau…|

4. suyāmanatthairagāthā

74.

 ‘‘kāmacchandau ca byāpādau, thinamiddhañ‍ca thīnamiddhañ‍ca (sī॰ syā॰) bhikkhunau|

uddhac‍caṃ vicikicchā ca, sabbasauva na vij‍jatī’’ti||

… suyāmanau thairau…|

5. susāradatthairagāthā

75.

 ‘‘sādhu suvihitāna dassanaṃ, kaṅkhā chij‍jati buddhi vaḍḍhati|

bālampi karaunti paṇḍitaṃ, tasmā sādhu sataṃ samāgamau’’ti||

… susāradau thairau…|

6. piyañ‍jahatthairagāthā

76.

 ‘‘uppatantaisu  nipatai, nipatantaisu uppatai|

vasai avasamānaisu, ramamānaisu nau ramai’’ti||

… piyañ‍jahau thairau…|

7. hatthārauhaputtatthairagāthā

77.

 ‘‘idaṃ  purai cittamacāri cārikaṃ, yainicchakaṃ yatthakāmaṃ yathāsukhaṃ|

tadaj‍jahaṃ niggahaissāmi yaunisau, hatthippabhin‍naṃ viya aṅkusaggahau’’ti||

… hatthārauhaputtau thairau…|

8. maiṇḍasiratthairagāthā

78.

 ‘‘anaikajātisaṃsāraṃ, sandhāvissaṃ anibbisaṃ|

tassa mai dukkhajātassa, dukkhakkhandhau aparaddhau’’ti||

… maiṇḍasirau thairau…|

9. rakkhitatthairagāthā

79.

 ‘‘sabbau rāgau pahīnau mai, sabbau dausau samūhatau|

sabbau mai vigatau mauhau, sītibhūtausmi nibbutau’’ti||

… rakkhitau thairau…|

10. uggatthairagāthā

80.

 ‘‘yaṃ  mayā pakataṃ kammaṃ, appaṃ vā yadi vā bahuṃ|

sabbamaitaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti||

… uggau thairau…|

vaggau aṭṭhamau niṭṭhitau|

tassuddānaṃ –

vacchapālau ca yau thairau, ātumau māṇavau isi|

suyāmanau susāradau, thairau yau ca piyañ‍jahau|

ārauhaputtau maiṇḍasirau, rakkhitau uggasavhayauti||

9. navamavaggau

1. samitiguttatthairagāthā

81.

 ‘‘yaṃ  mayā pakataṃ pāpaṃ, pubbai añ‍ñāsu jātisu|

idhaiva taṃ vaidanīyaṃ, vatthu añ‍ñaṃ na vij‍jatī’’ti||

… samitiguttau thairau…|

2. kassapatthairagāthā

82.

 ‘‘yaina  yaina subhikkhāni, sivāni abhayāni ca|

taina puttaka gacchassu, mā saukāpahatau bhavā’’ti||

… kassapau thairau…|

3. sīhatthairagāthā

83.

 ‘‘sīhappamattau vihara, rattindivamatanditau|

bhāvaihi kusalaṃ dhammaṃ, jaha sīghaṃ samussaya’’nti||

… sīhau thairau…|

4. nītatthairagāthā

84.

 ‘‘sabbarattiṃ  supitvāna, divā saṅgaṇikai ratau|

kudāssu nāma dummaidhau, dukkhassantaṃ karissatī’’ti||

… nītau thairau…|

5. sunāgatthairagāthā

85.

 ‘‘cittanimittassa kauvidau, pavivaikarasaṃ vijāniya|

jhāyaṃ nipakau patissatau, adhigacchaiyya sukhaṃ nirāmisa’’nti||

… sunāgau thairau…|

6. nāgitatthairagāthā

86.

 ‘‘itau  bahiddhā puthu añ‍ñavādinaṃ, maggau na nibbānagamau yathā ayaṃ|

itissu saṅghaṃ bhagavānusāsati, satthā sayaṃ pāṇitalaiva dassaya’’nti||

… nāgitau thairau…|

7. paviṭṭhatthairagāthā

87.

 ‘‘khandhā  diṭṭhā yathābhūtaṃ, bhavā sabbai padālitā|

vikkhīṇau jātisaṃsārau, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti||

… paviṭṭhau thairau…|

8. aj‍junatthairagāthā

88.

 ‘‘asakkhiṃ  vata attānaṃ, uddhātuṃ udakā thalaṃ|

vuyhamānau mahaughaiva, sac‍cāni paṭivijjhaha’’nti||

… aj‍junau thairau…|

9. (paṭhama)-daivasabhatthairagāthā

89.

 ‘‘uttiṇṇā paṅkapalipā, pātālā parivaj‍jitā|

muttau aughā ca ganthā ca, sabbai mānā visaṃhatā’’ti||

… daivasabhau thairau…|

10. sāmidattatthairagāthā

90.

 ‘‘pañ‍cakkhandhā pariñ‍ñātā, tiṭṭhanti chin‍namūlakā|

vikkhīṇau jātisaṃsārau, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti||

… sāmidattau thairau…|

vaggau navamau niṭṭhitau|

tassuddānaṃ –

thairau samitiguttau ca, kassapau sīhasavhayau|

nītau sunāgau nāgitau, paviṭṭhau aj‍junau isi|

daivasabhau ca yau thairau, sāmidattau mahabbalauti||

10. dasamavaggau

1. paripuṇṇakatthairagāthā

91.

 ‘‘na  tathā mataṃ satarasaṃ, sudhan‍naṃ yaṃ mayaj‍ja paribhuttaṃ|

aparimitadassinā gautamaina, buddhaina daisitau dhammau’’ti||

… paripuṇṇakau thairau…|

2. vijayatthairagāthā

92.

 ‘‘yassāsavā  parikkhīṇā, āhārai ca anissitau|

suñ‍ñatā animittau ca, vimaukkhau yassa gaucarau|

ākāsaiva sakuntānaṃ, padaṃ tassa duran‍naya’’nti||

… vijayau thairau…|

3. airakatthairagāthā

93.

 ‘‘dukkhā kāmā airaka, na sukhā kāmā airaka|

yau kāmai kāmayati, dukkhaṃ sau kāmayati airaka|

yau kāmai na kāmayati, dukkhaṃ sau na kāmayati airakā’’ti||

… airakau thairau…|

4. maittajitthairagāthā

94.

 ‘‘namau hi tassa bhagavatau, sakyaputtassa sirīmatau|

taināyaṃ aggappattaina, aggadhammau aggau dhammau (sī॰) sudaisitau’’ti||

… maittaji thairau…|

5. cakkhupālatthairagāthā

95.

 ‘‘andhauhaṃ hatanaittausmi, kantāraddhānapakkhandau pakkhan‍nau (sī॰), pak‍kantau (syā॰ sī॰ aṭṭha॰)|

sayamānaupi gacchissaṃ, na sahāyaina pāpainā’’ti||

… cakkhupālau thairau…|

6. khaṇḍasumanatthairagāthā

96.

 ‘‘aikapupphaṃ  cajitvāna, asīti asītiṃ (sī॰) vassakauṭiyau|

saggaisu paricāraitvā, saisakainamhi nibbutau’’ti||

… khaṇḍasumanau thairau…|

7. tissatthairagāthā

97.

 ‘‘hitvā satapalaṃ kaṃsaṃ, sauvaṇṇaṃ satarājikaṃ|

aggahiṃ mattikāpattaṃ, idaṃ dutiyābhisaicana’’nti||

… tissau thairau…|

8. abhayatthairagāthā

98.

 ‘‘rūpaṃ  disvā sati muṭṭhā, piyaṃ nimittaṃ manasikarautau|

sārattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca ajjhausa tiṭṭhati|

tassa vaḍḍhanti āsavā, bhavamūlaupagāminau’’ti bhavamūlā bhavagāminauti (sī॰ ka॰)||

… abhayau thairau…|

9. uttiyatthairagāthā

99.

 ‘‘saddaṃ sutvā sati muṭṭhā, piyaṃ nimittaṃ manasikarautau|

sārattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca ajjhausa tiṭṭhati|

tassa vaḍḍhanti āsavā, saṃsāraṃ upagāminau’’ti||

… uttiyau thairau…|

10. (dutiya)-daivasabhatthairagāthā

100.

 ‘‘sammappadhānasampan‍nau, satipaṭṭhānagaucarau|

vimuttikusumasañchan‍nau, parinibbissatyanāsavau’’ti||

… daivasabhau thairau…|

vaggau dasamau niṭṭhitau|

tassuddānaṃ –

paripuṇṇakau ca vijayau, airakau maittajī muni|

cakkhupālau khaṇḍasumanau, tissau ca abhayau tathā|

uttiyau ca mahāpañ‍ñau, thairau daivasabhaupi cāti||

11. aikādasamavaggau

1. bailaṭṭhānikatthairagāthā

101.

 ‘‘hitvā  gihittaṃ anavausitattau, mukhanaṅgalī audarikau kusītau|

mahāvarāhauva  nivāpapuṭṭhau, punappunaṃ gabbhamupaiti mandau’’ti||

… bailaṭṭhānikau thairau…|

2. saitucchatthairagāthā

102.

 ‘‘mānaina  vañ‍citāsai, saṅkhāraisu saṃkilissamānāsai|

lābhālābhaina mathitā, samādhiṃ nādhigacchantī’’ti||

… saitucchau thairau…|

3. bandhuratthairagāthā

103.

 ‘‘nāhaṃ aitaina atthikau, sukhitau dhammarasaina tappitau|

pitvā pītvāna (sī॰ syā॰) rasaggamuttamaṃ, na ca kāhāmi visaina santhava’’nti||

… bandhurau bandhanau (ka॰) thairau…|

4. khitakatthairagāthā

104.

 ‘‘lahukau vata mai kāyau, phuṭṭhau ca pītisukhaina vipulaina|

tūlamiva airitaṃ mālutaina, pilavatīva mai kāyau’’ti||

… khitakau thairau…|

5. malitavambhatthairagāthā

105.

 ‘‘uk‍kaṇṭhitaupi na vasai, ramamānaupi pak‍kamai|

na tvaivānatthasaṃhitaṃ, vasai vāsaṃ vicakkhaṇau’’ti||

… malitavambhau thairau…|

6. suhaimantatthairagāthā

106.

 ‘‘sataliṅgassa  atthassa, satalakkhaṇadhārinau|

aikaṅgadassī dummaidhau, satadassī ca paṇḍitau’’ti||

… suhaimantau thairau…|

7. dhammasavatthairagāthā

107.

 ‘‘pabbajiṃ  tulayitvāna, agārasmānagāriyaṃ|

tissau  vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

… dhammasavau thairau…|

8. dhammasavapitutthairagāthā

108.

 ‘‘sa vīsavassasatikau, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

… dhammasavapitu thairau…|

9. saṅgharakkhitatthairagāthā

109.

 ‘‘na  nūnāyaṃ paramahitānukampinau, rahaugatau anuvigaṇaiti sāsanaṃ|

tathāhayaṃ viharati pākatindriyau, migī yathā taruṇajātikā vanai’’ti||

… saṅgharakkhitau thairau…|

10. usabhatthairagāthā

110.

 ‘‘nagā nagaggaisu susaṃvirūḷhā, udaggamaighaina navaina sittā|

vivaikakāmassa arañ‍ñasañ‍ñinau, janaiti bhiyyau usabhassa kalyata’’nti||

… usabhau thairau…|

vaggau aikādasamau niṭṭhitau|

tassuddānaṃ –

bailaṭṭhānikau saitucchau, bandhurau khitakau isi|

malitavambhau suhaimantau, dhammasavau dhammasavapitā|

saṅgharakkhitatthairau  ca, usabhau ca mahāmunīti||

12. dvādasamavaggau

1. jaintatthairagāthā

111.

 ‘‘duppabbaj‍jaṃ  vai duradhivāsā gaihā, dhammau gambhīrau duradhigamā bhaugā|

kicchā vutti nau itarītarainaiva, yuttaṃ cintaituṃ satatamanic‍cata’’nti||

… jaintau thairau…|

2. vacchagauttatthairagāthā

112.

 ‘‘taivij‍jauhaṃ mahājhāyī, caitausamathakauvidau|

sadatthau mai anuppattau, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

… vacchagauttau thairau…|

3. vanavacchatthairagāthā

113.

 ‘‘acchaudikā puthusilā,gaunaṅgulamigāyutā|

ambusaivālasañchan‍nā, tai sailā ramayanti ma’’nti||

… vanavacchau thairau…|

4. adhimuttatthairagāthā

114.

 ‘‘kāyaduṭṭhul‍lagarunau, hiyyamānamhi hīyamānamhi (sī॰) jīvitai|

sarīrasukhagiddhassa, kutau samaṇasādhutā’’ti||

… adhimuttau thairau…|

5. mahānāmatthairagāthā

115.

 ‘‘aisāvahiyyasai pabbataina, bahukuṭajasal‍lakikaina sal‍lakitaina (sī॰), sal‍larikaina (syā॰)|

naisādakaina  girinā, yasassinā paricchadainā’’ti||

… mahānāmau thairau…|

6. pārāpariyatthairagāthā

116.

 ‘‘chaphassāyatanai  hitvā, guttadvārau susaṃvutau|

aghamūlaṃ vamitvāna, pattau mai āsavakkhayau’’ti||

… pārāpariyau pārāsariyau (sī॰), pāraṃpariyau (ka॰) thairau …|

7. yasatthairagāthā

117.

 ‘‘suvilittau suvasanau,sabbābharaṇabhūsitau|

tissau vij‍jā ajjhagamiṃ, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

… yasau thairau…|

8. kimilatthairagāthā

118.

 ‘‘abhisattauva nipatati, vayau rūpaṃ añ‍ñamiva tathaiva santaṃ|

tassaiva satau avippavasatau, añ‍ñassaiva sarāmi attāna’’nti||

… kimilau kimbilau (sī॰ syā॰) thairau…|

9. vaj‍jiputtatthairagāthā

119.

 ‘‘rukkhamūlagahanaṃ pasak‍kiya, nibbānaṃ hadayasmiṃ aupiya|

jhāya gautama mā ca pamādau, kiṃ tai biḷibiḷikā karissatī’’ti||

… vaj‍jiputtau thairau…|

10. isidattatthairagāthā

120.

 ‘‘pañ‍cakkhandhā pariñ‍ñātā, tiṭṭhanti chin‍namūlakā|

dukkhakkhayau  anuppattau,pattau mai āsavakkhayau’’ti||

… isidattau thairau…|

vaggau dvādasamau niṭṭhitau|

tassuddānaṃ  –

jaintau  ca vacchagauttau ca, vacchau ca vanasavhayau|

adhimuttau mahānāmau, pārāpariyau yasaupi ca|

kimilau vaj‍jiputtau ca, isidattau mahāyasauti||

aikakanipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

vīsuttarasataṃ thairā, katakic‍cā anāsavā|

aikakaiva nipātamhi, susaṅgītā mahaisibhīti||


2. dukanipātau

1. paṭhamavaggau

1. uttaratthairagāthā

121.

 ‘‘natthi  kauci bhavau nic‍cau, saṅkhārā vāpi sassatā|

uppaj‍janti ca tai khandhā, cavanti aparāparaṃ||

122.

 ‘‘aitamādīnaṃ ñatvā, bhavainamhi anatthikau|

nissaṭau sabbakāmaihi, pattau mai āsavakkhayau’’ti||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā uttarau thairau gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

2. piṇḍaulabhāradvājatthairagāthā

123.

 ‘‘nayidaṃ  anayaina jīvitaṃ, nāhārau hadayassa santikau|

āhāraṭṭhitikau samussayau, iti disvāna carāmi aisanaṃ||

124.

 ‘‘paṅkauti hi naṃ pavaidayuṃ, yāyaṃ vandanapūjanā kulaisu|

sukhumaṃ sal‍laṃ durubbahaṃ, sak‍kārau kāpurisaina duj‍jahau’’ti||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā piṇḍaulabhāradvājau thairau gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

3. val‍liyatthairagāthā

125.

 ‘‘mak‍kaṭau pañ‍cadvārāyaṃ, kuṭikāyaṃ pasak‍kiya|

dvāraina anupariyaiti, ghaṭṭayantau muhuṃ muhuṃ||

126.

 ‘‘tiṭṭha mak‍kaṭa mā dhāvi, na hi tai taṃ yathā purai|

niggahītausi pañ‍ñāya, naiva dūraṃ gamissatī’’ti||

… val‍liyau thairau…|

4. gaṅgātīriyatthairagāthā

127.

 ‘‘tiṇṇaṃ  mai tālapattānaṃ, gaṅgātīrai kuṭī katā|

chavasittauva mai pattau, paṃsukūlañ‍ca cīvaraṃ||

128.

 ‘‘dvin‍naṃ antaravassānaṃ, aikā vācā mai bhāsitā|

tatiyai antaravassamhi, tamaukhandhau tamaukkhandhau (sī॰ syā॰) padālitau’’ti||

… gaṅgātīriyau thairau…|

5. ajinatthairagāthā

129.

 ‘‘api cai hauti taivij‍jau, mac‍cuhāyī anāsavau|

appañ‍ñātauti naṃ bālā, avajānanti ajānatā||

130.

 ‘‘yau  ca khau an‍napānassa, lābhī hautīdha puggalau|

pāpadhammaupi cai hauti, sau naisaṃ hauti sak‍katau’’ti||

… ajinau thairau…|

6. maiḷajinatthairagāthā

131.

 ‘‘yadāhaṃ  dhammamassausiṃ, bhāsamānassa satthunau|

maggai paṭipadāyaṃ vā, kaṅkhā mayhaṃ na vij‍jatī’’ti||

… maiḷajinau thairau…|

 dha॰ pa॰ 13 dhammapadai ‘‘yathā agāraṃ ducchan‍naṃ, vuṭṭhī samativijjhati|

 dha॰ pa॰ 14 dhammapadai ‘‘yathā agāraṃ succhan‍naṃ, vuḍḍhī na samativijjhati|

… rādhau thairau…|

135.

9. gautamatthairagāthā

137.

 ‘‘sukhaṃ supanti munayau, yai itthīsu na bajjharai|

138.

 ‘‘vadhaṃ carimha tai kāma, anaṇā dāni tai mayaṃ|

… gautamau thairau…|

10. vasabhatthairagāthā

139.

 ‘‘pubbai  hanati attānaṃ, pacchā hanati sau parai|

suhataṃ hanti attānaṃ, vītaṃsainaiva pakkhimā||

140.

 ‘‘na brāhmaṇau bahivaṇṇau, antau vaṇṇau hi brāhmaṇau|

yasmiṃ pāpāni kammāni, sa vai kaṇhau sujampatī’’ti||

… vasabhau thairau…|

vaggau paṭhamau niṭṭhitau|

tassuddānaṃ –

uttarau caiva piṇḍaulau, val‍liyau tīriyau isi|

ajinau ca maiḷajinau, rādhau surādhau gautamau|

vasabhaina imai haunti, dasa thairā mahiddhikāti||

7. anūpamatthairagāthā

 ‘‘nandamānāgataṃ cittaṃ, sūlamāraupamānakaṃ|

214.

 ‘‘tāhaṃ cittakaliṃ brūmi, taṃ brūmi cittadubbhakaṃ|

satthā tai dul‍labhau laddhau, mānatthai maṃ niyaujayī’’ti||

… anūpamau thairau…|

8. vaj‍jitatthairagāthā

215.

 ‘‘saṃsaraṃ  dīghamaddhānaṃ, gatīsu parivattisaṃ|

apassaṃ ariyasac‍cāni, andhabhūtau andhībhūtau (ka॰) puthuj‍janau||

216.

 ‘‘tassa  mai appamattassa, saṃsārā vinaḷīkatā|

sabbā gatī samucchin‍nā, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti||

… vaj‍jitau thairau…|

9. sandhitatthairagāthā

217.

 ‘‘assatthai haritaubhāsai, saṃvirūḷhamhi pādapai|

aikaṃ buddhagataṃ sañ‍ñaṃ, alabhitthaṃ alabhiṃ haṃ (ka॰) patissatau||

218.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

tassā sañ‍ñāya vāhasā, pattau mai āsavakkhayau’’ti||

… sandhitau thairau…|

vaggau pañ‍camau niṭṭhitau|

tassuddānaṃ  –

kumārakassapau  thairau, dhammapālau ca brahmāli|

maugharājā visākhau ca, cūḷakau ca anūpamau|

vaj‍jitau sandhitau thairau, kilaisarajavāhanauti||

dukanipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

gāthādukanipātamhi, navuti caiva aṭṭha ca|

thairā aikūnapañ‍ñāsaṃ, bhāsitā nayakauvidāti||


3. tikanipātau

1. aṅgaṇikabhāradvājatthairagāthā

219.

 ‘‘ayauni  suddhimanvaisaṃ, aggiṃ paricariṃ vanai|

suddhimaggaṃ ajānantau, akāsiṃ amaraṃ tapaṃ akāsiṃ aparaṃ tapaṃ (syā॰), akāsiṃ amataṃ tapaṃ (ka॰)||

220.

 ‘‘taṃ sukhaina sukhaṃ laddhaṃ, passa dhammasudhammataṃ|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

221.

 ‘‘brahmabandhu purai āsiṃ, idāni khaumhi brāhmaṇau|

taivij‍jau nhātakau nahātakau (sī॰ aṭṭha॰) camhi, sauttiyau camhi vaidagū’’ti||

… aṅgaṇikabhāradvājau thairau…|

2. pac‍cayatthairagāthā

222.

 ‘‘pañ‍cāhāhaṃ pabbajitau, saikhau appattamānasau,

223.

 ‘‘nāsissaṃ na pivissāmi, vihāratau na nikkhamai|

napi passaṃ nipātaissaṃ, taṇhāsal‍lai anūhatai||

224.

 ‘‘tassa maivaṃ viharatau, passa vīriyaparak‍kamaṃ|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

… pac‍cayau thairau…|

3. bākulatthairagāthā

225.

 ‘‘yau pubbai karaṇīyāni, pacchā sau kātumicchati|

226.

 ‘‘yañhi kayirā tañhi vadai, yaṃ na kayirā na taṃ vadai|

akarauntaṃ  bhāsamānaṃ, parijānanti paṇḍitā||

227.

 ‘‘susukhaṃ  vata nibbānaṃ, sammāsambuddhadaisitaṃ|

asaukaṃ virajaṃ khaimaṃ, yattha dukkhaṃ nirujjhatī’’ti||

… bākulau bāk‍kulau (sī॰) thairau…|

228.

 ‘‘sukhaṃ cai jīvituṃ icchai, sāmañ‍ñasmiṃ apaikkhavā|

229.

 ‘‘sukhaṃ cai jīvituṃ icchai, sāmañ‍ñasmiṃ apaikkhavā|

ahi mūsikasaubbhaṃva, saivaitha sayanāsanaṃ||

itarītaraina tussaiyya, aikadhammañ‍ca bhāvayai’’ti||

… dhaniyau thairau…|

 ‘‘atisītaṃ atiuṇhaṃ, atisāyamidaṃ ahu|

232.

 ‘‘yau ca sītañ‍ca uṇhañ‍ca, tiṇā bhiyyau na mañ‍ñati|

karaṃ purisakic‍cāni, sau sukhā na vihāyati||

233.

 ‘‘dabbaṃ kusaṃ pauṭakilaṃ, usīraṃ muñ‍japabbajaṃ|

urasā panudissāmi, vivaikamanubrūhaya’’nti||

… mātaṅgaputtau thairau…|

6. khuj‍jasaubhitatthairagāthā

234.

 ‘‘yai cittakathī bahussutā, samaṇā pāṭaliputtavāsinau|

235.

 ‘‘yai  cittakathī bahussutā, samaṇā pāṭaliputtavāsinau|

taisañ‍ñatarauyamāyuvā, dvārai tiṭṭhati mālutairitau||

236.

 ‘‘suyuddhaina suyiṭṭhaina, saṅgāmavijayaina ca|

… khuj‍jasaubhitau thairau…|

7. vāraṇatthairagāthā

 ‘‘ahu  tuyhaṃ purai saddhā, sā tai aj‍ja na vij‍jati|

 ‘‘anic‍cā hi calā saddā, aivaṃ diṭṭhā hi sā mayā|

 ‘‘pac‍cati muninau bhattaṃ, thaukaṃ thaukaṃ kulai kulai|

 saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.185 ‘‘ārambhatha ārabhatha (sī॰ syā॰), ārabbhatha (ka॰) nik‍kamatha, yuñ‍jatha buddhasāsanai|

tikanipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

aṅgaṇikau bhāradvājau, pac‍cayau bākulau isi|

dhaniyau mātaṅgaputtau, saubhitau vāraṇau isi||

uttarapālau abhibhūtau, gautamau hāritaupi ca||

thairau tikanipātamhi, nibbānai vimalau katau|

aṭṭhatālīsa gāthāyau, thairā sauḷasa kittitāti||

3. sabhiyatthairagāthā

275.

 dha॰ pa॰ 6 dhammapadaipi ‘‘parai ca na vijānanti, mayamaittha yamāmasai|

yai ca tattha vijānanti, tatau sammanti maidhagā||

vijānanti ca yai dhammaṃ, āturaisu anāturā||

 ‘‘yaṃ  kiñ‍ci sithilaṃ kammaṃ, saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca yaṃ vataṃ|

278.

4. nandakatthairagāthā

279.

 ‘‘dhiratthu  pūrai duggandhai, mārapakkhai avassutai|

280.

 ‘‘mā  purāṇaṃ amañ‍ñitthau, māsādaisi tathāgatai|

281.

 ‘‘yai ca khau bālā dummaidhā, dummantī mauhapārutā|

282.

 jā॰ 1.10.102 jātakaipi ‘‘dhammau havai rakkhati dhammacāriṃ, dhammau suciṇṇau sukhamāvahati|

 jā॰ 1.15.385 ‘‘nahi dhammau adhammau ca, ubhau samavipākinau|

12. muditatthairagāthā

311.

 ‘‘pabbajiṃ jīvikatthauhaṃ, laddhāna upasampadaṃ|

tatau saddhaṃ paṭilabhiṃ, daḷhavīriyau parak‍kamiṃ||

312.

 ‘‘kāmaṃ bhij‍jatuyaṃ kāyau, maṃsapaisī visīyaruṃ visiyantu (ka॰)|

ubhau jaṇṇukasandhīhi, jaṅghāyau papatantu mai||

313.

 ‘‘nāsissaṃ  na pivissāmi, vihārā ca na nikkhamai|

napi passaṃ nipātaissaṃ, taṇhāsal‍lai anūhatai||

314.

 ‘‘tassa maivaṃ viharatau, passa vīriyaparak‍kamaṃ|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

… muditau thairau…|

catuk‍kanipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

nāgasamālau bhagu ca, sabhiyau nandakaupi ca|

jambukau sainakau thairau, sambhūtau rāhulaupi ca||

bhavati candanau thairau, dasaitai idāni navaiva thairā dissanti buddhasāvakā|

dhammikau sappakau thairau, muditau cāpi tai tayau|

gāthāyau dvai ca pañ‍ñāsa, thairā sabbaipi tairasāti idāni dvādasaiva thairā dissanti||


5. pañ‍cakanipātau

1. rājadattatthairagāthā

315.

 ‘‘bhikkhu  sivathikaṃ sīvathikaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) gantvā, addasa itthimujjhitaṃ|

apaviddhaṃ susānasmiṃ, khaj‍jantiṃ kimihī phuṭaṃ||

316.

 ‘‘yañhi aikai jigucchanti, mataṃ disvāna pāpakaṃ|

kāmarāgau pāturahu, andhauva savatī vasatī (sī॰) ahuṃ||

317.

 ‘‘auraṃ audanapākamhā, tamhā ṭhānā apak‍kamiṃ|

satimā sampajānauhaṃ, aikamantaṃ upāvisiṃ||

318.

 ‘‘tatau mai manasīkārau, yaunisau udapaj‍jatha|

ādīnavau pāturahu, nibbidā samatiṭṭhatha||

319.

 ‘‘tatau cittaṃ vimuc‍ci mai, passa dhammasudhammataṃ|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

… rājadattau thairau…|

2. subhūtatthairagāthā

320.

 ‘‘ayaugai yuñ‍jamattānaṃ, purisau kic‍camicchakau kic‍camicchatau (sī॰), kic‍camicchayaṃ (katthaci)|

caraṃ cai nādhigacchaiyya, ‘taṃ mai dubbhagalakkhaṇaṃ’||

321.

 ‘‘abbūḷhaṃ aghagataṃ vijitaṃ, aikañ‍cai aussajaiyya kalīva siyā|

sabbānipi cai aussajaiyya andhauva siyā, samavisamassa adassanatau||

322.

 ‘‘yañhi  kayirā tañhi vadai, yaṃ na kayirā na taṃ vadai|

akarauntaṃ bhāsamānaṃ, parijānanti paṇḍitā||

323.

 dha॰ pa॰ 51 dhammapadaipi ‘‘yathāpi ruciraṃ pupphaṃ, vaṇṇavantaṃ agandhakaṃ|

aivaṃ subhāsitā vācā, aphalā hauti akubbatau||

324.

 dha॰ pa॰ 52 ‘‘yathāpi  ruciraṃ pupphaṃ, vaṇṇavantaṃ sugandhakaṃ sagandhakaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

aivaṃ subhāsitā vācā, saphalā hauti kubbatau’’ti sakubbatau (sī॰ pī॰), sukubbatau (syā॰)||

… subhūtau thairau…|

3. girimānandatthairagāthā

325.

 ‘‘vassati  daivau yathā sugītaṃ, chan‍nā mai kuṭikā sukhā nivātā|

tassaṃ viharāmi vūpasantau, atha cai patthayasī pavassa daiva||

326.

 ‘‘vassati daivau yathā sugītaṃ, chan‍nā mai kuṭikā sukhā nivātā|

tassaṃ viharāmi santacittau, atha cai patthayasī pavassa daiva||

327.

 ‘‘vassati daivau…pai॰… tassaṃ viharāmi vītarāgau…pai॰…||

328.

 ‘‘vassati daivau…pai॰… tassaṃ viharāmi vītadausau…pai॰…||

329.

 ‘‘vassati daivau…pai॰… tassaṃ  viharāmi vītamauhau|

atha cai patthayasī pavassa daivā’’ti||

… girimānandau thairau…|

4. sumanatthairagāthā

330.

 ‘‘yaṃ patthayānau dhammaisu, upajjhāyau anuggahi|

amataṃ abhikaṅkhantaṃ, kataṃ kattabbakaṃ mayā||

331.

 ‘‘anuppattau sacchikatau, sayaṃ dhammau anītihau|

visuddhiñāṇau nik‍kaṅkhau, byākaraumi tavantikai||

332.

 ‘‘pubbainivāsaṃ jānāmi, dibbacakkhu visaudhitaṃ|

sadatthau mai anuppattau, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

333.

 ‘‘appamattassa mai sikkhā, sussutā tava sāsanai|

sabbai mai āsavā khīṇā, natthi dāni punabbhavau||

334.

 ‘‘anusāsi  maṃ ariyavatā, anukampi anuggahi|

amaughau tuyhamauvādau, antaivāsimhi sikkhitau’’ti||

… sumanau thairau…|

5. vaḍḍhatthairagāthā

335.

 ‘‘sādhū hi kira mai mātā, pataudaṃ upadaṃsayi|

yassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā, anusiṭṭhau janaittiyā|

āraddhavīriyau pahitattau, pattau sambaudhimuttamaṃ||

336.

 ‘‘arahā  dakkhiṇaiyyaumhi, taivij‍jau amataddasau|

jaitvā namucinau sainaṃ, viharāmi anāsavau||

337.

 ‘‘ajjhattañ‍ca  bahiddhā ca, yai mai vij‍jiṃsu āsavā|

sabbai asaisā ucchin‍nā, na ca uppaj‍jarai puna||

338.

 ‘‘visāradā khau bhaginī, aitamatthaṃ abhāsayi|

‘apihā nūna mayipi, vanathau tai na vij‍jati’||

339.

 ‘‘pariyantakataṃ dukkhaṃ, antimauyaṃ samussayau|

jātimaraṇasaṃsārau, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti||

… vaḍḍhau thairau…|

6. nadīkassapatthairagāthā

340.

 ‘‘atthāya vata mai buddhau, nadiṃ nairañ‍jaraṃ agā|

yassāhaṃ dhammaṃ sutvāna, micchādiṭṭhiṃ vivaj‍jayiṃ||

341.

 ‘‘yajiṃ  uc‍cāvacai yañ‍ñai, aggihuttaṃ juhiṃ ahaṃ|

‘aisā suddhī’ti mañ‍ñantau, andhabhūtau andhībhūtau (ka॰) puthuj‍janau||

342.

 ‘‘diṭṭhigahanapakkhandau pakkhantau (sī॰), pakkhan‍nau (syā॰ pī॰), parāmāsaina mauhitau|

asuddhiṃ mañ‍ñisaṃ suddhiṃ, andhabhūtau aviddasu||

343.

 ‘‘micchādiṭṭhi pahīnā mai, bhavā sabbai padālitā vidālitā (ka॰)|

juhāmi dakkhiṇaiyyaggiṃ, namassāmi tathāgataṃ||

344.

 ‘‘mauhā  sabbai pahīnā mai, bhavataṇhā padālitā|

vikkhīṇau jātisaṃsārau, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti||

… nadīkassapau thairau…|

7. gayākassapatthairagāthā

345.

 ‘‘pātau majjhanhikaṃ sāyaṃ, tikkhattuṃ divasassahaṃ|

autariṃ udakaṃ sauhaṃ, gayāya gayaphagguyā||

346.

 ‘‘‘yaṃ  mayā pakataṃ pāpaṃ, pubbai añ‍ñāsu jātisu|

taṃ dānīdha pavāhaimi’, aivaṃdiṭṭhi purai ahuṃ||

347.

 ‘‘sutvā  subhāsitaṃ vācaṃ, dhammatthasahitaṃ padaṃ|

tathaṃ yāthāvakaṃ atthaṃ, yaunisau pac‍cavaikkhisaṃ|

348.

 ‘‘ninhātasabbapāpaumhi, nimmalau payatau suci|

suddhau suddhassa dāyādau, puttau buddhassa aurasau||

349.

 ‘‘augayhaṭṭhaṅgikaṃ sautaṃ, sabbapāpaṃ pavāhayiṃ|

tissau vij‍jā ajjhagamiṃ, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

… gayākassapau thairau…|

8. vak‍kalitthairagāthā

350.

 ‘‘vātaraugābhinītau tvaṃ, viharaṃ kānanai vanai|

paviṭṭhagaucarai lūkhai, kathaṃ bhikkhu karissasi||

351.

 ‘‘pītisukhaina vipulaina, pharamānau samussayaṃ|

lūkhampi abhisambhauntau, viharissāmi kānanai||

352.

 ‘‘bhāvaintau satipaṭṭhānai, indriyāni balāni ca|

baujjhaṅgāni ca bhāvaintau, viharissāmi kānanai||

353.

 ‘‘āraddhavīriyai pahitattai, nic‍caṃ daḷhaparak‍kamai āraddhavīriyau pahitattau, nic‍caṃ daḷhaparak‍kamau (sī॰)|

samaggai sahitai disvā, viharissāmi kānanai||

354.

 ‘‘anussarantau sambuddhaṃ, aggaṃ dantaṃ samāhitaṃ|

atanditau  rattindivaṃ, viharissāmi kānanai’’ti||

… vak‍kalitthairau…|

9. vijitasainatthairagāthā

355.

 ‘‘aulaggaissāmi  tai citta, āṇidvāraiva hatthinaṃ|

na taṃ pāpai niyaujaissaṃ, kāmajāla kāmajālaṃ (syā॰) sarīraja sarīrajaṃ (syā॰ ka॰)||

356.

 ‘‘tvaṃ  aulaggau na gacchasi na gañchisi (pī), dvāravivaraṃ gajauva alabhantau|

na ca cittakali punappunaṃ, pasak‍ka pasahaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) pāparatau carissasi||

357.

 ‘‘yathā kuñ‍jaraṃ adantaṃ, navaggahamaṅkusaggahau|

balavā āvattaiti akāmaṃ, aivaṃ āvattayissaṃ taṃ||

358.

 ‘‘yathā varahayadamakusalau, sārathi pavarau damaiti ājañ‍ñaṃ|

aivaṃ damayissaṃ taṃ, patiṭṭhitau pañ‍casu balaisu||

359.

 ‘‘satiyā  taṃ nibandhissaṃ, payuttau tai damaissāmi payatattau vaudapaissāmi (sī॰)|

vīriyadhuraniggahitau, na yitau dūraṃ  gamissasai cittā’’ti||

… vijitasainau thairau…|

10. yasadattatthairagāthā

360.

 ‘‘upārambhacittau dummaidhau, suṇāti jinasāsanaṃ|

ārakā hauti saddhammā, nabhasau pathavī yathā||

361.

 ‘‘upārambhacittau dummaidhau, suṇāti jinasāsanaṃ|

parihāyati saddhammā, kāḷapakkhaiva candimā||

362.

 ‘‘upārambhacittau dummaidhau, suṇāti jinasāsanaṃ|

parisussati saddhammai, macchau appaudakai yathā||

363.

 ‘‘upārambhacittau dummaidhau, suṇāti jinasāsanaṃ|

na virūhati saddhammai, khaittai bījaṃva pūtikaṃ||

364.

 ‘‘yau ca tuṭṭhaina cittaina, suṇāti jinasāsanaṃ|

khaipaitvā āsavai sabbai, sacchikatvā akuppataṃ|

pappuyya paramaṃ santiṃ, parinibbātināsavau’’ti||

… yasadattau thairau…|

11. sauṇakuṭikaṇṇatthairagāthā

365.

 ‘‘upasampadā  ca mai laddhā, vimuttau camhi anāsavau|

sau ca mai bhagavā diṭṭhau, vihārai ca sahāvasiṃ||

366.

 ‘‘bahudaiva rattiṃ bhagavā, abbhaukāsaitināmayi|

vihārakusalau satthā, vihāraṃ pāvisī tadā||

367.

 ‘‘santharitvāna saṅghāṭiṃ, saiyyaṃ kappaisi gautamau|

sīhau  sailaguhāyaṃva, pahīnabhayabhairavau||

368.

 ‘‘tatau kalyāṇavāk‍karaṇau, sammāsambuddhasāvakau|

sauṇau abhāsi saddhammaṃ, buddhasaiṭṭhassa sammukhā||

369.

 ‘‘pañ‍cakkhandhai pariñ‍ñāya, bhāvayitvāna añ‍jasaṃ|

pappuyya paramaṃ santiṃ, parinibbissatyanāsavau’’ti||

… sauṇau kuṭikaṇṇathairau…|

12. kausiyatthairagāthā

370.

 ‘‘yau  vai garūnaṃ vacanañ‍ñu dhīrau, vasai ca tamhi janayaitha paimaṃ|

sau bhattimā nāma ca hauti paṇḍitau, ñatvā ca dhammaisu visaisi assa||

371.

 ‘‘yaṃ āpadā uppatitā uḷārā, nakkhambhayantai paṭisaṅkhayantaṃ|

sau thāmavā nāma ca hauti paṇḍitau, ñatvā ca dhammaisu visaisi assa||

372.

 ‘‘yau vai samuddauva ṭhitau anaijau, gambhīrapañ‍ñau nipuṇatthadassī|

asaṃhāriyau nāma ca hauti paṇḍitau, ñatvā ca dhammaisu visaisi assa||

373.

 ‘‘bahussutau dhammadharau ca hauti, dhammassa  hauti anudhammacārī|

sau tādisau nāma ca hauti paṇḍitau, ñatvā ca dhammaisu visaisi assa||

374.

 ‘‘atthañ‍ca  yau jānāti bhāsitassa, atthañ‍ca ñatvāna tathā karauti|

atthantarau nāma sa hauti paṇḍitau, ñatvā ca dhammaisu visaisi assā’’ti||

… kausiyau thairau…|

pañ‍cakanipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

rājadattau subhūtau ca, girimānandasumanā|

vaḍḍhau ca kassapau thairau, gayākassapavak‍kalī||

vijitau yasadattau ca, sauṇau kausiyasavhayau|

saṭṭhi ca pañ‍ca gāthāyau, thairā ca aittha dvādasāti||


6. chak‍kanipātau

1. uruvaiḷakassapatthairagāthā

375.

 ‘‘disvāna  pāṭihīrāni, gautamassa yasassinau|

na tāvāhaṃ paṇipatiṃ, issāmānaina vañ‍citau||

376.

 ‘‘mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, caudaisi narasārathi|

tatau  mai āsi saṃvaigau, abbhutau laumahaṃsanau||

377.

 ‘‘pubbai jaṭilabhūtassa, yā mai siddhi parittikā|

tāhaṃ tadā nirākatvā niraṃkatvā (syā॰ ka॰), pabbajiṃ jinasāsanai||

378.

 ‘‘pubbai yañ‍ñaina santuṭṭhau, kāmadhātupurakkhatau|

pacchā rāgañ‍ca dausañ‍ca, mauhañ‍cāpi samūhaniṃ||

379.

 ‘‘pubbainivāsaṃ jānāmi, dibbacakkhu visaudhitaṃ|

iddhimā paracittañ‍ñū, dibbasautañ‍ca pāpuṇiṃ||

380.

 ‘‘yassa catthāya pabbajitau, agārasmānagāriyaṃ|

sau mai atthau anuppattau, sabbasaṃyaujanakkhayau’’ti||

… uruvaiḷakassapau thairau…|

2. taikicchakāritthairagāthā

381.

 ‘‘atihitā vīhi, khalagatā sālī|

na ca labhai piṇḍaṃ, kathamahaṃ kassaṃ||

382.

 ‘‘buddhamappamaiyyaṃ anussara pasan‍nau|

pītiyā phuṭasarīrau hauhisi satatamudaggau||

383.

 ‘‘dhammamappamaiyyaṃ anussara pasan‍nau|

pītiyā phuṭasarīrau hauhisi satatamudaggau||

384.

 ‘‘saṅghamappamaiyyaṃ anussara pasan‍nau|

pītiyā phuṭasarīrau hauhisi satatamudaggau||

385.

 ‘‘abbhaukāsai  viharasi, sītā haimantikā imā ratyau|

mā sītaina paraitau vihañ‍ñitthau, pavisa  tvaṃ vihāraṃ phusitaggaḷaṃ||

386.

 ‘‘phusissaṃ catassau appamañ‍ñāyau, tāhi ca sukhitau viharissaṃ|

nāhaṃ sītaina vihañ‍ñissaṃ, aniñ‍jitau viharantau’’ti||

… taikicchakārī taikicchakāni (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) thairau…|

3. mahānāgatthairagāthā

387.

 ‘‘yassa  sabrahmacārīsu, gāravau nūpalabbhati|

parihāyati saddhammā, macchau appaudakai yathā||

388.

 ‘‘yassa sabrahmacārīsu, gāravau nūpalabbhati|

na virūhati saddhammai, khaittai bījaṃva pūtikaṃ||

389.

 ‘‘yassa sabrahmacārīsu, gāravau nūpalabbhati|

ārakā hauti nibbānā nibbāṇā (sī॰), dhammarājassa sāsanai||

390.

 ‘‘yassa sabrahmacārīsu, gāravau upalabbhati|

na vihāyati saddhammā, macchau bavhaudakai bahvaudakai (sī॰), bahaudakai (syā॰) yathā||

391.

 ‘‘yassa sabrahmacārīsu, gāravau upalabbhati|

sau virūhati saddhammai, khaittai bījaṃva bhaddakaṃ||

392.

 ‘‘yassa sabrahmacārīsu, gāravau upalabbhati|

santikai hauti nibbānaṃ nibbāṇaṃ (sī॰), dhammarājassa sāsanai’’ti||

… mahānāgau thairau…|

4. kul‍latthairagāthā

393.

 ‘‘kul‍lau sivathikaṃ gantvā, addasa itthimujjhitaṃ|

apaviddhaṃ  susānasmiṃ, khaj‍jantiṃ kimihī phuṭaṃ||

394.

 ‘‘āturaṃ  asuciṃ pūtiṃ, passa kul‍la samussayaṃ|

uggharantaṃ paggharantaṃ, bālānaṃ abhinanditaṃ||

395.

 ‘‘dhammādāsaṃ gahaitvāna, ñāṇadassanapattiyā|

pac‍cavaikkhiṃ imaṃ kāyaṃ, tucchaṃ santarabāhiraṃ||

396.

 ‘‘yathā idaṃ tathā aitaṃ, yathā aitaṃ tathā idaṃ|

yathā adhau tathā uddhaṃ, yathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau||

397.

 ‘‘yathā  divā tathā rattiṃ, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā|

yathā purai tathā pacchā, yathā pacchā tathā purai||

398.

 ‘‘pañ‍caṅgikaina turiyaina, na ratī hauti tādisī|

yathā aikaggacittassa, sammā dhammaṃ vipassatau’’ti||

… kul‍lau thairau…|

5. mālukyaputtatthairagāthā

399.

 ‘‘manujassa pamattacārinau, taṇhā vaḍḍhati māluvā viya|

sau p‍lavatī p‍lavati (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), parip‍lavati (syā॰) hurā huraṃ, phalamicchaṃva vanasmi vānarau||

400.

 ‘‘yaṃ aisā sahatai sahati (pī॰ ka॰) jammī, taṇhā laukai visattikā|

saukā tassa pavaḍḍhanti, abhivaṭṭhaṃva abhivuṭṭhaṃva (syā॰), abhivaḍḍhaṃva (ka॰) bīraṇaṃ||

401.

 ‘‘yau caitaṃ sahatai sahati (pī॰ ka॰) jammiṃ, taṇhaṃ laukai durac‍cayaṃ|

saukā tamhā papatanti, udabindūva paukkharā||

402.

 ‘‘taṃ  vau vadāmi bhaddaṃ vau, yāvantaittha samāgatā|

taṇhāya  mūlaṃ khaṇatha, usīratthauva bīraṇaṃ|

mā vau naḷaṃva sautauva, mārau bhañ‍ji punappunaṃ||

403.

 ‘‘karautha buddhavacanaṃ, khaṇau vau mā upac‍cagā|

khaṇātītā hi saucanti, nirayamhi samappitā||

404.

 ‘‘pamādau rajau pamādau sabbadā (sī॰ ka॰), suttanipātaṭṭhakathāyaṃ uṭṭhānasuttavaṇṇanā aulaukaitabbā, pamādānupatitau rajau|

appamādaina vij‍jāya, abbahai sal‍lamattanau’’ti||

… mālukyaputtau māluṅkyaputtau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) thairau…|

6. sappadāsatthairagāthā

405.

 ‘‘paṇṇavīsativassāni, yatau pabbajitau ahaṃ|

accharāsaṅghātamattampi, caitausantimanajjhagaṃ||

406.

 ‘‘aladdhā cittassaikaggaṃ, kāmarāgaina aṭṭitau additau (syā॰ sī॰ aṭṭha॰), aḍḍitau (ka॰)|

bāhā paggayha kandantau, vihārā upanikkhamiṃ nūpanikkhamiṃ (sabbattha), dupanikkhamiṃ (?)||

407.

 ‘‘satthaṃ vā āharissāmi, kau atthau jīvitaina mai|

kathaṃ hi sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhaṃ, kālaṃ kubbaitha mādisau||

408.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ  khuramādāya, mañ‍cakamhi upāvisiṃ|

parinītau khurau āsi, dhamaniṃ chaittumattanau||

409.

 ‘‘tatau mai manasīkārau, yaunisau udapaj‍jatha|

ādīnavau pāturahu, nibbidā samatiṭṭhatha||

410.

 ‘‘tatau cittaṃ vimuc‍ci mai, passa dhammasudhammataṃ|

tissau  vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

… sappadāsau thairau…|

7.kātiyānatthairagāthā

411.

 ‘‘uṭṭhaihi nisīda kātiyāna, mā niddābahulau ahu jāgarassu|

mā taṃ alasaṃ pamattabandhu, kūṭainaiva jinātu mac‍curājā||

412.

 ‘‘saiyyathāpi sayathāpi (sī॰ pī॰) mahāsamuddavaigau, aivaṃ jātijarātivattatai taṃ|

sau karauhi sudīpamattanau tvaṃ, na hi tāṇaṃ tava vij‍jataiva añ‍ñaṃ||

413.

 ‘‘satthā hi vijaisi maggamaitaṃ, saṅgā jātijarābhayā atītaṃ|

pubbāpararattamappamattau, anuyuñ‍jassu daḷhaṃ karauhi yaugaṃ||

414.

 ‘‘purimāni  pamuñ‍ca bandhanāni, saṅghāṭikhuramuṇḍabhikkhabhaujī|

mā khiḍḍāratiñ‍ca mā niddaṃ, anuyuñ‍jittha jhāya kātiyāna||

415.

 ‘‘jhāyāhi  jināhi kātiyāna, yaugakkhaimapathaisu  kauvidausi|

pappuyya anuttaraṃ visuddhiṃ, parinibbāhisi vārināva jauti||

416.

 ‘‘paj‍jautakarau parittaraṃsau, vātaina vinamyatai latāva|

aivampi tuvaṃ anādiyānau, māraṃ indasagautta niddhunāhi|

sau vaidayitāsu vītarāgau, kālaṃ kaṅkha idhaiva sītibhūtau’’ti||

… kātiyānau thairau…|

8. migajālatthairagāthā

417.

 ‘‘sudaisitau cakkhumatā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

sabbasaṃyaujanātītau, sabbavaṭṭavināsanau||

418.

 ‘‘niyyānikau uttaraṇau, taṇhāmūlavisausanau|

visamūlaṃ āghātanaṃ, chaitvā pāpaiti nibbutiṃ||

419.

 ‘‘añ‍ñāṇamūlabhaidāya, kammayantavighāṭanau|

viñ‍ñāṇānaṃ pariggahai, ñāṇavajiranipātanau||

420.

 ‘‘vaidanānaṃ viñ‍ñāpanau, upādānappamaucanau|

bhavaṃ aṅgārakāsuṃva, ñāṇaina anupassanau anupassakau (sī॰ pī॰)||

421.

 ‘‘mahārasau sugambhīrau, jarāmac‍cunivāraṇau|

ariyau  aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, dukkhūpasamanau sivau||

422.

 ‘‘kammaṃ kammanti ñatvāna, vipākañ‍ca vipākatau|

paṭic‍cuppan‍nadhammānaṃ, yathāvālaukadassanau|

mahākhaimaṅgamau santau, pariyausānabhaddakau’’ti||

… migajālau thairau…|

9. purauhitaputtajaintatthairagāthā

423.

 ‘‘jātimadaina  mattauhaṃ, bhaugaissariyaina ca|

saṇṭhānavaṇṇarūpaina, madamattau acārihaṃ||

424.

 ‘‘nāttanau samakaṃ kañ‍ci, atiraikaṃ ca mañ‍ñisaṃ|

atimānahatau bālau, patthaddhau ussitaddhajau||

425.

 ‘‘mātaraṃ pitarañ‍cāpi, añ‍ñaipi garusammatai|

na kañ‍ci abhivādaisiṃ, mānatthaddhau anādarau||

426.

 ‘‘disvā vināyakaṃ aggaṃ, sārathīnaṃ varuttamaṃ|

tapantamiva ādic‍caṃ, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhataṃ||

427.

 ‘‘mānaṃ madañ‍ca chaḍḍaitvā, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

sirasā abhivādaisiṃ, sabbasattānamuttamaṃ||

428.

 ‘‘atimānau  ca aumānau, pahīnā susamūhatā|

asmimānau samucchin‍nau, sabbai mānavidhā hatā’’ti||

… jaintau purauhitaputtau thairau…|

10. sumanatthairagāthā

429.

 ‘‘yadā  navau pabbajitau, jātiyā sattavassikau|

iddhiyā abhibhautvāna, pan‍nagindaṃ mahiddhikaṃ||

430.

 ‘‘upajjhāyassa  udakaṃ, anautattā mahāsarā|

āharāmi tatau disvā, maṃ satthā aitadabravi’’||

431.

 ‘‘sāriputta imaṃ passa, āgacchantaṃ kumārakaṃ|

udakakumbhamādāya, ajjhattaṃ susamāhitaṃ||

432.

 ‘‘pāsādikaina vattaina, kalyāṇairiyāpathau|

sāmaṇairaunuruddhassa, iddhiyā ca visāradau||

433.

 ‘‘ājānīyaina ājañ‍ñau, sādhunā sādhukāritau|

vinītau anuruddhaina, katakic‍caina sikkhitau||

434.

 ‘‘sau patvā paramaṃ santiṃ, sacchikatvā akuppataṃ|

sāmaṇairau sa sumanau, mā maṃ jañ‍ñāti icchatī’’ti||

… sumanau thairau…|

11. nhātakamunitthairagāthā

435.

 ‘‘vātaraugābhinītau  tvaṃ, viharaṃ kānanai vanai|

paviddhagaucarai lūkhai, kathaṃ bhikkhu karissasi’’||

436.

 ‘‘pītisukhaina vipulaina, pharitvāna samussayaṃ|

lūkhampi abhisambhauntau, viharissāmi kānanai||

437.

 ‘‘bhāvaintau satta baujjhaṅgai, indriyāni balāni ca|

jhānasaukhummasampan‍nau jhānasukhumasampan‍nau (syā॰ ka॰), viharissaṃ anāsavau||

438.

 ‘‘vippamuttaṃ  kilaisaihi, suddhacittaṃ anāvilaṃ|

abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhantau, viharissaṃ anāsavau||

439.

 ‘‘ajjhattañ‍ca bahiddhā ca, yai mai vij‍jiṃsu āsavā|

sabbai asaisā ucchin‍nā, na ca uppaj‍jarai puna||

440.

 ‘‘pañ‍cakkhandhā pariñ‍ñātā, tiṭṭhanti chin‍namūlakā|

dukkhakkhayau anuppattau, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti||

… nhātakamunitthairau…|

12. brahmadattatthairagāthā

441.

 ‘‘ak‍kaudhassa kutau kaudhau, dantassa samajīvinau|

sammadañ‍ñā vimuttassa, upasantassa tādinau||

442.

 ‘‘tassaiva  taina pāpiyau, yau kuddhaṃ paṭikujjhati|

kuddhaṃ appaṭikujjhantau, saṅgāmaṃ jaiti duj‍jayaṃ||

443.

 saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.188, 250 ‘‘ubhin‍namatthaṃ carati, attanau ca parassa ca|

paraṃ saṅkupitaṃ ñatvā, yau satau upasammati||

444.

 saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.188, 250 ‘‘ubhin‍naṃ tikicchantaṃ taṃ, attanau ca parassa ca|

janā mañ‍ñanti bālauti, yai dhammassa akauvidā||

445.

 ‘‘uppaj‍jai tai sacai kaudhau, āvaj‍ja kakacūpamaṃ|

uppaj‍jai cai rasai taṇhā, puttamaṃsūpamaṃ sara||

446.

 ‘‘sacai dhāvati cittaṃ tai, kāmaisu ca bhavaisu ca|

khippaṃ  niggaṇha satiyā, kiṭṭhādaṃ viya duppasu’’nti|

… brahmadattau thairau…|

13. sirimaṇḍatthairagāthā

447.

 udā॰ 45; cūḷava॰ 385; pari॰ 339 ‘‘chan‍namativassati, vivaṭaṃ nātivassati|

tasmā chan‍naṃ vivaraitha, aivaṃ taṃ nātivassati||

448.

 saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.66; naitti॰ 18 ‘‘mac‍cunābbhahatau laukau, jarāya parivāritau|

taṇhāsal‍laina autiṇṇau, icchādhūpāyitau sadā||

449.

 ‘‘mac‍cunābbhahatau laukau, parikkhittau jarāya ca|

hañ‍ñati nic‍camattāṇau, pattadaṇḍauva tak‍karau||

450.

 ‘‘āgacchantaggikhandhāva, mac‍cu byādhi jarā tayau|

pac‍cuggantuṃ balaṃ natthi, javau natthi palāyituṃ||

451.

 ‘‘amaughaṃ divasaṃ kayirā, appaina bahukaina vā|

yaṃ yaṃ vijahatai virahatai (sī॰ pī॰), vivahatai (syā॰) rattiṃ, tadūnaṃ tassa jīvitaṃ||

452.

 ‘‘caratau tiṭṭhatau vāpi, āsīnasayanassa vā|

upaiti carimā ratti, na tai kālau pamaj‍jitu’’nti||

… sirimaṇḍau sirimandau (sī॰) thairau…|

14. sabbakāmitthairagāthā

453.

 ‘‘dvipādakauyaṃ asuci, duggandhau parihīrati pariharati (ka॰)|

nānākuṇapaparipūrau, vissavantau tatau tatau||

454.

 ‘‘migaṃ  nilīnaṃ kūṭaina, baḷisainaiva ambujaṃ|

vānaraṃ viya laipaina, bādhayanti puthuj‍janaṃ||

455.

 ‘‘rūpā  saddā rasā gandhā, phauṭṭhabbā ca manauramā|

pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā aitai, itthirūpasmi dissarai||

456.

 ‘‘yai  aitā upasaivanti, rattacittā puthuj‍janā|

vaḍḍhainti kaṭasiṃ ghauraṃ, ācinanti punabbhavaṃ||

457.

 ‘‘yau caitā parivaj‍jaiti, sappassaiva padā sirau|

saumaṃ visattikaṃ laukai, satau samativattati||

458.

 ‘‘kāmaisvādīnavaṃ  disvā, naikkhammaṃ daṭṭhu khaimatau|

nissaṭau sabbakāmaihi, pattau mai āsavakkhayau’’ti||

… sabbakāmitthairau…|

chak‍kanipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

uruvaiḷakassapau ca, thairau taikicchakāri ca|

mahānāgau ca kul‍lau ca, mālukyau mālutau (sī॰ ka॰), māluṅkyau (syā॰) sappadāsakau||

kātiyānau migajālau, jaintau sumanasavhayau|

nhātamuni brahmadattau, sirimaṇḍau sabbakāmī ca|

gāthāyau caturāsīti, thairā caittha catuddasāti||


7. sattakanipātau

1. sundarasamuddatthairagāthā

459.

 ‘‘alaṅkatā  suvasanā, māladhārī mālābhārī (sī॰), mālabhārī (syā॰) vibhūsitā|

alattakakatāpādā, pādukāruyha vaisikā||

460.

 ‘‘pādukā auruhitvāna, puratau pañ‍jalīkatā|

sā maṃ saṇhaina mudunā, mhitapubbaṃ mihitapubbaṃ (sī॰) abhāsatha’’||

461.

 ‘‘yuvāsi tvaṃ pabbajitau, tiṭṭhāhi mama sāsanai|

bhuñ‍ja mānusakai kāmai, ahaṃ vittaṃ dadāmi tai|

sac‍caṃ tai paṭijānāmi, aggiṃ vā tai harāmahaṃ||

462.

 ‘‘yadā jiṇṇā bhavissāma, ubhau daṇḍaparāyanā|

ubhaupi pabbajissāma, ubhayattha kaṭaggahau’’||

463.

 ‘‘tañ‍ca disvāna yācantiṃ, vaisikaṃ pañ‍jalīkataṃ|

alaṅkataṃ suvasanaṃ, mac‍cupāsaṃva auḍḍitaṃ||

464.

 ‘‘tatau mai manasīkārau…pai॰… nibbidā samatiṭṭhatha||

465.

 ‘‘tatau cittaṃ vimuc‍ci mai…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

… sundarasamuddau thairau…|

2. lakuṇḍakabhaddiyatthairagāthā

466.

 parai ambāṭakārāmai, vanasaṇḍamhi bhaddiyau|

samūlaṃ taṇhamabbuyha, tattha bhaddauva jhāyati bhaddau’dhijhāyāyati (sī॰), bhaddau jhiyāyati (syā॰ sī॰ aṭṭha॰)||

467.

 ‘‘ramantaikai mudiṅgaihi mutiṅgaihi (sī॰ aṭṭha॰), vīṇāhi paṇavaihi ca|

ahañ‍ca  rukkhamūlasmiṃ, ratau buddhassa sāsanai||

468.

 ‘‘buddhau cai buddhau ca (sabbattha) mai varaṃ daj‍jā, sau ca labbhaitha mai varau|

gaṇhaihaṃ sabbalaukassa, nic‍caṃ kāyagataṃ satiṃ||

469.

 ‘‘yai  maṃ rūpaina pāmiṃsu, yai ca ghausaina anvagū|

chandarāgavasūpaitā, na maṃ jānanti tai janā||

470.

 ‘‘ajjhattañ‍ca  na jānāti, bahiddhā ca na passati|

samantāvaraṇau bālau, sa vai ghausaina vuyhati||

471.

 ‘‘ajjhattañ‍ca na jānāti, bahiddhā ca vipassati|

bahiddhā phaladassāvī, saupi ghausaina vuyhati||

472.

 ‘‘ajjhattañ‍ca pajānāti, bahiddhā ca vipassati|

anāvaraṇadassāvī, na sau ghausaina vuyhatī’’ti||

… lakuṇḍakabhaddiyau thairau…|

3. bhaddatthairagāthā

473.

 ‘‘aikaputtau  ahaṃ āsiṃ, piyau mātu piyau pitu|

bahūhi vatacariyāhi, laddhau āyācanāhi ca||

474.

 ‘‘tai ca maṃ anukampāya, atthakāmā hitaisinau|

ubhau pitā ca mātā ca, buddhassa upanāmayuṃ’’||

475.

 ‘‘kicchā laddhau ayaṃ puttau, sukhumālau sukhaidhitau|

imaṃ dadāma tai nātha, jinassa paricārakaṃ’’||

476.

 ‘‘satthā ca maṃ paṭiggayha, ānandaṃ aitadabravi|

‘pabbājaihi imaṃ khippaṃ, haissatyājāniyau ayaṃ||

477.

 ‘‘pabbājaitvāna  maṃ satthā, vihāraṃ pāvisī jinau|

anauggatasmiṃ sūriyasmiṃ, tatau cittaṃ vimuc‍ci mai||

478.

 ‘‘tatau satthā nirākatvā, paṭisal‍lānavuṭṭhitau|

‘aihi bhaddā’ti maṃ āha, sā mai āsūpasampadā||

479.

 ‘‘jātiyā sattavassaina, laddhā mai upasampadā|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, ahau dhammasudhammatā’’ti||

… bhaddau thairau…|

4. saupākatthairagāthā

480.

 ‘‘disvā  pāsādachāyāyaṃ, caṅkamantaṃ naruttamaṃ|

tattha naṃ upasaṅkamma, vandissaṃ vandisaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) purisuttamaṃ||

481.

 ‘‘aikaṃsaṃ  cīvaraṃ katvā, saṃharitvāna pāṇayau|

anucaṅkamissaṃ virajaṃ, sabbasattānamuttamaṃ||

482.

 ‘‘tatau pañhai apucchi maṃ, pañhānaṃ kauvidau vidū|

acchambhī ca abhītau ca, byākāsiṃ satthunau ahaṃ||

483.

 ‘‘vissaj‍jitaisu pañhaisu, anumaudi tathāgatau|

bhikkhusaṅghaṃ vilaukaitvā, imamatthaṃ abhāsatha’’||

484.

 ‘‘lābhā aṅgānaṃ magadhānaṃ, yaisāyaṃ paribhuñ‍jati|

cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātañ‍ca, pac‍cayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ|

pac‍cuṭṭhānañ‍ca sāmīciṃ, taisaṃ lābhā’’ti cābravi||

485.

 ‘‘aj‍jataggai maṃ saupāka, dassanāyaupasaṅkama|

aisā  caiva tai saupāka, bhavatu upasampadā’’||

486.

 ‘‘jātiyā sattavassauhaṃ, laddhāna upasampadaṃ|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, ahau dhammasudhammatā’’ti||

… saupākau thairau…|

5. sarabhaṅgatthairagāthā

487.

 ‘‘sarai hatthaihi bhañ‍jitvā, katvāna kuṭimacchisaṃ|

taina mai sarabhaṅgauti, nāmaṃ sammutiyā ahu||

488.

 ‘‘na  mayhaṃ kappatai aj‍ja, sarai hatthaihi bhañ‍jituṃ|

sikkhāpadā nau pañ‍ñattā, gautamaina yasassinā||

489.

 ‘‘sakalaṃ samattaṃ raugaṃ, sarabhaṅgau nāddasaṃ pubbai|

sauyaṃ raugau diṭṭhau, vacanakarainātidaivassa||

490.

 ‘‘yainaiva maggaina gatau vipassī, yainaiva maggaina sikhī ca vaissabhū|

kakusandhakauṇāgamanau ca kassapau, tainañ‍jasaina agamāsi gautamau||

491.

 ‘‘vītataṇhā  anādānā, satta buddhā khayaugadhā|

yaihāyaṃ daisitau dhammau, dhammabhūtaihi tādibhi||

492.

 ‘‘cattāri ariyasac‍cāni, anukampāya pāṇinaṃ|

dukkhaṃ samudayau maggau, niraudhau dukkhasaṅkhayau||

493.

 ‘‘yasmiṃ  nivattatai yasmiṃ na nibbattatai (ka॰) dukkhaṃ, saṃsārasmiṃ anantakaṃ|

bhaidā  imassa kāyassa, jīvitassa ca saṅkhayā|

añ‍ñau punabbhavau natthi, suvimuttaumhi sabbadhī’’ti||

… sarabhaṅgau thairau…|

sattakanipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

sundarasamuddau thairau, thairau lakuṇḍabhaddiyau|

bhaddau thairau ca saupākau, sarabhaṅgau mahāisi|

sattakai pañ‍cakā thairā, gāthāyau pañ‍catiṃsatīti||


8. aṭṭhakanipātau

1. mahākac‍cāyanatthairagāthā

494.

 ‘‘kammaṃ  bahukaṃ na kārayai, parivaj‍jaiyya janaṃ na uyyamai|

sau ussuk‍kau rasānugiddhau, atthaṃ riñ‍cati yau sukhādhivāhau||

495.

 ‘‘paṅkauti hi naṃ avaidayuṃ, yāyaṃ vandanapūjanā kulaisu|

sukhumaṃ sal‍laṃ durubbahaṃ, sak‍kārau kāpurisaina duj‍jahau||

496.

 ‘‘na parassupanidhāya, kammaṃ mac‍cassa pāpakaṃ|

attanā taṃ na saivaiyya, kammabandhūhi mātiyā||

497.

 ‘‘na parai vacanā caurau, na parai vacanā muni|

attā ca naṃ yathāvaidi yathā vaitti (sī॰), daivāpi naṃ tathā vidū||

498.

 ‘‘parai ca na vijānanti, mayamaittha yamāmasai|

yai  ca tattha vijānanti, tatau sammanti maidhagā||

499.

 ‘‘jīvatai vāpi sappañ‍ñau, api vittaparikkhayau|

pañ‍ñāya ca alābhaina abhāvaina (sī॰ aṭṭha॰), vittavāpi na jīvati||

500.

 ‘‘sabbaṃ suṇāti sautaina, sabbaṃ passati cakkhunā|

na ca diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ dhīrau, sabbaṃ ujjhitumarahati||

501.

 ‘‘cakkhumāssa yathā andhau, sautavā badhirau yathā|

pañ‍ñavāssa yathā mūgau, balavā dubbalauriva|

atha atthai samuppan‍nai, sayaitha passaitha (ka॰) matasāyika’’nti||

… mahākac‍cāyanau thairau…|

2. sirimittatthairagāthā

502.

 ‘‘ak‍kaudhanaunupanāhī, amāyau rittapaisuṇau|

sa vai tādisakau bhikkhu, aivaṃ paic‍ca na saucati||

503.

 ‘‘ak‍kaudhanaunupanāhī, amāyau rittapaisuṇau|

guttadvārau sadā bhikkhu, aivaṃ paic‍ca na saucati||

504.

 ‘‘ak‍kaudhanaunupanāhī, amāyau rittapaisuṇau|

kalyāṇasīlau sau yau (syā॰) bhikkhu, aivaṃ paic‍ca na saucati||

505.

 ‘‘ak‍kaudhanaunupanāhī, amāyau rittapaisuṇau|

kalyāṇamittau sau bhikkhu, aivaṃ paic‍ca na saucati||

506.

 ‘‘ak‍kaudhanaunupanāhī, amāyau rittapaisuṇau|

kalyāṇapañ‍ñau sau bhikkhu, aivaṃ paic‍ca na saucati||

507.

 ‘‘yassa saddhā tathāgatai, acalā suppatiṭṭhitā|

sīlañ‍ca  yassa kalyāṇaṃ, ariyakantaṃ pasaṃsitaṃ||

508.

 ‘‘saṅghai pasādau yassatthi, ujubhūtañ‍ca dassanaṃ|

‘adaliddau’ti taṃ āhu, amaughaṃ tassa jīvitaṃ||

509.

 ‘‘tasmā saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca, pasādaṃ dhammadassanaṃ|

anuyuñ‍jaitha maidhāvī, saraṃ buddhāna sāsana’’nti||

… sirimittau thairau…|

3. mahāpanthakatthairagāthā

510.

 ‘‘yadā paṭhamamaddakkhiṃ, satthāramakutaubhayaṃ|

tatau mai ahu saṃvaigau, passitvā purisuttamaṃ||

511.

 ‘‘siriṃ hatthaihi pādaihi, yau paṇāmaiyya āgataṃ|

aitādisaṃ sau satthāraṃ, ārādhaitvā virādhayai||

512.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ puttadārañ‍ca, dhanadhañ‍ñañ‍ca chaḍḍayiṃ|

kaisamassūni chaidaitvā, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ||

513.

 ‘‘sikkhāsājīvasampan‍nau, indriyaisu susaṃvutau|

namassamānau sambuddhaṃ, vihāsiṃ aparājitau||

514.

 ‘‘tatau mai paṇidhī āsi, caitasau abhipatthitau|

na nisīdai muhuttampi, taṇhāsal‍lai anūhatai||

515.

 ‘‘tassa maivaṃ viharatau, passa vīriyaparak‍kamaṃ|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

516.

 ‘‘pubbainivāsaṃ  jānāmi, dibbacakkhu visaudhitaṃ|

arahā dakkhiṇaiyyaumhi, vippamuttau nirūpadhi||

517.

 ‘‘tatau  ratyā vivasānai vivasanai (sī॰ syā॰), sūriyassuggamanaṃ pati|

sabbaṃ taṇhaṃ visausaitvā, pal‍laṅkaina upāvisi’’nti||

… mahāpanthakau thairau…|

aṭṭhakanipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

mahākac‍cāyanau thairau, sirimittau mahāpanthakau|

aitai aṭṭhanipātamhi, gāthāyau catuvīsatīti||


9. navakanipātau

1. bhūtatthairagāthā

518.

 ‘‘yadā  dukkhaṃ jarāmaraṇanti paṇḍitau, aviddasū yattha sitā puthuj‍janā|

dukkhaṃ pariñ‍ñāya satauva jhāyati, tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindati||

519.

 ‘‘yadā dukkhassāvahaniṃ visattikaṃ, papañ‍casaṅghātadukhādhivāhiniṃ|

taṇhaṃ pahantvāna satauva jhāyati, tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindati||

520.

 ‘‘yadā sivaṃ dvaicaturaṅgagāminaṃ, magguttamaṃ  sabbakilaisasaudhanaṃ|

pañ‍ñāya passitva satauva jhāyati, tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindati||

521.

 ‘‘yadā asaukaṃ virajaṃ asaṅkhataṃ, santaṃ padaṃ sabbakilaisasaudhanaṃ|

bhāvaiti sañ‍ñaujanabandhanacchidaṃ, tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindati||

522.

 ‘‘yadā nabhai gaj‍jati maighadundubhi, dhārākulā vihagapathai samantatau|

bhikkhū ca pabbhāragatauva jhāyati, tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindati||

523.

 ‘‘yadā nadīnaṃ kusumākulānaṃ, vicitta-vānaiyya-vaṭaṃsakānaṃ|

tīrai nisin‍nau sumanauva jhāyati, tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindati||

524.

 ‘‘yadā  nisīthai rahitamhi kānanai, daivai gaḷantamhi nadanti dāṭhinau|

bhikkhū ca pabbhāragatauva jhāyati, tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindati||

525.

 ‘‘yadā  vitak‍kai uparundhiyattanau, nagantarai nagavivaraṃ samassitau|

vītaddarau vītakhilauva jhāyati, tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindati||

526.

 ‘‘yadā  sukhī malakhilasaukanāsanau, niraggaḷau nibbanathau visal‍lau|

sabbāsavai byantikatauva jhāyati, tatau ratiṃ paramataraṃ na vindatī’’ti||

… bhūtau thairau…|

navakanipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

bhūtau tathaddasau thairau, aikau khaggavisāṇavā|

navakamhi nipātamhi, gāthāyaupi imā navāti||


10. dasakanipātau

1. kāḷudāyitthairagāthā

527.

 ‘‘aṅgārinau  dāni dumā bhadantai, phalaisinau chadanaṃ vippahāya|

tai ac‍cimantauva pabhāsayanti, samayau mahāvīra bhāgī rasānaṃ||

528.

 ‘‘dumāni  phul‍lāni manauramāni, samantatau sabbadisā pavanti|

pattaṃ pahāya phalamāsasānā phalamāsamānau (ka॰), kālau itau pak‍kamanāya vīra||

529.

 ‘‘naivātisītaṃ na panātiuṇhaṃ, sukhā utu addhaniyā bhadantai|

passantu taṃ sākiyā kauḷiyā ca, pacchāmukhaṃ rauhiniyaṃ tarantaṃ||

530.

 ‘‘āsāya kasatai khaittaṃ, bījaṃ āsāya vappati|

āsāya vāṇijā yanti, samuddaṃ dhanahārakā|

yāya āsāya tiṭṭhāmi, sā mai āsā samijjhatu||

531.

 saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.198 ‘‘punappunaṃ caiva vapanti bījaṃ, punappunaṃ vassati daivarājā|

punappunaṃ khaittaṃ kasanti kassakā, punappunaṃ dhañ‍ñamupaiti raṭṭhaṃ||

532.

 saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.198 ‘‘punappunaṃ yācanakā caranti, punappunaṃ dānapatī dadanti|

punappunaṃ dānapatī daditvā, punappunaṃ saggamupainti ṭhānaṃ||

533.

 ‘‘vīrau  havai sattayugaṃ punaiti, yasmiṃ kulai jāyati bhūripañ‍ñau|

mañ‍ñāmahaṃ sak‍kati daivadaivau, tayā hi jātau tayābhijātau (sī॰) muni sac‍canāmau||

534.

 ‘‘suddhaudanau  nāma pitā mahaisinau, buddhassa mātā pana māyanāmā|

yā baudhisattaṃ parihariya kucchinā, kāyassa bhaidā tidivamhi maudati||

535.

 ‘‘sā gautamī kālakatā itau cutā, dibbaihi kāmaihi samaṅgibhūtā|

sā maudati kāmaguṇaihi pañ‍cahi, parivāritā daivagaṇaihi taihi||

536.

 ‘‘buddhassa puttaumhi asayhasāhinau, aṅgīrasassappaṭimassa tādinau|

pitupitā mayhaṃ tuvaṃsi sak‍ka, dhammaina mai gautama ayyakausī’’ti||

… kāḷudāyī thairau…|

2. aikavihāriyatthairagāthā

537.

 ‘‘puratau  pacchatau vāpi, aparau cai na vij‍jati|

atīva phāsu bhavati, aikassa vasatau vanai||

538.

 ‘‘handa  aikau gamissāmi, arañ‍ñaṃ buddhavaṇṇitaṃ|

phāsu phāsuṃ (syā॰ pī॰) aikavihārissa, pahitattassa bhikkhunau||

539.

 ‘‘yaugī-pītikaraṃ rammaṃ, mattakuñ‍jarasaivitaṃ|

aikau attavasī khippaṃ, pavisissāmi kānanaṃ||

540.

 ‘‘supupphitai sītavanai, sītalai girikandarai|

gattāni parisiñ‍citvā, caṅkamissāmi aikakau||

541.

 ‘‘aikākiyau adutiyau, ramaṇīyai mahāvanai|

kadāhaṃ viharissāmi, katakic‍cau anāsavau||

542.

 ‘‘aivaṃ mai kattukāmassa, adhippāyau samijjhatu|

sādhiyissāmahaṃyaiva, nāñ‍ñau añ‍ñassa kārakau||

543.

 ‘‘aisa  bandhāmi san‍nāhaṃ, pavisissāmi kānanaṃ|

na tatau nikkhamissāmi, appattau āsavakkhayaṃ||

544.

 ‘‘mālutai upavāyantai, sītai surabhigandhikai gandhakai (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰)|

avij‍jaṃ dālayissāmi, nisin‍nau nagamuddhani||

545.

 ‘‘vanai kusumasañchan‍nai, pabbhārai nūna sītalai|

vimuttisukhaina sukhitau, ramissāmi giribbajai||

546.

 ‘‘sauhaṃ  paripuṇṇasaṅkappau, candau pan‍narasau yathā|

sabbāsavaparikkhīṇau, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti||

… aikavihāriyau thairau…|

3. mahākappinatthairagāthā

547.

 ‘‘anāgataṃ  yau paṭikac‍ca paṭigac‍ca (sī॰) passati, hitañ‍ca atthaṃ ahitañ‍ca taṃ dvayaṃ|

viddaisinau tassa hitaisinau vā, randhaṃ na passanti samaikkhamānā||

548.

 paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.160 paṭisambhidāmaggai ‘‘ānāpānasatī yassa, paripuṇṇā subhāvitā|

anupubbaṃ paricitā, yathā buddhaina daisitā|

saumaṃ laukaṃ pabhāsaiti, abbhā muttauva candimā||

549.

 ‘‘audātaṃ  vata mai cittaṃ, appamāṇaṃ subhāvitaṃ|

nibbiddhaṃ paggahītañ‍ca, sabbā aubhāsatai disā||

550.

 ‘‘jīvatai vāpi sappañ‍ñau, api vittaparikkhayau|

pañ‍ñāya ca alābhaina, vittavāpi na jīvati||

551.

 ‘‘pañ‍ñā sutavinicchinī, pañ‍ñā kittisilaukavaddhanī|

pañ‍ñāsahitau narau idha, api dukkhaisu sukhāni vindati||

552.

 ‘‘nāyaṃ aj‍jatanau dhammau, nacchairau napi abbhutau|

yattha jāyaitha mīyaitha, tattha kiṃ viya abbhutaṃ||

553.

 ‘‘anantaraṃ hi jātassa, jīvitā maraṇaṃ dhuvaṃ|

jātā jātā marantīdha, aivaṃdhammā hi pāṇinau||

554.

 ‘‘na  haitadatthāya matassa hauti, yaṃ jīvitatthaṃ parapaurisānaṃ|

matamhi ruṇṇaṃ na yasau na laukyaṃ, na  vaṇṇitaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇaihi||

555.

 ‘‘cakkhuṃ sarīraṃ upahanti taina upahanti ruṇṇaṃ (sī॰), upahanti rauṇṇaṃ (syā॰ pī॰), nihīyati vaṇṇabalaṃ matī ca|

ānandinau tassa disā bhavanti, hitaisinau nāssa sukhī bhavanti||

556.

 ‘‘tasmā hi icchaiyya kulai vasantai, maidhāvinau caiva bahussutai ca|

yaisaṃ  hi pañ‍ñāvibhavaina kic‍caṃ, taranti nāvāya nadiṃva puṇṇa’’nti||

… mahākappinau thairau…|

4. cūḷapanthakatthairagāthā

557.

 ‘‘dandhā mayhaṃ gatī āsi, paribhūtau purai ahaṃ|

bhātā ca maṃ paṇāmaisi, ‘gaccha dāni tuvaṃ gharaṃ’||

558.

 ‘‘sauhaṃ paṇāmitau santau bhātā (aṭṭha॰), saṅghārāmassa kauṭṭhakai|

dummanau tattha aṭṭhāsiṃ, sāsanasmiṃ apaikkhavā||

559.

 ‘‘bhagavā tattha āgacchi āgañchi (sī॰ pī॰), sīsaṃ mayhaṃ parāmasi|

bāhāya maṃ gahaitvāna, saṅghārāmaṃ pavaisayi||

560.

 ‘‘anukampāya mai satthā, pādāsi pādapuñchaniṃ|

‘aitaṃ suddhaṃ adhiṭṭhaihi, aikamantaṃ svadhiṭṭhitaṃ’||

561.

 ‘‘tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā, vihāsiṃ sāsanai ratau|

samādhiṃ  paṭipādaisiṃ, uttamatthassa pattiyā||

562.

 ‘‘pubbainivāsaṃ  jānāmi, dibbacakkhu visaudhitaṃ|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

563.

 ‘‘sahassakkhattumattānaṃ, nimminitvāna panthakau|

nisīdambavanai rammai, yāva kālappavaidanā||

564.

 ‘‘tatau mai satthā pāhaisi, dūtaṃ kālappavaidakaṃ|

pavaiditamhi kālamhi, vaihāsādupasaṅkamiṃ vaihāsānupasaṅkamiṃ (syā॰ ka॰)||

565.

 ‘‘vanditvā satthunau pādai, aikamantaṃ nisīdahaṃ|

nisin‍naṃ maṃ viditvāna, atha satthā paṭiggahi||

566.

 ‘‘āyāgau sabbalaukassa, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ manussānaṃ, paṭigaṇhittha dakkhiṇa’’nti||

… cūḷapanthakau thairau…|

5. kappatthairagāthā

567.

 ‘‘nānākulamalasampuṇṇau, mahāuk‍kārasambhavau|

candanikaṃva paripak‍kaṃ, mahāgaṇḍau mahāvaṇau||

568.

 ‘‘pubbaruhirasampuṇṇau, gūthakūpaina gāḷhitau gūthakūpai nigāḷhitau (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰)|

āpaupaggharaṇau kāyau, sadā sandati pūtikaṃ||

569.

 ‘‘saṭṭhikaṇḍarasambandhau, maṃsalaipanalaipitau|

cammakañ‍cukasan‍naddhau, pūtikāyau niratthakau||

570.

 ‘‘aṭṭhisaṅghātaghaṭitau, nhārusuttanibandhanau|

naikaisaṃ saṃgatībhāvā, kappaiti iriyāpathaṃ||

571.

 ‘‘dhuvappayātau  maraṇāya, mac‍curājassa santikai|

idhaiva chaḍḍayitvāna, yainakāmaṅgamau narau||

572.

 ‘‘avij‍jāya nivutau kāyau, catuganthaina ganthitau|

aughasaṃsīdanau kāyau, anusayajālamautthatau||

573.

 ‘‘pañ‍canīvaraṇai yuttau, vitak‍kaina samappitau|

taṇhāmūlainānugatau, mauhacchādanachāditau||

574.

 ‘‘aivāyaṃ vattatai kāyau, kammayantaina yantitau|

sampatti ca vipatyantā, nānābhāvau vipaj‍jati||

575.

 ‘‘yaimaṃ  kāyaṃ mamāyanti, andhabālā puthuj‍janā|

vaḍḍhainti kaṭasiṃ ghauraṃ, ādiyanti punabbhavaṃ||

576.

 ‘‘yaimaṃ kāyaṃ vivaj‍jainti, gūthalittaṃva pan‍nagaṃ|

bhavamūlaṃ vamitvāna, parinibbissantināsavā’’ti parinibbantunāsavā (sī॰)||

… kappau thairau…|

6. vaṅgantaputtaupasainatthairagāthā

577.

 ‘‘vivittaṃ appanigghausaṃ, vāḷamiganisaivitaṃ|

saivai saināsanaṃ bhikkhu, paṭisal‍lānakāraṇā||

578.

 ‘‘saṅkārapuñ‍jā āhatvā āhitvā (ka॰), susānā rathiyāhi ca|

tatau saṅghāṭikaṃ katvā, lūkhaṃ dhāraiyya cīvaraṃ||

579.

 ‘‘nīcaṃ manaṃ karitvāna, sapadānaṃ kulā kulaṃ|

piṇḍikāya carai bhikkhu, guttadvārau susaṃvutau||

580.

 ‘‘lūkhainapi vā lūkhainapi ca (bahūsu) santussai, nāñ‍ñaṃ patthai rasaṃ bahuṃ|

rasaisu  anugiddhassa, jhānai na ramatī manau||

581.

 ‘‘appicchau caiva santuṭṭhau, pavivittau vasai muni|

asaṃsaṭṭhau gahaṭṭhaihi, anāgāraihi cūbhayaṃ||

582.

 ‘‘yathā jaḷau va mūgau va, attānaṃ dassayai tathā|

nātivailaṃ sambhāsaiyya, saṅghamajjhamhi paṇḍitau||

583.

 ‘‘na sau upavadai kañ‍ci, upaghātaṃ vivaj‍jayai|

saṃvutau pātimaukkhasmiṃ, mattañ‍ñū cassa bhaujanai||

584.

 ‘‘suggahītanimittassa, cittassuppādakauvidau|

samaṃ anuyuñ‍jaiyya, kālaina ca vipassanaṃ||

585.

 ‘‘vīriyasātac‍casampan‍nau, yuttayaugau sadā siyā|

na ca appatvā dukkhantaṃ, vissāsaṃ aiyya paṇḍitau||

586.

 ‘‘aivaṃ viharamānassa, suddhikāmassa bhikkhunau|

khīyanti āsavā sabbai, nibbutiñ‍cādhigacchatī’’ti||

… upasainau vaṅgantaputtau thairau…|

7. (apara)-gautamatthairagāthā

587.

 ‘‘vijānaiyya  sakaṃ atthaṃ, avalaukaiyyātha pāvacanaṃ|

yañ‍caittha assa patirūpaṃ, sāmañ‍ñaṃ ajjhupagatassa||

588.

 ‘‘mittaṃ idha ca kalyāṇaṃ, sikkhā vipulaṃ samādānaṃ|

sussūsā ca garūnaṃ, aitaṃ samaṇassa patirūpaṃ||

589.

 ‘‘buddhaisu sagāravatā, dhammai apaciti yathābhūtaṃ|

saṅghai  ca cittikārau, aitaṃ samaṇassa patirūpaṃ||

590.

 ‘‘ācāragaucarai yuttau, ājīvau saudhitau agārayhau|

cittassa ca saṇṭhapanaṃ, aitaṃ samaṇassa patirūpaṃ||

591.

 ‘‘cārittaṃ atha vārittaṃ, iriyāpathiyaṃ pasādaniyaṃ|

adhicittai ca āyaugau, aitaṃ samaṇassa patirūpaṃ||

592.

 ‘‘ārañ‍ñakāni saināsanāni, pantāni appasaddāni|

bhajitabbāni muninā, aitaṃ samaṇassa patirūpaṃ||

593.

 ‘‘sīlañ‍ca bāhusac‍cañ‍ca, dhammānaṃ pavicayau yathābhūtaṃ|

sac‍cānaṃ abhisamayau, aitaṃ samaṇassa patirūpaṃ||

594.

 ‘‘bhāvaiyya ca anic‍canti, anattasañ‍ñaṃ asubhasañ‍ñañ‍ca|

laukamhi ca anabhiratiṃ, aitaṃ samaṇassa patirūpaṃ||

595.

 ‘‘bhāvaiyya ca baujjhaṅgai, iddhipādāni indriyāni balāni|

aṭṭhaṅgamaggamariyaṃ, aitaṃ samaṇassa patirūpaṃ||

596.

 ‘‘taṇhaṃ pajahaiyya muni, samūlakai āsavai padālaiyya|

viharaiyya vippamuttau, aitaṃ samaṇassa patirūpa’’nti||

… gautamau thairau…|

dasakanipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

kāḷudāyī ca sau thairau, aikavihārī ca kappinau|

cūḷapanthakau kappau ca, upasainau ca gautamau|

sattimai dasakai thairā, gāthāyau caittha sattatīti||


11. aikādasanipātau

1. saṃkic‍catthairagāthā

597.

 ‘‘kiṃ  tavatthau vanai tāta, uj‍juhānauva pāvusai|

vairambhā ramaṇīyā tai, pavivaikau hi jhāyinaṃ||

598.

 ‘‘yathā abbhāni vairambhau, vātau nudati pāvusai|

sañ‍ñā mai abhikiranti, vivaikapaṭisañ‍ñutā||

599.

 ‘‘apaṇḍarau aṇḍasambhavau, sīvathikāya nikaitacārikau|

uppādayataiva mai satiṃ, sandaihasmiṃ virāganissitaṃ||

600.

 ‘‘yañ‍ca añ‍ñai na rakkhanti, yau ca añ‍ñai na rakkhati|

sa vai bhikkhu sukhaṃ saiti, kāmaisu anapaikkhavā||

601.

 ‘‘acchaudikā puthusilā, gaunaṅgulamigāyutā|

ambusaivālasañchan‍nā, tai sailā ramayanti maṃ||

602.

 ‘‘vasitaṃ mai arañ‍ñaisu, kandarāsu guhāsu ca|

saināsanaisu pantaisu, vāḷamiganisaivitai||

603.

 ‘‘‘imai hañ‍ñantu vajjhantu, dukkhaṃ pappauntu pāṇinau’|

saṅkappaṃ nābhijānāmi, anariyaṃ dausasaṃhitaṃ||

604.

 ‘‘pariciṇṇau mayā satthā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ|

auhitau garukau bhārau, bhavanaitti samūhatā||

605.

 ‘‘yassa catthāya yassatthāya (sī॰) pabbajitau, agārasmānagāriyaṃ|

sau mai atthau anuppattau, sabbasaṃyaujanakkhayau||

606.

 ‘‘nābhinandāmi  maraṇaṃ, nābhinandāmi jīvitaṃ|

kālañ‍ca paṭikaṅkhāmi, nibbisaṃ bhatakau yathā||

607.

 ‘‘nābhinandāmi  maraṇaṃ, nābhinandāmi jīvitaṃ|

kālañ‍ca paṭikaṅkhāmi, sampajānau patissatau’’ti||

… saṃkic‍cau thairau…|

aikādasanipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

saṃkic‍cathairau aikauva, katakic‍cau anāsavau|

aikādasanipātamhi, gāthā aikādasaiva cāti||


12. dvādasakanipātau

1. sīlavatthairagāthā

608.

 ‘‘sīlamaividha  sikkhaitha, asmiṃ laukai susikkhitaṃ|

sīlaṃ hi sabbasampattiṃ, upanāmaiti saivitaṃ||

609.

 ‘‘sīlaṃ rakkhaiyya maidhāvī, patthayānau tayau sukhai|

pasaṃsaṃ vittilābhañ‍ca, paic‍ca saggai pamaudanaṃ paic‍ca saggai ca maudanaṃ (sī॰ pī॰)||

610.

 ‘‘sīlavā hi bahū mittai, sañ‍ñamainādhigacchati|

dussīlau pana mittaihi, dhaṃsatai pāpamācaraṃ||

611.

 ‘‘avaṇṇañ‍ca akittiñ‍ca, dussīlau labhatai narau|

vaṇṇaṃ kittiṃ pasaṃsañ‍ca, sadā labhati sīlavā||

612.

 ‘‘ādi  sīlaṃ patiṭṭhā ca, kalyāṇānañ‍ca mātukaṃ|

pamukhaṃ sabbadhammānaṃ, tasmā sīlaṃ visaudhayai||

613.

 ‘‘vailā ca saṃvaraṃ sīlaṃ saṃvarau sīlaṃ (sī॰), saṃvarasīlaṃ (sī॰ aṭṭha॰), cittassa abhihāsanaṃ|

titthañ‍ca sabbabuddhānaṃ, tasmā sīlaṃ visaudhayai||

614.

 ‘‘sīlaṃ balaṃ appaṭimaṃ, sīlaṃ āvudhamuttamaṃ|

sīlamābharaṇaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ, sīlaṃ kavacamabbhutaṃ||

615.

 ‘‘sīlaṃ saitu mahaisakkhau, sīlaṃ gandhau anuttarau|

sīlaṃ vilaipanaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ, yaina vāti disaudisaṃ||

616.

 ‘‘sīlaṃ sambalamaivaggaṃ, sīlaṃ pāthaiyyamuttamaṃ|

sīlaṃ saiṭṭhau ativāhau, yaina yāti disaudisaṃ||

617.

 ‘‘idhaiva nindaṃ labhati, paic‍cāpāyai ca dummanau|

sabbattha dummanau bālau, sīlaisu asamāhitau||

618.

 ‘‘idhaiva kittiṃ labhati, paic‍ca saggai ca summanau|

sabbattha sumanau dhīrau, sīlaisu susamāhitau||

619.

 ‘‘sīlamaiva  idha aggaṃ, pañ‍ñavā pana uttamau|

manussaisu ca daivaisu, sīlapañ‍ñāṇatau jaya’’nti||

… sīlavau thairau…|

2. sunītatthairagāthā

620.

 ‘‘nīcai  kulamhi jātauhaṃ, daliddau appabhaujanau|

hīnakammaṃ hīnaṃ kammaṃ (syā॰) mamaṃ āsi, ahausiṃ pupphachaḍḍakau||

621.

 ‘‘jigucchitau  manussānaṃ, paribhūtau ca vambhitau|

nīcaṃ manaṃ karitvāna, vandissaṃ bahukaṃ janaṃ||

622.

 ‘‘athaddasāsiṃ  sambuddhaṃ, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhataṃ|

pavisantaṃ mahāvīraṃ, magadhānaṃ puruttamaṃ||

623.

 ‘‘nikkhipitvāna byābhaṅgiṃ, vandituṃ upasaṅkamiṃ|

mamaiva anukampāya, aṭṭhāsi purisuttamau||

624.

 ‘‘vanditvā satthunau pādai, aikamantaṃ ṭhitau tadā|

pabbaj‍jaṃ ahamāyāciṃ, sabbasattānamuttamaṃ||

625.

 ‘‘tatau kāruṇikau satthā, sabbalaukānukampakau|

‘aihi bhikkhū’ti maṃ āha, sā mai āsūpasampadā||

626.

 ‘‘sauhaṃ aikau arañ‍ñasmiṃ, viharantau atanditau|

akāsiṃ satthuvacanaṃ, yathā maṃ auvadī jinau||

627.

 ‘‘rattiyā paṭhamaṃ yāmaṃ, pubbajātimanussariṃ|

rattiyā majjhimaṃ yāmaṃ, dibbacakkhuṃ visaudhayiṃ dibbacakkhu visaudhitaṃ (ka॰)|

rattiyā pacchimai yāmai, tamaukhandhaṃ padālayiṃ||

628.

 ‘‘tatau ratyā vivasānai, sūriyassuggamanaṃ pati|

indau brahmā ca āgantvā, maṃ namassiṃsu pañ‍jalī||

629.

 ‘‘‘namau tai purisājañ‍ña, namau tai purisuttama|

yassa tai āsavā khīṇā, dakkhiṇaiyyausi mārisa’||

630.

 ‘‘tatau  disvāna maṃ satthā, daivasaṅghapurakkhataṃ|

sitaṃ pātukaritvāna, imamatthaṃ abhāsatha||

631.

 su॰ ni॰ 660 suttanipātaipi ‘‘‘tapaina  brahmacariyaina, saṃyamaina damaina ca|

aitaina brāhmaṇau hauti, aitaṃ brāhmaṇamuttama’’’nti||

… sunītau thairau…|

dvādasakanipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

sīlavā ca sunītau ca, thairā dvai tai mahiddhikā|

dvādasamhi nipātamhi, gāthāyau catuvīsatīti||


13. tairasanipātau

1. sauṇakauḷivisatthairagāthā

632.

 ‘‘yāhu  raṭṭhai samuk‍kaṭṭhau, rañ‍ñau aṅgassa paddhagū patthagū (syā॰), paṭṭhagū (ka॰)|

svāj‍ja dhammaisu uk‍kaṭṭhau, sauṇau dukkhassa pāragū||

633.

 ‘‘pañ‍ca chindai pañ‍ca jahai, pañ‍ca cuttari bhāvayai|

pañ‍casaṅgātigau bhikkhu, aughatiṇṇauti vuc‍cati||

634.

 ‘‘un‍naḷassa pamattassa, bāhirāsassa bāhirāsayassa (ka॰) bhikkhunau|

sīlaṃ samādhi pañ‍ñā ca, pāripūriṃ na gacchati||

635.

 ‘‘yañhi kic‍caṃ apaviddhaṃ tadapaviddhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), akic‍caṃ pana karīyati|

un‍naḷānaṃ  pamattānaṃ, taisaṃ vaḍḍhanti āsavā||

636.

 ‘‘yaisañ‍ca susamāraddhā, nic‍caṃ kāyagatā sati|

akic‍caṃ tai na saivanti, kic‍cai sātac‍cakārinau|

satānaṃ sampajānānaṃ, atthaṃ gacchanti āsavā||

637.

 ‘‘ujumaggamhi akkhātai, gacchatha mā nivattatha|

attanā caudayattānaṃ, nibbānamabhihārayai||

638.

 ‘‘ac‍cāraddhamhi vīriyamhi, satthā laukai anuttarau|

vīṇaupamaṃ karitvā mai, dhammaṃ daisaisi cakkhumā|

tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā, vihāsiṃ sāsanai ratau||

639.

 ‘‘samathaṃ paṭipādaisiṃ, uttamatthassa pattiyā|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

640.

 ‘‘naikkhammai nikkhamai (ka॰), naikkhammaṃ (mahāva॰ 244; a॰ ni॰ 6.55) adhimuttassa, pavivaikañ‍ca caitasau|

abyāpajjhādhimuttassa abyāpajjhādhimhattassa (ka॰), upādānakkhayassa ca||

641.

 ‘‘taṇhakkhayādhimuttassa, asammauhañ‍ca caitasau|

disvā āyatanuppādaṃ, sammā cittaṃ vimuc‍cati||

642.

 ‘‘tassa  sammā vimuttassa, santacittassa bhikkhunau|

katassa paṭicayau natthi, karaṇīyaṃ na vij‍jati||

643.

 ‘‘sailau  yathā aikaghanau aikaghanau (ka॰), vātaina na samīrati|

aivaṃ rūpā rasā saddā, gandhā phassā ca kaivalā||

644.

 ‘‘iṭṭhā  dhammā aniṭṭhā ca, nappavaidhainti tādinau|

ṭhitaṃ cittaṃ visañ‍ñuttaṃ, vayañ‍cassānupassatī’’ti||

… sauṇau kauḷivisau thairau…|

tairasanipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

sauṇau kauḷivisau thairau, aikauyaiva mahiddhikau|

tairasamhi nipātamhi, gāthāyau caittha tairasāti||


14. cuddasakanipātau

1. khadiravaniyaraivatatthairagāthā

645.

 ‘‘yadā  ahaṃ pabbajitau, agārasmānagāriyaṃ|

nābhijānāmi saṅkappaṃ, anariyaṃ dausasaṃhitaṃ||

646.

 ‘‘‘imai hañ‍ñantu vajjhantu, dukkhaṃ pappauntu pāṇinau’|

saṅkappaṃ nābhijānāmi, imasmiṃ dīghamantarai||

647.

 ‘‘maittañ‍ca abhijānāmi, appamāṇaṃ subhāvitaṃ|

anupubbaṃ paricitaṃ, yathā buddhaina daisitaṃ||

648.

 ‘‘sabbamittau sabbasakhau, sabbabhūtānukampakau|

maittacittañ‍ca maittaṃ cittaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) bhāvaimi, abyāpaj‍jaratau abyāpajjharatau (sī॰ syā॰) sadā||

649.

 ‘‘asaṃhīraṃ asaṃkuppaṃ, cittaṃ āmaudayāmahaṃ|

brahmavihāraṃ bhāvaimi, akāpurisasaivitaṃ||

650.

 ‘‘avitak‍kaṃ  samāpan‍nau, sammāsambuddhasāvakau|

ariyaina tuṇhībhāvaina, upaitau hauti tāvadai||

651.

 ‘‘yathāpi pabbatau sailau, acalau suppatiṭṭhitau|

aivaṃ mauhakkhayā bhikkhu, pabbatauva na vaidhati||

652.

 ‘‘anaṅgaṇassa pausassa, nic‍caṃ sucigavaisinau|

vālaggamattaṃ pāpassa, abbhamattaṃva khāyati||

653.

 ‘‘nagaraṃ yathā pac‍cantaṃ, guttaṃ santarabāhiraṃ|

aivaṃ gaupaitha attānaṃ, khaṇau vau mā upac‍cagā||

654.

 ‘‘nābhinandāmi maraṇaṃ, nābhinandāmi jīvitaṃ|

kālañ‍ca paṭikaṅkhāmi, nibbisaṃ bhatakau yathā||

655.

 ‘‘nābhinandāmi maraṇaṃ…pai॰… sampajānau patissatau||

656.

 ‘‘pariciṇṇau mayā satthā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ|

auhitau garukau bhārau, bhavanaitti samūhatā||

657.

 ‘‘yassa  catthāya pabbajitau, agārasmānagāriyaṃ|

sau mai atthau anuppattau, sabbasaṃyaujanakkhayau||

658.

 ‘‘sampādaithappamādaina, aisā mai anusāsanī|

handāhaṃ parinibbissaṃ, vippamuttaumhi sabbadhī’’ti||

… khadiravaniyaraivatau thairau…|

2. gaudattatthairagāthā

659.

 ‘‘yathāpi bhaddau ājañ‍ñau, dhurai yuttau dhurassahau dhurāsahau (aṭṭha॰)|

mathitau  atibhāraina, saṃyugaṃ nātivattati||

660.

 ‘‘aivaṃ pañ‍ñāya yai tittā, samuddau vārinā yathā|

na parai atimañ‍ñanti, ariyadhammauva pāṇinaṃ||

661.

 ‘‘kālai  kālavasaṃ pattā, bhavābhavavasaṃ gatā|

narā dukkhaṃ nigacchanti, taidha saucanti māṇavā mānavā (sī॰)||

662.

 ‘‘un‍natā sukhadhammaina, dukkhadhammaina caunatā|

dvayaina bālā hañ‍ñanti, yathābhūtaṃ adassinau||
