663.

 ‘‘yai ca dukkhai sukhasmiñ‍ca, majjhai sibbinimac‍cagū|

ṭhitā tai indakhīlauva, na tai un‍nataaunatā||

664.

 ‘‘na haiva lābhai nālābhai, na yasai na ca kittiyā|

na nindāyaṃ pasaṃsāya, na tai dukkhai sukhamhi||

665.

 ‘‘sabbattha tai na limpanti, udabinduva paukkharai|

sabbattha sukhitā dhīrā, sabbattha aparājitā||

666.

 ‘‘dhammaina ca alābhau yau, yau ca lābhau adhammikau|

alābhau dhammikau saiyyau, yaṃ cai lābhau adhammikau||

667.

 ‘‘yasau ca appabuddhīnaṃ, viñ‍ñūnaṃ ayasau ca yau|

ayasauva saiyyau viñ‍ñūnaṃ, na yasau appabuddhinaṃ||

668.

 ‘‘dummaidhaihi  pasaṃsā ca, viñ‍ñūhi garahā ca yā|

garahāva saiyyau viñ‍ñūhi, yaṃ cai bālappasaṃsanā||

669.

 ‘‘sukhañ‍ca  kāmamayikaṃ, dukkhañ‍ca pavivaikiyaṃ|

pavivaikadukkhaṃ saiyyau, yaṃ cai kāmamayaṃ sukhaṃ||

670.

 ‘‘jīvitañ‍ca adhammaina, dhammaina maraṇañ‍ca yaṃ|

maraṇaṃ dhammikaṃ saiyyau, yaṃ cai jīvai adhammikaṃ||

671.

 ‘‘kāmakaupappahīnā yai, santacittā bhavābhavai|

caranti laukai asitā, natthi taisaṃ piyāpiyaṃ||

672.

 ‘‘bhāvayitvāna baujjhaṅgai, indriyāni balāni ca|

pappuyya paramaṃ santiṃ, parinibbantināsavā’’ti||

… gaudattau thairau…|

cuddasakanipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

raivatau caiva gaudattau, thairā dvai tai mahiddhikā|

cuddasamhi nipātamhi, gāthāyau aṭṭhavīsatīti||


15. sauḷasakanipātau

1. añ‍ñāsikauṇḍañ‍ñatthairagāthā

673.

 ‘‘aisa  bhiyyau pasīdāmi, sutvā dhammaṃ mahārasaṃ|

virāgau daisitau dhammau, anupādāya sabbasau||

674.

 ‘‘bahūni laukai citrāni, asmiṃ pathavimaṇḍalai|

mathainti  mañ‍ñai saṅkappaṃ, subhaṃ rāgūpasaṃhitaṃ||

675.

 ‘‘rajamuhatañ‍ca vātaina, yathā maighaupasammayai|

aivaṃ sammanti saṅkappā, yadā pañ‍ñāya passati||

676.

 dha॰ pa॰ 277 dhammapadai ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cāti, yadā pañ‍ñāya passati|

atha nibbindati dukkhai, aisa maggau visuddhiyā||

677 .

 dha॰ pa॰ 278 dhammapadai ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhāti, yadā pañ‍ñāya passati

atha nibbindati dukkhai, aisa maggau visuddhiyā||

678.

 dha॰ pa॰ 279 dhammapadai ‘‘sabbai dhammā anattāti, yadā pañ‍ñāya passati|

atha nibbindati dukkhai, aisa maggau visuddhiyā||

679.

 ‘‘buddhānubuddhau yau thairau, kauṇḍañ‍ñau tibbanik‍kamau|

pahīnajātimaraṇau, brahmacariyassa kaivalī||

680.

 ‘‘aughapāsau daḷhakhilau daḷhau khilau (syā॰ ka॰), pabbatau duppadālayau|

chaitvā khilañ‍ca pāsañ‍ca, sailaṃ bhaitvāna chaitvāna (ka॰) dubbhidaṃ|

tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau jhāyī, muttau sau mārabandhanā||

681.

 ‘‘uddhatau capalau bhikkhu, mittai āgamma pāpakai|

saṃsīdati mahaughasmiṃ, ūmiyā paṭikuj‍jitau||

682.

 ‘‘anuddhatau acapalau, nipakau saṃvutindriyau|

kalyāṇamittau maidhāvī, dukkhassantakarau siyā||

683.

 ‘‘kālapabbaṅgasaṅkāsau, kisau dhamanisanthatau|

mattañ‍ñū  an‍napānasmiṃ, adīnamanasau narau||

684.

 ‘‘phuṭṭhau  ḍaṃsaihi makasaihi, arañ‍ñasmiṃ brahāvanai|

nāgau saṅgāmasīsaiva, satau tatrādhivāsayai||

685.

 ‘‘nābhinandāmi  maraṇaṃ…pai॰… nibbisaṃ bhatakau yathā||

686.

 ‘‘nābhinandāmi maraṇaṃ…pai॰… sampajānau patissatau||

687.

 ‘‘pariciṇṇau mayā satthā…pai॰… bhavanaitti samūhatā||

688.

 ‘‘yassa catthāya pabbajitau, agārasmānagāriyaṃ|

sau mai atthau anuppattau, kiṃ mai saddhivihārinā’’ti||

… añ‍ñāsikauṇḍañ‍ñau añ‍ñākauṇḍañ‍ñau (sī॰ syā॰) thairau…|

2. udāyitthairagāthā

689.

 a॰ ni॰ 6.43 ‘‘manussabhūtaṃ sambuddhaṃ, attadantaṃ samāhitaṃ|

iriyamānaṃ brahmapathai, cittassūpasamai rataṃ||

690.

 ‘‘yaṃ manussā namassanti, sabbadhammāna pāraguṃ|

daivāpi taṃ namassanti, iti mai arahatau sutaṃ||

691.

 ‘‘sabbasaṃyaujanātītaṃ, vanā nibbanamāgataṃ|

kāmaihi naikkhammarataṃ nikkhammarataṃ (ka॰), muttaṃ sailāva kañ‍canaṃ||

692.

 ‘‘sa vai ac‍caruci nāgau, himavāvañ‍ñai siluc‍cayai|

sabbaisaṃ  nāganāmānaṃ, sac‍canāmau anuttarau||

693.

 ‘‘nāgaṃ vau kittayissāmi, na hi āguṃ karauti sau|

saurac‍caṃ avihiṃsā ca, pādā nāgassa tai duvai||

694.

 ‘‘sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca, caraṇā nāgassa taiparai|

saddhāhatthau mahānāgau, upaikkhāsaitadantavā||

695.

 ‘‘sati gīvā sirau pañ‍ñā, vīmaṃsā dhammacintanā|

dhammakucchisamāvāsau, vivaikau tassa vāladhi||

696.

 ‘‘sau jhāyī assāsaratau, ajjhattaṃ susamāhitau|

gacchaṃ samāhitau nāgau, ṭhitau nāgau samāhitau||

697.

 ‘‘sayaṃ samāhitau nāgau, nisin‍naupi samāhitau|

sabbattha saṃvutau nāgau, aisā nāgassa sampadā||

698.

 ‘‘bhuñ‍jati  anavaj‍jāni, sāvaj‍jāni na bhuñ‍jati|

ghāsamacchādanaṃ laddhā, san‍nidhiṃ parivaj‍jayaṃ||

699.

 ‘‘saṃyaujanaṃ  aṇuṃ thūlaṃ, sabbaṃ chaitvāna bandhanaṃ|

yaina yainaiva gacchati, anapakkhauva gacchati||

700.

 ‘‘yathāpi udakai jātaṃ, puṇḍarīkaṃ pavaḍḍhati|

naupalippati tauyaina, sucigandhaṃ manauramaṃ||

701.

 ‘‘tathaiva ca laukai jātau, buddhau laukai viharati|

naupalippati laukaina, tauyaina padumaṃ yathā||

702.

 ‘‘mahāgini paj‍jalitau, anāhāraupasammati|

aṅgāraisu  ca santaisu, nibbutauti pavuc‍cati||

703.

 ‘‘atthassāyaṃ viñ‍ñāpanī, upamā viñ‍ñūhi daisitā|

viñ‍ñissanti mahānāgā, nāgaṃ nāgaina daisitaṃ||

704.

 ‘‘vītarāgau vītadausau, vītamauhau anāsavau|

sarīraṃ vijahaṃ nāgau, parinibbissatyanāsavau’’ti||

… udāyī thairau…|

sauḷasakanipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

kauṇḍañ‍ñau ca udāyī ca, thairā dvai tai mahiddhikā|

sauḷasamhi nipātamhi, gāthāyau dvai ca tiṃsa cāti||


16. vīsatinipātau

1. adhimuttatthairagāthā

705.

 ‘‘yañ‍ñatthaṃ  vā dhanatthaṃ vā, yai hanāma mayaṃ purai|

avasaisaṃ avasai taṃ (sī॰ aṭṭha॰ mūlapāṭhau), avasaisānaṃ (aṭṭha॰?) bhayaṃ hauti, vaidhanti vilapanti ca||

706.

 ‘‘tassa tai natthi bhītattaṃ, bhiyyau vaṇṇau pasīdati|

kasmā na paridaivaisi, aivarūpai mahabbhayai||

707.

 ‘‘natthi caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ, anapaikkhassa gāmaṇi|

atik‍kantā bhayā sabbai, khīṇasaṃyaujanassa vai||

708.

 ‘‘khīṇāya bhavanaittiyā, diṭṭhai dhammai yathātathai|

na  bhayaṃ maraṇai hauti, bhāranikkhaipanai yathā||

709.

 ‘‘suciṇṇaṃ brahmacariyaṃ mai, maggau cāpi subhāvitau|

maraṇai mai bhayaṃ natthi, raugānamiva saṅkhayai||

710.

 ‘‘suciṇṇaṃ brahmacariyaṃ mai, maggau cāpi subhāvitau|

nirassādā bhavā diṭṭhā, visaṃ pitvāva pītvāva (sī॰) chaḍḍitaṃ||

711.

 ‘‘pāragū anupādānau, katakic‍cau anāsavau|

tuṭṭhau āyukkhayā hauti, muttau āghātanā yathā||

712.

 ‘‘uttamaṃ dhammataṃ pattau, sabbalaukai anatthikau|

ādittāva gharā muttau, maraṇasmiṃ na saucati||

713.

 ‘‘yadatthi saṅgataṃ kiñ‍ci, bhavau vā yattha labbhati|

sabbaṃ anissaraṃ aitaṃ, iti vuttaṃ mahaisinā||

714.

 ‘‘yau taṃ tathā pajānāti, yathā buddhaina daisitaṃ|

na gaṇhāti bhavaṃ kiñ‍ci, sutattaṃva ayauguḷaṃ||

715.

 ‘‘na mai hauti ‘ahausi’nti, ‘bhavissa’nti na hauti mai|

saṅkhārā vigamissanti, tattha kā paridaivanā||

716.

 ‘‘suddhaṃ dhammasamuppādaṃ, suddhaṃ saṅkhārasantatiṃ|

passantassa yathābhūtaṃ, na bhayaṃ hauti gāmaṇi||

717.

 ‘‘tiṇakaṭṭhasamaṃ  laukaṃ, yadā pañ‍ñāya passati|

mamattaṃ sau asaṃvindaṃ, ‘natthi mai’ti na saucati||

718.

 ‘‘uk‍kaṇṭhāmi  sarīraina, bhavainamhi anatthikau|

sauyaṃ  bhij‍jissati kāyau, añ‍ñau ca na bhavissati||

719.

 ‘‘yaṃ vau kic‍caṃ sarīraina, taṃ karautha yadicchatha|

na mai tappac‍cayā tattha, dausau paimañ‍ca haihiti’’||

720.

 tassa taṃ vacanaṃ sutvā, abbhutaṃ laumahaṃsanaṃ|

satthāni nikkhipitvāna, māṇavā aitadabravuṃ||

721.

 ‘‘kiṃ bhadantai karitvāna, kau vā ācariyau tava|

kassa sāsanamāgamma, labbhatai taṃ asaukatā’’||

722.

 ‘‘sabbañ‍ñū sabbadassāvī, jinau ācariyau mama|

mahākāruṇikau satthā, sabbalaukatikicchakau||

723.

 ‘‘taināyaṃ daisitau dhammau, khayagāmī anuttarau|

tassa sāsanamāgamma, labbhatai taṃ asaukatā’’||

724.

 sutvāna caurā isinau subhāsitaṃ, nikkhippa satthāni ca āvudhāni ca|

tamhā ca kammā viramiṃsu aikai, aikai ca pabbaj‍jamaraucayiṃsu||

725.

 tai pabbajitvā sugatassa sāsanai, bhāvaitva baujjhaṅgabalāni paṇḍitā|

udaggacittā sumanā katindriyā, phusiṃsu nibbānapadaṃ asaṅkhatanti||

…adhimuttau thairau…|

2. pārāpariyatthairagāthā

726.

 ‘‘samaṇassa  ahu cintā, pārāpariyassa bhikkhunau|

aikakassa nisin‍nassa, pavivittassa jhāyinau||

727.

 ‘‘kimānupubbaṃ purisau, kiṃ vataṃ kiṃ samācāraṃ|

attanau kic‍cakārīssa, na ca kañ‍ci vihaiṭhayai||

728.

 ‘‘indriyāni  manussānaṃ, hitāya ahitāya ca|

arakkhitāni ahitāya, rakkhitāni hitāya ca||

729.

 ‘‘indriyānaiva sārakkhaṃ, indriyāni ca gaupayaṃ|

attanau kic‍cakārīssa, na ca kañ‍ci vihaiṭhayai||

730.

 ‘‘cakkhundriyaṃ  cai rūpaisu, gacchantaṃ anivārayaṃ|

anādīnavadassāvī, sau dukkhā na hi muc‍cati||

731.

 ‘‘sautindriyaṃ cai saddaisu, gacchantaṃ anivārayaṃ|

anādīnavadassāvī, sau dukkhā na hi muc‍cati||

732.

 ‘‘anissaraṇadassāvī, gandhai cai paṭisaivati|

na sau muc‍cati dukkhamhā, gandhaisu adhimucchitau||

733.

 ‘‘ambilaṃ madhuraggañ‍ca, tittakaggamanussaraṃ|

rasataṇhāya gadhitau, hadayaṃ nāvabujjhati||

734.

 ‘‘subhānyappaṭikūlāni, phauṭṭhabbāni anussaraṃ|

rattau rāgādhikaraṇaṃ, vividhaṃ vindatai dukhaṃ||

735.

 ‘‘manaṃ caitaihi dhammaihi, yau na sak‍kauti rakkhituṃ|

tatau  naṃ dukkhamanvaiti, sabbaihaitaihi pañ‍cahi||

736.

 ‘‘pubbalauhitasampuṇṇaṃ, bahussa kuṇapassa ca|

naravīrakataṃ vagguṃ, samuggamiva cittitaṃ||

737.

 ‘‘kaṭukaṃ madhurassādaṃ, piyanibandhanaṃ dukhaṃ|

khuraṃva madhunā littaṃ, ul‍lihaṃ nāvabujjhati||

738.

 ‘‘itthirūpai itthisarai, phauṭṭhabbaipi ca itthiyā|

itthigandhaisu sārattau, vividhaṃ vindatai dukhaṃ||

739.

 ‘‘itthisautāni sabbāni, sandanti pañ‍ca pañ‍casu|

taisamāvaraṇaṃ kātuṃ, yau sak‍kauti vīriyavā||

740.

 ‘‘sau atthavā sau dhammaṭṭhau, sau dakkhau sau vicakkhaṇau|

karaiyya ramamānaupi, kic‍caṃ dhammatthasaṃhitaṃ||

741.

 ‘‘athau  sīdati sañ‍ñuttaṃ, vaj‍jai kic‍caṃ niratthakaṃ|

‘na taṃ kic‍ca’nti mañ‍ñitvā, appamattau vicakkhaṇau||

742.

 ‘‘yañ‍ca atthaina sañ‍ñuttaṃ, yā ca dhammagatā rati|

taṃ samādāya vattaitha, sā hi vai uttamā rati||

743.

 ‘‘uc‍cāvacaihupāyaihi, paraisamabhijigīsati|

hantvā vadhitvā atha saucayitvā, ālaupati sāhasā yau paraisaṃ||

744.

 ‘‘tacchantau āṇiyā āṇiṃ, nihanti balavā yathā|

indriyānindriyaihaiva, nihanti kusalau tathā||

745.

 ‘‘saddhaṃ  vīriyaṃ samādhiñ‍ca, satipañ‍ñañ‍ca bhāvayaṃ|

pañ‍ca pañ‍cahi hantvāna, anīghau yāti brāhmaṇau||

746.

 ‘‘sau atthavā sau dhammaṭṭhau, katvā vākyānusāsaniṃ|

sabbaina sabbaṃ buddhassa, sau narau sukhamaidhatī’’ti||

…pārāpariyau thairau…|

3. tailakānitthairagāthā

747.

 ‘‘cirarattaṃ vatātāpī, dhammaṃ anuvicintayaṃ|

samaṃ cittassa nālatthaṃ, pucchaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇai||

748.

 ‘‘‘kau  sau pāraṅgatau laukai, kau pattau amataugadhaṃ|

kassa dhammaṃ paṭicchāmi, paramatthavijānanaṃ’||

749.

 ‘‘antauvaṅkagatau āsi, macchauva ghasamāmisaṃ|

baddhau mahindapāsaina, vaipacityasurau yathā||

750.

 ‘‘añchāmi naṃ na muñ‍cāmi, asmā saukapariddavā|

kau mai bandhaṃ muñ‍caṃ laukai, sambaudhiṃ vaidayissati||

751.

 ‘‘samaṇaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ vā kaṃ, ādisantaṃ pabhaṅgunaṃ||

kassa dhammaṃ paṭicchāmi, jarāmac‍cupavāhanaṃ||

752.

 ‘‘vicikicchākaṅkhāganthitaṃ, sārambhabalasañ‍ñutaṃ|

kaudhappattamanatthaddhaṃ, abhijappappadāraṇaṃ||

753.

 ‘‘taṇhādhanusamuṭṭhānaṃ, dvai ca pan‍narasāyutaṃ dvaidhāpan‍narasāyutaṃ (?)|

passa  aurasikaṃ bāḷhaṃ, bhaitvāna yadi yada (sī॰ aṭṭha॰) hadi (?) ‘‘hadayai’’ti taṃsaṃvaṇṇanā tiṭṭhati||

754.

 ‘‘anudiṭṭhīnaṃ appahānaṃ, saṅkappaparataijitaṃ|

taina viddhau pavaidhāmi, pattaṃva mālutairitaṃ||

755.

 ‘‘ajjhattaṃ mai samuṭṭhāya, khippaṃ pac‍cati māmakaṃ|

chaphassāyatanī kāyau, yattha sarati sabbadā||

756.

 ‘‘taṃ na passāmi taikicchaṃ, yau maitaṃ sal‍lamuddharai|

nānāraj‍jaina satthaina nāraggaina na satthaina (?), nāñ‍ñaina vicikicchitaṃ||

757.

 ‘‘kau mai asatthau avaṇau, sal‍lamabbhantarapassayaṃ|

ahiṃsaṃ sabbagattāni, sal‍laṃ mai uddharissati||

758.

 ‘‘dhammappati  hi sau saiṭṭhau, visadausappavāhakau|

gambhīrai patitassa mai, thalaṃ pāṇiñ‍ca dassayai||

759.

 ‘‘rahadaihamasmi augāḷhau, ahāriyarajamattikai|

māyāusūyasārambha, thinamiddhamapatthaṭai||

760.

 ‘‘uddhac‍camaighathanitaṃ, saṃyaujanavalāhakaṃ|

vāhā vahanti kuddiṭṭhiṃ duddiṭṭhiṃ (sī॰ dha॰ pa॰ 339), saṅkappā rāganissitā||

761.

 ‘‘savanti sabbadhi sautā, latā ubbhij‍ja tiṭṭhati|

tai sautai kau nivāraiyya, taṃ lataṃ kau hi chaicchati||

762.

 ‘‘vailaṃ  karautha bhaddantai, sautānaṃ san‍nivāraṇaṃ|

mā tai manaumayau sautau, rukkhaṃva sahasā luvai||

763.

 ‘‘aivaṃ  mai bhayajātassa, apārā pāramaisatau|

tāṇau pañ‍ñāvudhau satthā, isisaṅghanisaivitau||

764.

 ‘‘saupāṇaṃ sugataṃ suddhaṃ, dhammasāramayaṃ daḷhaṃ|

pādāsi vuyhamānassa, ‘mā bhāyī’ti ca mabravi||

765.

 ‘‘satipaṭṭhānapāsādaṃ, āruyha pac‍cavaikkhisaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ pubbai amañ‍ñissaṃ, sak‍kāyābhirataṃ pajaṃ||

766.

 ‘‘yadā  ca maggamaddakkhiṃ, nāvāya abhirūhanaṃ|

anadhiṭṭhāya attānaṃ, titthamaddakkhimuttamaṃ||

767.

 ‘‘sal‍laṃ attasamuṭṭhānaṃ, bhavanaittippabhāvitaṃ|

aitaisaṃ appavattāya appavattiyā (?), daisaisi maggamuttamaṃ||

768.

 ‘‘dīgharattānusayitaṃ, cirarattamadhiṭṭhitaṃ|

buddhau maipānudī ganthaṃ, visadausappavāhanau’’ti||

…tailakāni thairau…|

4. raṭṭhapālatthairagāthā

769.

 ma॰ ni॰ 2.302 ‘‘passa cittakataṃ bimbaṃ, arukāyaṃ samussitaṃ|

āturaṃ bahusaṅkappaṃ, yassa natthi dhuvaṃ ṭhiti||

770.

 ‘‘passa cittakataṃ rūpaṃ, maṇinā kuṇḍalaina ca|

aṭṭhiṃ tacaina aunaddhaṃ, saha vatthaihi saubhati||

771.

 ‘‘alattakakatā  pādā, mukhaṃ cuṇṇakamakkhitaṃ|

alaṃ bālassa mauhāya, nau ca pāragavaisinau||

772.

 ‘‘aṭṭhapadakatā  kaisā, naittā añ‍janamakkhitā|

alaṃ bālassa mauhāya, nau ca pāragavaisinau||

773.

 ‘‘añ‍janīva navā cittā, pūtikāyau alaṅkatau|

alaṃ bālassa mauhāya, nau ca pāragavaisinau||

774.

 ‘‘audahi migavau pāsaṃ, nāsadā vāguraṃ migau|

bhutvā nivāpaṃ gacchāma, kandantai migabandhakai||

775.

 ‘‘chin‍nau pāsau migavassa, nāsadā vāguraṃ migau|

bhutvā nivāpaṃ gacchāma, saucantai migaluddakai||

776.

 ‘‘passāmi laukai sadhanai manussai, laddhāna vittaṃ na dadanti mauhā|

luddhā  dhanaṃ san‍nicayaṃ karaunti, bhiyyauva kāmai abhipatthayanti||

777.

 ‘‘rājā  pasayhappathaviṃ vijaitvā, sasāgarantaṃ mahimāvasantau|

auraṃ samuddassa atittarūpau, pāraṃ samuddassapi patthayaitha||

778.

 ‘‘rājā ca añ‍ñai ca bahū manussā, avītataṇhā maraṇaṃ upainti|

ūnāva hutvāna jahanti daihaṃ, kāmaihi laukamhi na hatthi titti||

779.

 ‘‘kandanti  naṃ ñātī pakiriya kaisai, ahau vatā nau amarāti cāhu|

vatthaina naṃ pārutaṃ nīharitvā, citaṃ samaudhāya tatau ḍahanti||

780.

 ‘‘sau ḍayhati sūlaihi tuj‍jamānau, aikaina vatthaina aitaina gatthaina (ka॰) pahāya bhaugai|

na mīyamānassa bhavanti tāṇā, ñātī ca mittā atha vā sahāyā||

781.

 ‘‘dāyādakā tassa dhanaṃ haranti, sattau pana gacchati yaina kammaṃ|

na mīyamānaṃ dhanamanvaiti manviti (ka॰) kiñ‍ci, puttā ca dārā ca dhanañ‍ca raṭṭhaṃ||

782.

 ‘‘na dīghamāyuṃ labhatai dhanaina, na cāpi vittaina jaraṃ vihanti|

appappaṃ hidaṃ jīvitamāhu dhīrā, asassataṃ vippariṇāmadhammaṃ||

783.

 ‘‘aḍḍhā daliddā ca phusanti phassaṃ, bālau ca dhīrau ca tathaiva phuṭṭhau|

bālau hi bālyā vadhitauva saiti, dhīrau  ca nau vaidhati phassaphuṭṭhau||

784.

 ‘‘tasmā  hi pañ‍ñāva dhanaina saiyyā, yāya vausānamidhādhigacchati|

abyausitattā hi bhavābhavaisu, pāpāni kammāni karauti mauhā||

785.

 ‘‘upaiti  gabbhañ‍ca parañ‍ca laukaṃ, saṃsāramāpaj‍ja paramparāya|

tassappapañ‍ñau abhisaddahantau, upaiti gabbhañ‍ca parañ‍ca laukaṃ||

786.

 ‘‘caurau  yathā sandhimukhai gahītau, sakammunā hañ‍ñati pāpadhammau|

aivaṃ pajā paic‍ca paramhi laukai, sakammunā hañ‍ñati pāpadhammau||

787.

 ‘‘kāmā hi citrā madhurā manauramā, virūparūpaina mathainti cittaṃ|

ādīnavaṃ kāmaguṇaisu disvā, tasmā ahaṃ pabbajitaumhi rāja||

788.

 ‘‘dumapphalānīva patanti māṇavā, daharā ca vuḍḍhā ca sarīrabhaidā|

aitampi  disvā pabbajitaumhi rāja, apaṇṇakaṃ sāmañ‍ñamaiva saiyyau||

789.

 ‘‘saddhāyāhaṃ pabbajitau, upaitau jinasāsanai|

avajjhā mayhaṃ pabbaj‍jā, anaṇau bhuñ‍jāmi bhaujanaṃ||

790.

 ‘‘kāmai ādittatau disvā, jātarūpāni satthatau|

gabbhavauk‍kantitau dukkhaṃ, nirayaisu mahabbhayaṃ||

791.

 ‘‘aitamādīnavaṃ ñatvā, saṃvaigaṃ alabhiṃ tadā|

sauhaṃ viddhau tadā santau, sampattau āsavakkhayaṃ||

792.

 ‘‘pariciṇṇau mayā satthā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ|

auhitau garukau bhārau, bhavanaitti samūhatā||

793.

 ‘‘yassatthāya pabbajitau, agārasmānagāriyaṃ|

sau mai atthau anuppattau, sabbasaṃyaujanakkhayau’’ti||

… raṭṭhapālau thairau…|

5. mālukyaputtatthairagāthā

794.

 saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.95 ‘‘rūpaṃ  disvā sati muṭṭhā, piyaṃ nimittaṃ manasi karautau|

sārattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca ajjhaussa tiṭṭhati||

795.

 ‘‘tassa  vaḍḍhanti vaidanā, anaikā rūpasambhavā|

abhijjhā ca vihaisā ca, cittamassūpahañ‍ñati|

aivamācinatau dukkhaṃ, ārā nibbāna nibbānaṃ (sī॰) vuc‍cati||

796.

 ‘‘saddaṃ sutvā sati muṭṭhā, piyaṃ nimittaṃ manasi karautau|

sārattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca ajjhaussa tiṭṭhati||

797.

 ‘‘tassa vaḍḍhanti vaidanā, anaikā saddasambhavā|

abhijjhā ca vihaisā ca, cittamassūpahañ‍ñati|

aivamācinatau dukkhaṃ, ārā nibbāna vuc‍cati||

798.

 ‘‘gandhaṃ ghatvā sati muṭṭhā, piyaṃ nimittaṃ manasi karautau|

sārattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca ajjhaussa tiṭṭhati||

799.

 ‘‘tassa  vaḍḍhanti vaidanā, anaikā gandhasambhavā|

abhijjhā ca vihaisā ca, cittamassūpahañ‍ñati|

aivamācinatau dukkhaṃ, ārā nibbāna vuc‍cati||

800.

 ‘‘rasaṃ bhautvā sati muṭṭhā, piyaṃ nimittaṃ manasi karautau|

sārattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca ajjhaussa tiṭṭhati||

801.

 ‘‘tassa  vaḍḍhanti vaidanā, anaikā rasasambhavā|

abhijjhā ca vihaisā ca, cittamassūpahañ‍ñati|

aivamācinatau dukkhaṃ, ārā nibbāna vuc‍cati||

802.

 ‘‘phassaṃ  phussa sati muṭṭhā, piyaṃ nimittaṃ manasi karautau|

sārattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca ajjhaussa tiṭṭhati||

803.

 ‘‘tassa vaḍḍhanti vaidanā, anaikā phassasambhavā|

abhijjhā ca vihaisā ca, cittamassūpahañ‍ñati|

aivamācinatau dukkhaṃ, ārā nibbāna vuc‍cati||

804.

 ‘‘dhammaṃ ñatvā sati muṭṭhā, piyaṃ nimittaṃ manasi karautau|

sārattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca ajjhaussa tiṭṭhati||

805.

 ‘‘tassa  vaḍḍhanti vaidanā, anaikā dhammasambhavā|

abhijjhā ca vihaisā ca, cittamassūpahañ‍ñati|

aivamācinatau dukkhaṃ, ārā nibbāna vuc‍cati||

806.

 ‘‘na sau raj‍jati rūpaisu, rūpaṃ disvā patissatau|

virattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca nājjhaussa tiṭṭhati||

807.

 ‘‘yathāssa passatau rūpaṃ, saivatau cāpi vaidanaṃ|

khīyati naupacīyati, aivaṃ sau caratī satau|

aivaṃ apacinatau dukkhaṃ, santikai nibbāna vuc‍cati||

808.

 ‘‘na sau raj‍jati saddaisu, saddaṃ sutvā patissatau|

virattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca nājjhaussa tiṭṭhati||

809.

 ‘‘yathāssa suṇatau saddaṃ, saivatau cāpi vaidanaṃ|

khīyati naupacīyati, aivaṃ sau caratī satau|

aivaṃ apacinatau dukkhaṃ, santikai nibbāna vuc‍cati||

810.

 ‘‘na sau raj‍jati gandhaisu, gandhaṃ ghatvā patissatau|

virattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca nājjhaussa tiṭṭhati||

811.

 ‘‘yathāssa  ghāyatau gandhaṃ, saivatau cāpi vaidanaṃ|

khīyati naupacīyati, aivaṃ sau caratī satau|

aivaṃ apacinatau dukkhaṃ, santikai nibbāna vuc‍cati||

812.

 ‘‘na  sau raj‍jati rasaisu, rasaṃ bhautvā patissatau|

virattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca nājjhaussa tiṭṭhati||

813.

 ‘‘yathāssa sāyaratau rasaṃ, saivatau cāpi vaidanaṃ|

khīyati naupacīyati, aivaṃ sau caratī satau|

aivaṃ apacinatau dukkhaṃ, santikai nibbāna vuc‍cati||

814.

 ‘‘na sau raj‍jati phassaisu, phassaṃ phussa patissatau|

virattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca nājjhaussa tiṭṭhati||

815.

 ‘‘yathāssa phusatau phassaṃ, saivatau cāpi vaidanaṃ|

khīyati naupacīyati, aivaṃ sau caratī satau|

aivaṃ apacinatau dukkhaṃ, santikai nibbāna vuc‍cati||

816.

 ‘‘na sau raj‍jati dhammaisu, dhammaṃ ñatvā patissatau|

virattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca nājjhaussa tiṭṭhati||

817.

 ‘‘yathāssa  vijānatau dhammaṃ, saivatau cāpi vaidanaṃ|

khīyati naupacīyati, aivaṃ sau caratī satau|

aivaṃ apacinatau dukkhaṃ, santikai nibbāna vuc‍cati’’||

… mālukyaputtau thairau…|

6. sailatthairagāthā

818.

 ‘‘paripuṇṇakāyau suruci, sujātau cārudassanau|

suvaṇṇavaṇṇausi bhagavā, susuk‍kadāṭhausi vīriyavā susuk‍kadāṭhau virīyavā (sī॰)||

819.

 ‘‘narassa hi sujātassa, yai bhavanti viyañ‍janā|

sabbai  tai tava kāyasmiṃ, mahāpurisalakkhaṇā||

820.

 ‘‘pasan‍nanaittau sumukhau, brahā uju patāpavā|

majjhai samaṇasaṅghassa, ādic‍cauva viraucasi||

821.

 ‘‘kalyāṇadassanau bhikkhu, kañ‍canasan‍nibhattacau|

kiṃ tai samaṇabhāvaina, aivaṃ uttamavaṇṇinau||

822.

 ‘‘rājā  arahasi bhavituṃ, cak‍kavattī rathaisabhau|

cāturantau vijitāvī, jambusaṇḍassa jambumaṇḍassa (ka॰) issarau||

823.

 ‘‘khattiyā bhaugī rājānau bhaugā rājānau (sī॰ ka॰), bhaujarājānau (syā॰), anuyantā bhavanti tai|

rājābhirājā rājādhirājā (sī॰ ka॰) manujindau, raj‍jaṃ kāraihi gautama’’||

824.

 ‘‘rājāhamasmi  saila, (sailāti bhagavā) dhammarājā anuttarau|

dhammaina cak‍kaṃ vattaimi, cak‍kaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ’’||

825.

 ‘‘sambuddhau paṭijānāsi, (iti sailau brāhmaṇau) dhammarājā anuttarau|

‘dhammaina cak‍kaṃ vattaimi’, iti bhāsatha gautama||

826.

 ‘‘kau nu saināpati bhautau, sāvakau satthuranvayau anvayau (sī॰)|

kau taitamanuvattaiti, dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ’’||

827.

 ‘‘mayā  pavattitaṃ cak‍kaṃ, (sailāti bhagavā) dhammacak‍kaṃ anuttaraṃ|

sāriputtau anuvattaiti, anujātau tathāgataṃ||

828.

 ‘‘abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhiñ‍ñātaṃ, bhāvaitabbañ‍ca bhāvitaṃ|

pahātabbaṃ pahīnaṃ mai, tasmā buddhausmi brāhmaṇa||

829.

 ‘‘vinayassu  mayi kaṅkhaṃ, adhimuñ‍cassu brāhmaṇa|

dul‍labhaṃ dassanaṃ hauti, sambuddhānaṃ abhiṇhasau||

830.

 ‘‘yaisaṃ vai dul‍labhau laukai, pātubhāvau abhiṇhasau|

sauhaṃ brāhmaṇa buddhausmi, sal‍lakattau sal‍lakantau (sī॰) anuttarau||

831.

 ‘‘brahmabhūtau atitulau, mārasainappamaddanau|

sabbāmittai vasai vasī (syā॰ ka॰, ma॰ ni॰ 2.399; su॰ ni॰ 966) katvā, maudāmi akutaubhayau’’||

832.

 ‘‘idaṃ bhauntau nisāmaitha, yathā bhāsati cakkhumā|

sal‍lakattau mahāvīrau, sīhauva nadatī vanai||

833.

 ‘‘brahmabhūtaṃ atitulaṃ, mārasainappamaddanaṃ|

kau disvā nappasīdaiyya, api kaṇhābhijātikau||

834.

 ‘‘yau maṃ icchati anvaitu, yau vā nicchati gacchatu|

idhāhaṃ pabbajissāmi, varapañ‍ñassa santikai’’||

835.

 ‘‘aitaṃ cai ruc‍cati bhautau, sammāsambuddhasāsanaṃ|

mayampi pabbajissāma, varapañ‍ñassa santikai||

836.

 ‘‘brāhmaṇā tisatā imai, yācanti pañ‍jalīkatā|

‘brahmacariyaṃ carissāma, bhagavā tava santikai’’’||

837.

 ‘‘svākhātaṃ  brahmacariyaṃ, (sailāti bhagavā) sandiṭṭhikamakālikaṃ|

yattha amaughā pabbaj‍jā, appamattassa sikkhatau’’||

838.

 ‘‘yaṃ  taṃ saraṇamāgamha saraṇamāgamma (sabbattha), itau aṭṭhamai aṭṭhami (syā॰ ka॰) cakkhuma|

sattarattaina bhagavā, dantāmha tava sāsanai||

839.

 ‘‘tuvaṃ  buddhau tuvaṃ satthā, tuvaṃ mārābhibhū muni|

tuvaṃ anusayai chaitvā, tiṇṇau tāraisimaṃ pajaṃ||

840.

 ‘‘upadhī tai samatik‍kantā, āsavā tai padālitā|

sīhauva anupādānau, pahīnabhayabhairavau||

841.

 ‘‘bhikkhavau tisatā imai, tiṭṭhanti pañ‍jalīkatā|

pādai vīra pasāraihi, nāgā vandantu satthunau’’ti||

… sailau thairau…|

7. kāḷigaudhāputtabhaddiyatthairagāthā

842.

 ‘‘yātaṃ mai hatthigīvāya, sukhumā vatthā padhāritā|

sālīnaṃ audanau bhuttau, sucimaṃsūpasaicanau||

843.

 ‘‘sauj‍ja bhaddau sātatikau, uñchāpattāgatai ratau|

jhāyati anupādānau, puttau gaudhāya bhaddiyau||

844.

 ‘‘paṃsukūlī sātatikau, uñchāpattāgatai ratau|

jhāyati anupādānau, puttau gaudhāya bhaddiyau||

845.

 ‘‘piṇḍapātī sātatikau…pai॰…||

846.

 ‘‘taicīvarī sātatikau…pai॰…||

847.

 ‘‘sapadānacārī sātatikau…pai॰…||

848.

 ‘‘aikāsanī sātatikau…pai॰…||

849.

 ‘‘pattapiṇḍī sātatikau…pai॰…||

850.

 ‘‘khalupacchābhattī sātatikau…pai॰…||

851.

 ‘‘ārañ‍ñikau sātatikau…pai॰…||

852.

 ‘‘rukkhamūlikau  sātatikau…pai॰…||

853.

 ‘‘abbhaukāsī sātatikau…pai॰…||

854.

 ‘‘sausānikau sātatikau…pai॰…||

855.

 ‘‘yathāsanthatikau sātatikau…pai॰…||

856.

 ‘‘naisaj‍jikau  sātatikau…pai॰…||

857.

 ‘‘appicchau sātatikau…pai॰…||

858.

 ‘‘santuṭṭhau sātatikau…pai॰…||

859.

 ‘‘pavivittau sātatikau…pai॰…||

860.

 ‘‘asaṃsaṭṭhau sātatikau…pai॰…||

861.

 ‘‘āraddhavīriyau sātatikau…pai॰…||

862.

 ‘‘hitvā satapalaṃ kaṃsaṃ, sauvaṇṇaṃ satarājikaṃ|

aggahiṃ mattikāpattaṃ, idaṃ dutiyābhisaicanaṃ||

863.

 ‘‘uc‍cai maṇḍalipākārai, daḷhamaṭṭālakauṭṭhakai|

rakkhitau khaggahatthaihi, uttasaṃ vihariṃ purai||

864.

 ‘‘sauj‍ja bhaddau anutrāsī, pahīnabhayabhairavau|

jhāyati vanamaugayha, puttau gaudhāya bhaddiyau||

865.

 ‘‘sīlakkhandhai patiṭṭhāya, satiṃ pañ‍ñañ‍ca bhāvayaṃ|

pāpuṇiṃ anupubbaina, sabbasaṃyaujanakkhaya’’nti||

… bhaddiyau kāḷigaudhāya puttau thairau…|

8. aṅgulimālatthairagāthā

866.

 ‘‘gacchaṃ  vadaisi samaṇa ‘ṭṭhitaumhi’, mamañ‍ca brūsi ṭhitamaṭṭhitauti|

pucchāmi  taṃ samaṇa aitamatthaṃ, ‘kathaṃ ṭhitau tvaṃ ahamaṭṭhitaumhi’’’||

867.

 ‘‘ṭhitau ahaṃ aṅgulimāla sabbadā, sabbaisu bhūtaisu nidhāya daṇḍaṃ|

tuvañ‍ca pāṇaisu asañ‍ñatausi, tasmā ṭhitauhaṃ tuvamaṭṭhitausi’’||

868.

 ‘‘cirassaṃ vata mai mahitau mahaisī, mahāvanaṃ samaṇau pac‍capādi pac‍cupādi (sabbattha)|

sauhaṃ cajissāmi sahassapāpaṃ, sutvāna gāthaṃ tava dhammayuttaṃ’’||

869.

 ic‍caiva  caurau asimāvudhañ‍ca, saubbhai papātai narakai anvakāsi akiri (ma॰ ni॰ 2.349)|

avandi caurau sugatassa pādai, tatthaiva pabbaj‍jamayāci buddhaṃ||

870.

 buddhau ca khau kāruṇikau mahaisi, yau satthā laukassa sadaivakassa|

‘tamaihi bhikkhū’ti tadā avauca, aisaiva tassa ahu bhikkhubhāvau||

871.

 ‘‘yau  ca pubbai pamaj‍jitvā, pacchā sau nappamaj‍jati|

saumaṃ laukaṃ pabhāsaiti, abbhā muttauva candimā||

872.

 ‘‘yassa  pāpaṃ kataṃ kammaṃ, kusalaina pidhīyati pithīyati (sī॰ syā॰)|

saumaṃ laukaṃ pabhāsaiti, abbhā muttauva candimā||

873.

 ‘‘yau havai daharau bhikkhu, yuñ‍jati buddhasāsanai|

saumaṃ laukaṃ pabhāsaiti, abbhā muttauva candimā||

874.

 disā hi (syā॰ ka॰, ma॰ ni॰ 2.352) ‘‘disāpi mai dhammakathaṃ suṇantu, disāpi mai yuñ‍jantu buddhasāsanai|

disāpi mai tai manujai bhajantu, yai dhammamaivādapayanti santau||

875.

 ‘‘disā hi mai khantivādānaṃ, aviraudhappasaṃsinaṃ|

suṇantu dhammaṃ kālaina, tañ‍ca anuvidhīyantu||

876.

 ‘‘na hi jātu sau mamaṃ hiṃsai, añ‍ñaṃ vā pana kiñ‍canaṃ kañ‍cinaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), kañ‍canaṃ (?)|

pappuyya paramaṃ santiṃ, rakkhaiyya tasathāvarai||

877.

 thairagā॰ 19 ‘‘udakañhi  nayanti naittikā, usukārā namayanti damayanti (ka॰) taijanaṃ|

dāruṃ namayanti damayanti (ka॰) tacchakā, attānaṃ damayanti paṇḍitā||

878.

 ‘‘daṇḍainaikai  damayanti, aṅkusaibhi kasāhi ca|

adaṇḍaina asatthaina, ahaṃ dantaumhi tādinā||

879.

 ‘‘‘ahiṃsakau’ti  mai nāmaṃ, hiṃsakassa purai satau|

aj‍jāhaṃ sac‍canāmaumhi, na naṃ hiṃsāmi kiñ‍canaṃ kañ‍cinaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), kañ‍canaṃ (?)||

880.

 ‘‘caurau ahaṃ purai āsiṃ, aṅgulimālauti vissutau|

vuyhamānau mahaughaina, buddhaṃ saraṇamāgamaṃ||

881.

 ‘‘lauhitapāṇi purai āsiṃ, aṅgulimālauti vissutau|

saraṇagamanaṃ passa, bhavanaitti samūhatā||

882.

 ‘‘tādisaṃ kammaṃ katvāna, bahuṃ duggatigāminaṃ|

phuṭṭhau kammavipākaina, anaṇau bhuñ‍jāmi bhaujanaṃ||

883.

 ‘‘pamādamanuyuñ‍janti, bālā dummaidhinau janā|

appamādañ‍ca maidhāvī, dhanaṃ saiṭṭhaṃva rakkhati||

884.

 ‘‘mā pamādamanuyuñ‍jaitha, mā kāmaratisanthavaṃ sandhavaṃ (ka॰)|

appamattau hi jhāyantau, pappauti paramaṃ sukhaṃ||

885.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ nāpagataṃ, naitaṃ dummantitaṃ mama|

savibhattaisu dhammaisu, yaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ tadupāgamaṃ||

886.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ nāpagataṃ, naitaṃ dummantitaṃ mama|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

887.

 ‘‘arañ‍ñai  rukkhamūlai vā, pabbataisu guhāsu vā|

tattha tatthaiva aṭṭhāsiṃ, ubbiggamanasau tadā||

888.

 ‘‘sukhaṃ sayāmi ṭhāyāmi, sukhaṃ kappaimi jīvitaṃ|

ahatthapāsau mārassa, ahau satthānukampitau||

889.

 ‘‘brahmajac‍cau  purai āsiṃ, udic‍cau ubhatau ahu|

sauj‍ja puttau sugatassa, dhammarājassa satthunau||

890.

 ‘‘vītataṇhau anādānau, guttadvārau susaṃvutau|

aghamūlaṃ vadhitvāna, pattau mai āsavakkhayau||

891.

 ‘‘pariciṇṇau  mayā satthā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ|

auhitau garukau bhārau, bhavanaitti samūhatā’’ti||

… aṅgulimālau thairau…|

9. anuruddhatthairagāthā

892.

 ‘‘pahāya  mātāpitarau, bhaginī ñātibhātarau|

pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai hitvā, anuruddhauva jhāyatu||

893.

 ‘‘samaitau nac‍cagītaihi, sammatāḷappabaudhanau|

na taina suddhimajjhagaṃ suddhamajjhagā (sī॰ ka॰), suddhimajjhagamā (syā॰), mārassa visayai ratau||

894.

 ‘‘aitañ‍ca samatik‍kamma, ratau buddhassa sāsanai|

sabbaughaṃ samatik‍kamma, anuruddhauva jhāyati||

895.

 ‘‘rūpā saddā rasā gandhā, phauṭṭhabbā ca manauramā|

aitai ca samatik‍kamma, anuruddhauva jhāyati||

896.

 ‘‘piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau, aikau adutiyau muni|

aisati paṃsukūlāni, anuruddhau anāsavau||

897.

 ‘‘vicinī aggahī dhauvi, rajayī dhārayī muni|

paṃsukūlāni matimā, anuruddhau anāsavau||

898.

 ‘‘mahicchau  ca asantuṭṭhau, saṃsaṭṭhau yau ca uddhatau|

tassa dhammā imai haunti, pāpakā saṃkilaisikā||

899.

 ‘‘satau ca hauti appicchau, santuṭṭhau avighātavā|

pavivaikaratau vittau, nic‍camāraddhavīriyau||

900.

 ‘‘tassa  dhammā imai haunti, kusalā baudhipakkhikā|

anāsavau ca sau hauti, iti vuttaṃ mahaisinā||

901.

 ‘‘mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, satthā laukai anuttarau|

manaumayaina kāyaina, iddhiyā upasaṅkami||

902.

 ‘‘yadā mai ahu saṅkappau, tatau uttari daisayi|

nippapañ‍caratau buddhau, nippapañ‍camadaisayi||

903.

 ‘‘tassāhaṃ dhammamañ‍ñāya, vihāsiṃ sāsanai ratau|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

904.

 ‘‘pañ‍capañ‍ñāsavassāni, yatau naisaj‍jikau ahaṃ|

pañ‍cavīsativassāni, yatau middhaṃ samūhataṃ||

905.

 dī॰ ni॰ 2.222 ‘‘nāhu assāsapassāsā, ṭhitacittassa tādinau|

anaijau santimārabbha, cakkhumā parinibbutau||

906.

 dī॰ ni॰ 2.222 ‘‘asal‍līnaina cittaina, vaidanaṃ ajjhavāsayi|

paj‍jautassaiva nibbānaṃ, vimaukkhau caitasau ahu||

907.

 ‘‘aitai pacchimakā dāni, muninau phassapañ‍camā|

nāñ‍ñai  dhammā bhavissanti, sambuddhai parinibbutai||

908.

 ‘‘natthi dāni punāvāsau, daivakāyasmi jālini|

vikkhīṇau jātisaṃsārau, natthi dāni punabbhavau||

909.

 ‘‘yassa  muhuttaina sahassadhā, laukau saṃviditau sabrahmakappau|

vasī iddhiguṇai cutūpapātai, kālai passati daivatā sa bhikkhu sabhikkhunau (sī॰ ka॰)||

910.

 ‘‘an‍nabhārau an‍nahārau (sī॰) purai āsiṃ, daliddau ghāsahārakau|

samaṇaṃ paṭipādaisiṃ, upariṭṭhaṃ yasassinaṃ||

911.

 ‘‘saumhi sakyakulai jātau, anuruddhauti maṃ vidū|

upaitau nac‍cagītaihi, sammatāḷappabaudhanau||

912.

 ‘‘athaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ, satthāraṃ akutaubhayaṃ|

tasmiṃ cittaṃ pasādaitvā, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ||

913.

 ‘‘pubbainivāsaṃ jānāmi, yattha mai vusitaṃ purai|

tāvatiṃsaisu daivaisu, aṭṭhāsiṃ sak‍kajātiyā satajātiyā (sī॰)||

914.

 ‘‘sattakkhattuṃ  manussindau, ahaṃ raj‍jamakārayiṃ|

cāturantau vijitāvī, jambusaṇḍassa issarau|

adaṇḍaina asatthaina, dhammaina anusāsayiṃ||

915.

 ‘‘itau satta tatau satta, saṃsārāni catuddasa|

nivāsamabhijānissaṃ, daivalaukai ṭhitā tadā||

916.

 ‘‘pañ‍caṅgikai  samādhimhi, santai aikaudibhāvitai|

paṭippassaddhiladdhamhi, dibbacakkhu visujjhi mai||

917.

 ‘‘cutūpapātaṃ jānāmi, sattānaṃ āgatiṃ gatiṃ|

itthabhāvañ‍ñathābhāvaṃ, jhānai pañ‍caṅgikai ṭhitau||

918.

 ‘‘pariciṇṇau mayā satthā…pai॰… bhavanaitti samūhatā||

919.

 ‘‘vaj‍jīnaṃ vaiḷuvagāmai, ahaṃ jīvitasaṅkhayā|

haiṭṭhatau vaiḷugumbasmiṃ, nibbāyissaṃ anāsavau’’ti||

… anuruddhau thairau…|

10. pārāpariyatthairagāthā

920.

 samaṇassa ahu cintā, pupphitamhi mahāvanai|

aikaggassa nisin‍nassa, pavivittassa jhāyinau||

921.

 ‘‘añ‍ñathā laukanāthamhi, tiṭṭhantai purisuttamai|

iriyaṃ āsi bhikkhūnaṃ, añ‍ñathā dāni dissati||

922.

 ‘‘sītavātaparittānaṃ, hirikaupīnachādanaṃ|

mattaṭṭhiyaṃ abhuñ‍jiṃsu, santuṭṭhā itarītarai||

923.

 ‘‘paṇītaṃ yadi vā lūkhaṃ, appaṃ vā yadi vā bahuṃ|

yāpanatthaṃ abhuñ‍jiṃsu, agiddhā nādhimucchitā||

924.

 ‘‘jīvitānaṃ  parikkhārai, bhaisaj‍jai atha pac‍cayai|

na bāḷhaṃ ussukā āsuṃ, yathā tai āsavakkhayai||

925.

 ‘‘arañ‍ñai rukkhamūlaisu, kandarāsu guhāsu ca|

vivaikamanubrūhantā, vihaṃsu tapparāyanā||

926.

 ‘‘nīcā  niviṭṭhā subharā, mudū atthaddhamānasā|

abyāsaikā amukharā, atthacintā vasānugā||

927.

 ‘‘tatau  pāsādikaṃ āsi, gataṃ bhuttaṃ nisaivitaṃ|

siniddhā tailadhārāva, ahausi iriyāpathau||

928.

 ‘‘sabbāsavaparikkhīṇā, mahājhāyī mahāhitā|

nibbutā dāni tai thairā, parittā dāni tādisā||

929.

 ‘‘kusalānañ‍ca  dhammānaṃ, pañ‍ñāya ca parikkhayā|

sabbākāravarūpaitaṃ, luj‍jatai jinasāsanaṃ||

930.

 ‘‘pāpakānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ, kilaisānañ‍ca yau utu|

upaṭṭhitā vivaikāya, yai ca saddhammasaisakā||

931.

 ‘‘tai kilaisā pavaḍḍhantā, āvisanti bahuṃ janaṃ|

kīḷanti mañ‍ñai bālaihi, ummattaihiva rakkhasā||

932.

 ‘‘kilaisaihābhibhūtā tai, taina taina vidhāvitā|

narā kilaisavatthūsu, sasaṅgāmaiva ghausitai||

933.

 ‘‘paric‍cajitvā saddhammaṃ, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi bhaṇḍarai|

diṭṭhigatāni anvaintā, idaṃ saiyyauti mañ‍ñarai||

934.

 ‘‘dhanañ‍ca puttaṃ bhariyañ‍ca, chaḍḍayitvāna niggatā|

kaṭacchubhikkhahaitūpi, akicchāni nisaivarai||

935.

 ‘‘udarāvadaihakaṃ bhutvā, sayantuttānasaiyyakā|

kathaṃ vattainti kathā vaḍḍhainti (sī॰ ka॰) paṭibuddhā, yā kathā satthugarahitā||

936.

 ‘‘sabbakārukasippāni, cittiṃ katvāna cittīkatvāna (sī॰), cittaṃ katvāna (syā॰) sikkharai|

avūpasantā ajjhattaṃ, sāmañ‍ñatthauti acchati tiriñ‍cati (?)||

937.

 ‘‘mattikaṃ tailacuṇṇañ‍ca, udakāsanabhaujanaṃ|

gihīnaṃ upanāmainti, ākaṅkhantā bahuttaraṃ||

938.

 ‘‘dantapaunaṃ kapitthañ‍ca, pupphaṃ khādaniyāni ca|

piṇḍapātai ca sampan‍nai, ambai āmalakāni ca||

939.

 ‘‘bhaisaj‍jaisu yathā vaij‍jā, kic‍cākic‍cai yathā gihī|

gaṇikāva vibhūsāyaṃ, issarai khattiyā yathā||

940.

 ‘‘naikatikā  vañ‍canikā, kūṭasakkhī apāṭukā|

bahūhi parikappaihi, āmisaṃ paribhuñ‍jarai||

941.

 ‘‘laisakappai pariyāyai, parikappainudhāvitā|

jīvikatthā upāyaina, saṅkaḍḍhanti bahuṃ dhanaṃ||

942.

 ‘‘upaṭṭhāpainti  parisaṃ, kammatau nau ca dhammatau|

dhammaṃ paraisaṃ daisainti, lābhatau nau ca atthatau||

943.

 ‘‘saṅghalābhassa bhaṇḍanti, saṅghatau paribāhirā|

paralābhaupajīvantā, ahirīkā na laj‍jarai||

944.

 ‘‘nānuyuttā tathā aikai, muṇḍā saṅghāṭipārutā|

sambhāvanaṃyaivicchanti, lābhasak‍kāramucchitā||

945.

 ‘‘aivaṃ nānappayātamhi, na dāni sukaraṃ tathā|

aphusitaṃ  vā phusituṃ, phusitaṃ vānurakkhituṃ||

946.

 ‘‘yathā kaṇṭakaṭṭhānamhi, caraiyya anupāhanau|

satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvāna, aivaṃ gāmai munī carai||

947.

 ‘‘saritvā pubbakai yaugī, taisaṃ vattamanussaraṃ|

kiñ‍cāpi pacchimau kālau, phusaiyya amataṃ padaṃ||

948.

 ‘‘idaṃ vatvā sālavanai, samaṇau bhāvitindriyau|

brāhmaṇau parinibbāyī, isi khīṇapunabbhavau’’ti||

… pārāpariyau pārāsariyau (syā॰) thairau…|

vīsatinipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

adhimuttau pārāpariyau, tailakāni raṭṭhapālau|

mālukyasailau bhaddiyau, aṅguli dibbacakkhukau||

pārāpariyau dasaitai, vīsamhi parikittitā|

gāthāyau dvai satā haunti, pañ‍catālīsa 244 gāthāyauyaiva dissanti uttarinti||


17. tiṃsanipātau

1. phussatthairagāthā

949.

 pāsādikai  bahū disvā, bhāvitattai susaṃvutai|

isi paṇḍarasagauttau paṇḍarassa gauttau (sī॰), apucchi phussasavhayaṃ||

950.

 ‘‘kiṃchandā  kimadhippāyā, kimākappā bhavissarai|

anāgatamhi kālamhi, taṃ mai akkhāhi pucchitau’’||

951.

 ‘‘suṇauhi vacanaṃ mayhaṃ, isipaṇḍarasavhaya|

sak‍kac‍caṃ upadhāraihi, ācikkhissāmyanāgataṃ||

952.

 ‘‘kaudhanā upanāhī ca, makkhī thambhī saṭhā bahū|

ussukī nānāvādā ca, bhavissanti anāgatai||

953.

 ‘‘añ‍ñātamāninau dhammai, gambhīrai tīragaucarā|

lahukā agaru dhammai, añ‍ñamañ‍ñamagāravā||

954.

 ‘‘bahū ādīnavā laukai, uppaj‍jissantyanāgatai|

sudaisitaṃ imaṃ dhammaṃ, kilaisaissanti kilaisissanti (sī॰), kilisissanti (syā॰ ka॰) dummatī||

955.

 ‘‘guṇahīnāpi saṅghamhi, vauharantā visāradā|

balavantau bhavissanti, mukharā assutāvinau||

956.

 ‘‘guṇavantaupi saṅghamhi, vauharantā yathātthatau|

dubbalā tai bhavissanti, hirīmanā anatthikā||

957.

 ‘‘rajataṃ jātarūpañ‍ca, khaittaṃ vatthumajaiḷakaṃ|

dāsidāsañ‍ca dummaidhā, sādiyissantyanāgatai||

958.

 ‘‘ujjhānasañ‍ñinau bālā, sīlaisu asamāhitā|

un‍naḷā vicarissanti, kalahābhiratā magā||

959.

 ‘‘uddhatā ca bhavissanti, nīlacīvarapārutā|

kuhā  thaddhā lapā siṅgī, carissantyariyā viya||

960.

 ‘‘tailasaṇṭhaihi kaisaihi, capalā añ‍janakkhikā|

rathiyāya gamissanti, dantavaṇṇikapārutā||

961.

 ‘‘ajaigucchaṃ  vimuttaihi, surattaṃ arahaddhajaṃ|

jigucchissanti kāsāvaṃ, audātaisu samucchitā audātai susamucchitā (sī॰)||

962.

 ‘‘lābhakāmā bhavissanti, kusītā hīnavīriyā|

kicchantā vanapatthāni, gāmantaisu vasissarai||

963.

 ‘‘yai  yai lābhaṃ labhissanti, micchājīvaratā sadā|

tai taiva anusikkhantā, bhajissanti asaṃyatā||

964.

 ‘‘yai yai alābhinau lābhaṃ, na tai puj‍jā bhavissarai|

supaisalaipi tai dhīrai, saivissanti na tai tadā||

965.

 ‘‘milakkhurajanaṃ rattaṃ pilakkharajanaṃ rattaṃ (?), garahantā sakaṃ dhajaṃ|

titthiyānaṃ dhajaṃ kaici, dhārissantyavadātakaṃ||

966.

 ‘‘agāravau ca kāsāvai, tadā taisaṃ bhavissati|

paṭisaṅkhā ca kāsāvai, bhikkhūnaṃ na bhavissati||

967.

 ‘‘abhibhūtassa dukkhaina, sal‍laviddhassa ruppatau|

paṭisaṅkhā mahāghaurā, nāgassāsi acintiyā||

968.

 ‘‘chaddantau hi tadā disvā, surattaṃ arahaddhajaṃ|

tāvadaiva bhaṇī gāthā, gajau atthaupasaṃhitā’’||

969.

 dha॰ pa॰ 9; jā॰ 1.2.141; 1.16.122 ‘‘anik‍kasāvau  kāsāvaṃ, yau vatthaṃ paridhassati paridahissati (sī॰ syā॰)|

apaitau damasac‍caina, na sau kāsāvamarahati||

970.

 ‘‘yau ca vantakāsāvassa, sīlaisu susamāhitau|

upaitau damasac‍caina, sa vai kāsāvamarahati||

971.

 ‘‘vipan‍nasīlau dummaidhau, pākaṭau kāmakāriyau|

vibbhantacittau nissuk‍kau, na sau kāsāvamarahati||

972.

 ‘‘yau ca sīlaina sampan‍nau, vītarāgau samāhitau|

audātamanasaṅkappau, sa vai kāsāvamarahati||

973.

 ‘‘uddhatau un‍naḷau bālau, sīlaṃ yassa na vij‍jati|

audātakaṃ arahati, kāsāvaṃ kiṃ karissati||

974.

 ‘‘bhikkhū  ca bhikkhuniyau ca, duṭṭhacittā anādarā|

tādīnaṃ maittacittānaṃ, niggaṇhissantyanāgatai||

975.

 ‘‘sikkhāpaintāpi thairaihi, bālā cīvaradhāraṇaṃ|

na suṇissanti dummaidhā, pākaṭā kāmakāriyā||

976.

 ‘‘tai  tathā sikkhitā bālā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ agāravā|

nādiyissantupajjhāyai, khaḷuṅkau viya sārathiṃ||

977.

 ‘‘aivaṃ anāgataddhānaṃ, paṭipatti bhavissati|

bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnañ‍ca, pattai kālamhi pacchimai||

978.

 ‘‘purā āgacchatai aitaṃ, anāgataṃ mahabbhayaṃ|

subbacā hautha sakhilā, añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sagāravā||

979.

 ‘‘maittacittā  kāruṇikā, hautha sīlaisu saṃvutā|

āraddhavīriyā pahitattā, nic‍caṃ daḷhaparak‍kamā||

980.

 ‘‘pamādaṃ  bhayatau disvā, appamādañ‍ca khaimatau|

bhāvaithaṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ, phusantā amataṃ pada’’nti||

… phussau thairau…|

2. sāriputtatthairagāthā

981.

 ‘‘yathācārī yathāsatau satīmā, yatasaṅkappajjhāyi appamattau|

ajjhattaratau samāhitattau, aikau santusitau tamāhu bhikkhuṃ||

982.

 ‘‘al‍laṃ sukkhaṃ vā bhuñ‍jantau, na bāḷhaṃ suhitau siyā|

ūnūdarau mitāhārau, satau bhikkhu paribbajai||

983.

 ‘‘cattārau pañ‍ca ālaupai, abhutvā udakaṃ pivai|

alaṃ phāsuvihārāya, pahitattassa bhikkhunau||

984.

 ‘‘kappiyaṃ taṃ cai chādaiti, cīvaraṃ idamatthikaṃ idamatthitaṃ (sī॰)|

alaṃ phāsuvihārāya, pahitattassa bhikkhunau||

985.

 ‘‘pal‍laṅkaina  nisin‍nassa, jaṇṇukai nābhivassati|

alaṃ phāsuvihārāya, pahitattassa bhikkhunau||

986.

 saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.253; itivu॰ 53 ‘‘yau sukhaṃ dukkhatau adda, dukkhamaddakkhi sal‍latau|

ubhayantaraina  ubhayamantarai (sī॰) nāhausi, kaina laukasmi kiṃ siyā||

987.

 ‘‘mā mai kadāci pāpicchau, kusītau hīnavīriyau|

appassutau anādarau, kaina laukasmi kiṃ siyā||

988.

 ‘‘bahussutau  ca maidhāvī, sīlaisu susamāhitau|

caitausamathamanuyuttau, api muddhani tiṭṭhatu||

989.

 ‘‘yau papañ‍camanuyuttau, papañ‍cābhiratau magau|

virādhayī sau nibbānaṃ, yaugakkhaimaṃ anuttaraṃ||

990.

 ‘‘yau ca papañ‍caṃ hitvāna, nippapañ‍capathai ratau|

ārādhayī sau nibbānaṃ, yaugakkhaimaṃ anuttaraṃ||

991.

 dha॰ pa॰ 98 ‘‘gāmai vā yadi vārañ‍ñai, nin‍nai vā yadi vā thalai|

yattha arahantau viharanti, taṃ bhūmirāmaṇaiyyakaṃ||

992.

 ‘‘ramaṇīyāni arañ‍ñāni, yattha na ramatī janau|

vītarāgā ramissanti, na tai kāmagavaisinau||

993.

 dha॰ pa॰ 76 ‘‘nidhīnaṃva pavattāraṃ, yaṃ passai vaj‍jadassinaṃ|

niggayhavādiṃ  maidhāviṃ, tādisaṃ paṇḍitaṃ bhajai|

tādisaṃ bhajamānassa, saiyyau hauti na pāpiyau||

994.

 dha॰ pa॰ 77 ‘‘auvadaiyyānusāsaiyya, asabbhā ca nivārayai|

satañhi sau piyau hauti, asataṃ hauti appiyau||

995.

 ‘‘añ‍ñassa bhagavā buddhau, dhammaṃ daisaisi cakkhumā|

dhammai  daisiyamānamhi, sautamaudhaisimatthikau|

taṃ mai amaughaṃ savanaṃ, vimuttaumhi anāsavau||

996.

 ‘‘naiva pubbainivāsāya, napi dibbassa cakkhunau|

caitaupariyāya iddhiyā, cutiyā upapattiyā|

sautadhātuvisuddhiyā, paṇidhī mai na vij‍jati kathā॰ 378||

997.

 ‘‘rukkhamūlaṃva  nissāya, muṇḍau saṅghāṭipārutau|

pañ‍ñāya uttamau thairau, upatissauva upatissau ca (sī॰ ka॰) jhāyati||

998.

 ‘‘avitak‍kaṃ samāpan‍nau, sammāsambuddhasāvakau|

ariyaina tuṇhībhāvaina, upaitau hauti tāvadai||

999.

 udā॰ 24 ‘‘yathāpi pabbatau sailau, acalau suppatiṭṭhitau|

aivaṃ mauhakkhayā bhikkhu, pabbatauva na vaidhati||

1000.

 ‘‘anaṅgaṇassa pausassa, nic‍caṃ sucigavaisinau|

vālaggamattaṃ pāpassa, abbhamattaṃva khāyati||

1001.

 ‘‘nābhinandāmi  maraṇaṃ, nābhinandāmi jīvitaṃ|

nikkhipissaṃ imaṃ kāyaṃ, sampajānau patissatau||

1002.

 ‘‘nābhinandāmi maraṇaṃ, nābhinandāmi jīvitaṃ|

kālañ‍ca paṭikaṅkhāmi, nibbisaṃ bhatakau yathā||

1003.

 ‘‘ubhayaina midaṃ maraṇamaiva, nāmaraṇaṃ pacchā vā purai vā|

paṭipaj‍jatha mā vinassatha, khaṇau vau mā upac‍cagā||

1004.

 ‘‘nagaraṃ yathā pac‍cantaṃ, guttaṃ santarabāhiraṃ|

aivaṃ gaupaitha attānaṃ, khaṇau vau mā upac‍cagā|

khaṇātītā  hi saucanti, nirayamhi samappitā||

1005.

 ‘‘upasantau uparatau, mantabhāṇī mattabhāṇī (sī॰) anuddhatau|

dhunāti pāpakai dhammai, dumapattaṃva mālutau||

1006.

 ‘‘upasantau uparatau, mantabhāṇī anuddhatau|

appāsi abbahi (syā॰), abhāsi (?) pāpakai dhammai, dumapattaṃva mālutau||

1007.

 ‘‘upasantau  anāyāsau, vippasan‍nau anāvilau|

kalyāṇasīlau maidhāvī, dukkhassantakarau siyā||

1008.

 ‘‘na vissasai aikatiyaisu aivaṃ, agārisu pabbajitaisu cāpi|

sādhūpi hutvā na asādhu haunti, asādhu hutvā puna sādhu haunti||

1009.

 ‘‘kāmacchandau  ca byāpādau, thinamiddhañ‍ca bhikkhunau|

uddhac‍caṃ vicikicchā ca, pañ‍caitai cittakailisā||

1010.

 ‘‘yassa sak‍kariyamānassa, asak‍kāraina cūbhayaṃ|

samādhi na vikampati, appamādavihārinau||

1011.

 ‘‘taṃ jhāyinaṃ sātatikaṃ, sukhumadiṭṭhivipassakaṃ|

upādānakkhayārāmaṃ, āhu sappurisau iti||

1012.

 ‘‘mahāsamuddau pathavī, pabbatau anilaupi ca|

upamāya na yuj‍janti, satthu varavimuttiyā||

1013.

 ‘‘cak‍kānuvattakau  thairau, mahāñāṇī samāhitau|

pathavāpaggisamānau, na raj‍jati na dussati||

1014.

 ‘‘pañ‍ñāpāramitaṃ pattau, mahābuddhi mahāmati|

ajaḷau jaḷasamānau, sadā carati nibbutau||

1015.

 ‘‘pariciṇṇau mayā satthā…pai॰… bhavanaitti samūhatā||

1016.

 ‘‘sampādaithappamādaina, aisā mai anusāsanī|

handāhaṃ parinibbissaṃ, vippamuttaumhi sabbadhī’’ti||

… sāriputtau thairau…|

3. ānandatthairagāthā

1017.

 ‘‘pisuṇaina ca kaudhanaina ca, maccharinā ca vibhūtanandinā|

sakhitaṃ na karaiyya paṇḍitau, pāpau kāpurisaina saṅgamau||

1018.

 ‘‘saddhaina ca paisalaina ca, pañ‍ñavatā bahussutaina ca|

sakhitaṃ karaiyya paṇḍitau, bhaddau sappurisaina saṅgamau||

1019.

 ‘‘passa cittakataṃ bimbaṃ…pai॰… yassa natthi dhuvaṃ ṭhiti||

1020.

 ‘‘passa cittakataṃ bimbaṃ…pai॰… vatthaihi saubhati||

1021.

 ‘‘alattakakatā  …pai॰… nau ca pāragavaisinau||

1022.

 ‘‘aṭṭhapadakatā…pai॰… nau ca pāragavaisinau||

1023.

 ‘‘añ‍janīva navā…pai॰… nau ca pāragavaisinau||

1024.

 ‘‘bahussutau cittakathī, buddhassa paricārakau|

pan‍nabhārau visañ‍ñuttau, saiyyaṃ kappaiti gautamau||

1025.

 ‘‘khīṇāsavau visañ‍ñuttau, saṅgātītau sunibbutau|

dhāraiti antimaṃ daihaṃ, jātimaraṇapāragū||

1026.

 ‘‘yasmiṃ  patiṭṭhitā dhammā, buddhassādic‍cabandhunau|

nibbānagamanai maggai, sauyaṃ tiṭṭhati gautamau||

1027.

 ‘‘dvāsīti  buddhatau gaṇhiṃ, dvai sahassāni bhikkhutau|

caturāsītisahassāni, yai mai dhammā pavattinau||

1028.

 ‘‘appassutāyaṃ purisau, balibaddauva jīrati|

maṃsāni tassa vaḍḍhanti, pañ‍ñā tassa na vaḍḍhati||

1029.

 ‘‘bahussutau appassutaṃ, yau sutainātimañ‍ñati|

andhau padīpadhārauva, tathaiva paṭibhāti maṃ||

1030.

 ‘‘bahussutaṃ upāsaiyya, sutañ‍ca na vināsayai|

taṃ mūlaṃ brahmacariyassa, tasmā dhammadharau siyā||

1031.

 ‘‘pubbāparañ‍ñū atthañ‍ñū, niruttipadakauvidau|

suggahītañ‍ca gaṇhāti, atthañ‍caupaparikkhati||

1032.

 ‘‘khantyā  chandikatau khantiyā chanditau (?) hauti, ussahitvā tulaiti taṃ|

samayai sau padahati, ajjhattaṃ susamāhitau||

1033.

 ‘‘bahussutaṃ dhammadharaṃ, sappañ‍ñaṃ buddhasāvakaṃ|

dhammaviñ‍ñāṇamākaṅkhaṃ, taṃ bhajaitha tathāvidhaṃ||

1034.

 ‘‘bahussutau dhammadharau, kausārakkhau mahaisinau|

cakkhu sabbassa laukassa, pūjanīyau bahussutau||

1035.

 ‘‘dhammārāmau dhammaratau, dhammaṃ anuvicintayaṃ|

dhammaṃ anussaraṃ bhikkhu, saddhammā na parihāyati||

1036.

 ‘‘kāyamacchairagarunau  garukau (sī॰), hiyyamānai hiyyamānau (sī॰) anuṭṭhahai|

sarīrasukhagiddhassa, kutau samaṇaphāsutā||

1037.

 ‘‘na  pakkhanti disā sabbā, dhammā na paṭibhanti maṃ|

gatai kalyāṇamittamhi, andhakāraṃva khāyati||

1038.

 ‘‘abbhatītasahāyassa, atītagatasatthunau|

natthi aitādisaṃ mittaṃ, yathā kāyagatā sati||

1039.

 ‘‘yai purāṇā atītā tai, navaihi na samaiti mai|

svaj‍ja aikauva jhāyāmi, vassupaitauva pakkhimā||

1040.

 ‘‘dassanāya abhik‍kantai, nānāvairaj‍jakai bahū|

mā vārayittha sautārau, passantu samayau mamaṃ||

1041.

 ‘‘dassanāya  abhik‍kantai, nānāvairaj‍jakai puthu|

karauti satthā aukāsaṃ, na nivāraiti cakkhumā||

1042.

 ‘‘paṇṇavīsativassāni, saikhabhūtassa mai satau|

na kāmasañ‍ñā uppaj‍ji, passa dhammasudhammataṃ||

1043.

 ‘‘paṇṇavīsativassāni, saikhabhūtassa mai satau|

na dausasañ‍ñā uppaj‍ji, passa dhammasudhammataṃ||

1044.

 ‘‘paṇṇavīsativassāni, bhagavantaṃ upaṭṭhahiṃ|

maittaina kāyakammaina, chāyāva anapāyinī anupāyinī (syā॰ ka॰)||

1045.

 ‘‘paṇṇavīsativassāni, bhagavantaṃ upaṭṭhahiṃ|

maittaina vacīkammaina, chāyāva anapāyinī||

1046.

 ‘‘paṇṇavīsativassāni, bhagavantaṃ upaṭṭhahiṃ|

maittaina manaukammaina, chāyāva anapāyinī||

1047.

 ‘‘buddhassa  caṅkamantassa, piṭṭhitau anucaṅkamiṃ|

dhammai daisiyamānamhi, ñāṇaṃ mai udapaj‍jatha||

1048.

 ‘‘ahaṃ sakaraṇīyaumhi, saikhau appattamānasau|

satthu ca parinibbānaṃ, yau amhaṃ anukampakau||

1049.

 ‘‘tadāsi  yaṃ bhiṃsanakaṃ, tadāsi laumahaṃsanaṃ|

sabbākāravarūpaitai, sambuddhai parinibbutai||

1050.

 ‘‘bahussutau dhammadharau, kausārakkhau mahaisinau|

cakkhu sabbassa laukassa, ānandau parinibbutau||

1051.

 ‘‘bahussutau dhammadharau, kausārakkhau mahaisinau|

cakkhu sabbassa laukassa, andhakārai tamaunudau||

1052.

 ‘‘gatimantau satimantau, dhitimantau ca yau isi|

saddhammadhārakau thairau, ānandau ratanākarau||

1053.

 ‘‘pariciṇṇau mayā satthā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ|

auhitau garukau bhārau, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti||

… ānandau thairau…|

tiṃsanipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

phussaupatissau ānandau, tayautimai pakittitā|

gāthāyau tattha saṅkhātā, sataṃ pañ‍ca ca uttarīti|


18. cattālīsanipātau

1. mahākassapatthairagāthā

1054.

 ‘‘na  gaṇaina purakkhatau carai, vimanau hauti samādhi dul‍labhau|

nānājanasaṅgahau dukhau, iti disvāna gaṇaṃ na raucayai||

1055.

 ‘‘na kulāni upabbajai muni, vimanau hauti samādhi dul‍labhau|

sau ussuk‍kau rasānugiddhau, atthaṃ riñ‍cati yau sukhāvahau||

1056.

 ‘‘paṅkauti hi naṃ avaidayuṃ, yāyaṃ vandanapūjanā kulaisu|

sukhumaṃ sal‍la durubbahaṃ, sak‍kārau kāpurisaina duj‍jahau||

1057.

 ‘‘saināsanamhā auruyha, nagaraṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ|

bhuñ‍jantaṃ purisaṃ kuṭṭhiṃ, sak‍kac‍caṃ taṃ upaṭṭhahiṃ||

1058.

 ‘‘sau mai taṃ (sī॰ ka॰) pak‍kaina hatthaina, ālaupaṃ upanāmayi|

ālaupaṃ pakkhipantassa, aṅguli caittha paittha (sī॰ ka॰) chij‍jatha||

1059.

 ‘‘kuṭṭamūlañ‍ca kuḍḍamūlañ‍ca (sī॰ syā॰) nissāya, ālaupaṃ taṃ abhuñ‍jisaṃ|

bhuñ‍jamānai vā bhuttai vā, jaigucchaṃ mai na vij‍jati||

1060.

 ‘‘uttiṭṭhapiṇḍau āhārau, pūtimuttañ‍ca ausadhaṃ|

saināsanaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ, paṃsukūlañ‍ca cīvaraṃ|

yassaitai abhisambhutvā abhibhuñ‍jati (?), sa vai cātuddisau narau||

1061.

 ‘‘yattha aikai vihañ‍ñanti, āruhantā siluc‍cayaṃ|

tassa buddhassa dāyādau, sampajānau patissatau|

iddhibalainupatthaddhau, kassapau abhirūhati||

1062.

 ‘‘piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau, sailamāruyha kassapau|

jhāyati anupādānau, pahīnabhayabhairavau||

1063.

 ‘‘piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau, sailamāruyha kassapau|

jhāyati anupādānau, ḍayhamānaisu nibbutau||

1064.

 ‘‘piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau, sailamāruyha kassapau|

jhāyati anupādānau, katakic‍cau anāsavau||

1065.

 ‘‘karairimālāvitatā, bhūmibhāgā manauramā|

kuñ‍jarābhirudā rammā, tai sailā ramayanti maṃ||

1066.

 ‘‘nīlabbhavaṇṇā rucirā, vārisītā sucindharā|

indagaupakasañchan‍nā, tai sailā ramayanti maṃ||

1067.

 ‘‘nīlabbhakūṭasadisā, kūṭāgāravarūpamā|

vāraṇābhirudā rammā, tai sailā ramayanti maṃ||

1068.

 ‘‘abhivuṭṭhā rammatalā, nagā isibhi saivitā|

abbhun‍naditā sikhīhi, tai sailā ramayanti maṃ||

1069.

 ‘‘alaṃ jhāyitukāmassa, pahitattassa mai satau|

alaṃ mai atthakāmassa attakāmassa (?), pahitattassa bhikkhunau||

1070.

 ‘‘alaṃ mai phāsukāmassa, pahitattassa bhikkhunau|

alaṃ mai yaugakāmassa, pahitattassa tādinau||

1071.

 ‘‘umāpupphaina samānā, gaganāvabbhachāditā|

nānādijagaṇākiṇṇā, tai sailā ramayanti maṃ||

1072.

 ‘‘anākiṇṇā gahaṭṭhaihi, migasaṅghanisaivitā|

nānādijagaṇākiṇṇā, tai sailā ramayanti maṃ||

1073.

 ‘‘acchaudikā puthusilā, gaunaṅgulamigāyutā|

ambusaivālasañchan‍nā, tai sailā ramayanti maṃ||

1074.

 ‘‘na pañ‍caṅgikaina turiyaina, rati mai hauti tādisī|

yathā aikaggacittassa, sammā dhammaṃ vipassatau||

1075.

 ‘‘kammaṃ  bahukaṃ na kārayai, parivaj‍jaiyya janaṃ na uyyamai|

ussuk‍kau sau rasānugiddhau, atthaṃ riñ‍cati yau sukhāvahau||

1076.

 ‘‘kammaṃ bahukaṃ na kārayai, parivaj‍jaiyya anattanaiyyamaitaṃ|

kicchati kāyau kilamati, dukkhitau sau samathaṃ na vindati||

1077.

 ‘‘auṭṭhappahatamattaina, attānampi na passati|

patthaddhagīvau carati, ahaṃ saiyyauti mañ‍ñati||

1078.

 ‘‘asaiyyau saiyyasamānaṃ, bālau mañ‍ñati attānaṃ|

na taṃ viñ‍ñū pasaṃsanti, patthaddhamānasaṃ naraṃ||

1079.

 ‘‘yau  ca saiyyauhamasmīti, nāhaṃ saiyyauti vā pana|

hīnau taṃsadisau tīnauhaṃ sadisau (syā॰) vāti, vidhāsu na vikampati||

1080.

 ‘‘pañ‍ñavantaṃ  tathā tādiṃ, sīlaisu susamāhitaṃ|

caitausamathamanuttaṃ, tañ‍cai viñ‍ñū pasaṃsarai||

1081.

 ‘‘yassa  sabrahmacārīsu, gāravau nūpalabbhati|

ārakā hauti saddhammā, nabhatau puthavī yathā||

1082.

 ‘‘yaisañ‍ca hiri auttappaṃ, sadā sammā upaṭṭhitaṃ|

virūḷhabrahmacariyā tai, taisaṃ khīṇā punabbhavā||

1083.

 ‘‘uddhatau capalau bhikkhu, paṃsukūlaina pārutau|

kapīva sīhacammaina, na sau tainupasaubhati||

1084.

 ‘‘anuddhatau acapalau, nipakau saṃvutindriyau|

saubhati paṃsukūlaina, sīhauva girigabbharai||

1085.

 ‘‘aitai sambahulā daivā, iddhimantau yasassinau|

dasadaivasahassāni, sabbai tai brahmakāyikā||

1086.

 ‘‘dhammasaināpatiṃ vīraṃ, mahājhāyiṃ samāhitaṃ|

sāriputtaṃ namassantā, tiṭṭhanti pañ‍jalīkatā||

1087.

 ‘‘‘namau  tai purisājañ‍ña, namau tai purisuttama|

yassa tai nābhijānāma, yampi nissāya jhāyati jhāyasi (ka॰ aṭṭha॰)||

1088.

 ‘‘‘acchairaṃ vata buddhānaṃ, gambhīrau gaucarau sakau|

yai mayaṃ nābhijānāma, vālavaidhisamāgatā’||

1089.

 ‘‘taṃ tathā daivakāyaihi, pūjitaṃ pūjanārahaṃ|

sāriputtaṃ tadā disvā, kappinassa sitaṃ ahu||

1090.

 ‘‘yāvatā buddhakhaittamhi, ṭhapayitvā mahāmuniṃ|

dhutaguṇai visiṭṭhauhaṃ, sadisau mai na vij‍jati||

1091.

 ‘‘pariciṇṇau  mayā satthā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ|

auhitau garukau bhārau, natthi dāni punabbhavau||

1092.

 ‘‘na  cīvarai na sayanai, bhaujanai nupalimpati|

gautamau anappamaiyyau, muḷālapupphaṃ vimalaṃva|

ambunā naikkhammanin‍nau, tibhavābhinissaṭau||

1093.

 ‘‘satipaṭṭhānagīvau sau, saddhāhatthau mahāmuni|

pañ‍ñāsīsau mahāñāṇī, sadā carati nibbutau’’ti||

… mahākassapau thairau…|

cattālīsanipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

cattālīsanipātamhi, mahākassapasavhayau|

aikauva thairau gāthāyau, cattāsīla duvaipi cāti||


19. pañ‍ñāsanipātau

1. tālapuṭatthairagāthā

1094.

 ‘‘kadā  nuhaṃ pabbatakandarāsu, aikākiyau addutiyau vihassaṃ|

anic‍catau sabbabhavaṃ vipassaṃ, taṃ mai idaṃ taṃ nu kadā bhavissati||

1095.

 ‘‘kadā nuhaṃ bhin‍napaṭandharau muni, kāsāvavatthau  amamau nirāsau|

rāgañ‍ca dausañ‍ca tathaiva mauhaṃ, hantvā sukhī pavanagatau vihassaṃ||

1096.

 ‘‘kadā anic‍caṃ vadharauganīḷaṃ, kāyaṃ imaṃ mac‍cujarāyupaddutaṃ|

vipassamānau vītabhayau vihassaṃ, aikau vanai taṃ nu kadā bhavissati||

1097.

 ‘‘kadā nuhaṃ bhayajananiṃ dukhāvahaṃ, taṇhālataṃ bahuvidhānuvattaniṃ|

pañ‍ñāmayaṃ tikhiṇamasiṃ gahaitvā, chaitvā vasai tampi kadā bhavissati||

1098.

 ‘‘kadā nu pañ‍ñāmayamuggataijaṃ, satthaṃ isīnaṃ sahasādiyitvā|

māraṃ sasainaṃ sahasā bhañ‍jissaṃ, sīhāsanai taṃ nu kadā bhavissati||

1099.

 ‘‘kadā nuhaṃ sabbhi samāgamaisu, diṭṭhau bhavai dhammagarūhi tādibhi|

yāthāvadassīhi jitindriyaihi, padhāniyau  taṃ nu kadā bhavissati||

1100.

 ‘‘kadā nu maṃ tandi khudā pipāsā, vātātapā kīṭasarīsapā vā|

na bādhayissanti na taṃ giribbajai, atthatthiyaṃ taṃ nu kadā bhavissati||

1101.

 ‘‘kadā  nu khau yaṃ viditaṃ mahaisinā, cattāri sac‍cāni sududdasāni|

samāhitattau satimā agacchaṃ, pañ‍ñāya taṃ taṃ nu kadā bhavissati||

1102.

 ‘‘kadā nu rūpai amitai ca saddai, gandhai rasai phusitabbai ca dhammai|

ādittatauhaṃ samathaihi yuttau, pañ‍ñāya dacchaṃ tadidaṃ kadā mai||

1103.

 ‘‘kadā nuhaṃ dubbacanaina vuttau, tataunimittaṃ vimanau na haissaṃ|

athau pasatthaupi tataunimittaṃ, tuṭṭhau na haissaṃ tadidaṃ kadā mai||

1104.

 ‘‘kadā nu kaṭṭhai ca tiṇai latā ca, khandhai imaihaṃ amitai ca dhammai|

ajjhattikānaiva ca bāhirāni ca, samaṃ  tulaiyyaṃ tadidaṃ kadā mai||

1105.

 ‘‘kadā nu maṃ pāvusakālamaighau, navaina tauyaina sacīvaraṃ vanai|

isippayātamhi pathai vajantaṃ, auvassatai taṃ nu kadā bhavissati||

1106.

 ‘‘kadā  mayūrassa sikhaṇḍinau vanai, dijassa sutvā girigabbharai rutaṃ|

pac‍cuṭṭhahitvā amatassa pattiyā, saṃcintayai taṃ nu kadā bhavissati||

1107.

 ‘‘kadā nu gaṅgaṃ yamunaṃ sarassatiṃ, pātālakhittaṃ vaḷavāmukhañ‍ca balavāmukhañ‍ca (ka॰)|

asaj‍jamānau pataraiyyamiddhiyā, vibhiṃsanaṃ taṃ nu kadā bhavissati||

1108.

 ‘‘kadā  nu nāgauva asaṅgacārī, padālayai kāmaguṇaisu chandaṃ|

nibbaj‍jayaṃ sabbasubhaṃ nimittaṃ, jhānai yutau taṃ nu kadā bhavissati||

1109.

 ‘‘kadā  iṇaṭṭauva daliddakau daḷiddakau (sī॰) nidhiṃ, ārādhayitvā dhanikaihi pīḷitau|

tuṭṭhau bhavissaṃ  adhigamma sāsanaṃ, mahaisinau taṃ nu kadā bhavissati||

1110.

 ‘‘bahūni vassāni tayāmhi yācitau, ‘agāravāsaina alaṃ nu tai idaṃ’|

taṃ dāni maṃ pabbajitaṃ samānaṃ, kiṃkāraṇā citta tuvaṃ na yuñ‍jasi||

1111.

 ‘‘nanu ahaṃ citta tayāmhi yācitau, ‘giribbajai citrachadā vihaṅgamā’|

mahindaghausatthanitābhigaj‍jinau, tai taṃ ramaissanti vanamhi jhāyinaṃ||

1112.

 ‘‘kulamhi mittai ca piyai ca ñātakai, khiḍḍāratiṃ kāmaguṇañ‍ca laukai|

sabbaṃ pahāya imamajjhupāgatau, athaupi tvaṃ citta na mayha tussasi||

1113.

 ‘‘mamaiva aitaṃ na hi tvaṃ paraisaṃ, san‍nāhakālai paridaivitaina kiṃ|

sabbaṃ idaṃ calamiti paikkhamānau, abhinikkhamiṃ amatapadaṃ jigīsaṃ||

1114.

 ‘‘suyuttavādī dvipadānamuttamau, mahābhisak‍kau naradammasārathi sārathī (sī॰)|

‘cittaṃ  calaṃ mak‍kaṭasan‍nibhaṃ iti, avītarāgaina sudun‍nivārayaṃ’||

1115.

 ‘‘kāmā  hi citrā madhurā manauramā, aviddasū yattha sitā puthuj‍janā|

tai dukkhamicchanti punabbhavaisinau, cittaina nītā nirayai nirākatā||

1116.

 ‘‘‘mayūrakauñ‍cābhirutamhi kānanai, dīpīhi byagghaihi purakkhatau vasaṃ|

kāyai apaikkhaṃ jaha mā virādhaya’, itissu maṃ citta purai niyuñ‍jasi||

1117.

 ‘‘‘bhāvaihi  jhānāni ca indriyāni ca, balāni baujjhaṅgasamādhibhāvanā|

tissau ca vij‍jā phusa buddhasāsanai’, itissu maṃ citta purai niyuñ‍jasi||

1118.

 ‘‘‘bhāvaihi maggaṃ amatassa pattiyā, niyyānikaṃ sabbadukhakkhayaugadhaṃ|

aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ sabbakilaisasaudhanaṃ’, itissu maṃ citta purai niyuñ‍jasi||

1119.

 ‘‘‘dukkhanti khandhai paṭipassa yaunisau, yatau  ca dukkhaṃ samudaiti taṃ jaha|

idhaiva dukkhassa karauhi antaṃ’, itissu maṃ citta purai niyuñ‍jasi||

1120.

 ‘‘‘anic‍caṃ  dukkhanti vipassa yaunisau, suñ‍ñaṃ anattāti aghaṃ vadhanti ca|

manauvicārai uparundha caitasau’, itissu maṃ citta purai niyuñ‍jasi||

1121.

 ‘‘‘muṇḍau virūpau abhisāpamāgatau, kapālahatthauva kulaisu bhikkhasu|

yuñ‍jassu satthuvacanai mahaisinau’, itissu maṃ citta purai niyuñ‍jasi||

1122.

 ‘‘‘susaṃvutattau  visikhantarai caraṃ, kulaisu kāmaisu asaṅgamānasau|

candau yathā dausinapuṇṇamāsiyā’, itissu maṃ citta purai niyuñ‍jasi||

1123.

 ‘‘‘ārañ‍ñikau hauhi ca piṇḍapātikau, sausānikau hauhi ca paṃsukūlikau|

naisaj‍jikau hauhi sadā dhutai ratau’, itissu maṃ citta purai niyuñ‍jasi||

1124.

 ‘‘raupaitva  rukkhāni yathā phalaisī, mūlai taruṃ chaittu tamaiva icchasi|

tathūpamaṃ cittamidaṃ karausi, yaṃ maṃ anic‍camhi calai niyuñ‍jasi||

1125.

 ‘‘arūpa dūraṅgama aikacāri, na tai karissaṃ vacanaṃ idānihaṃ|

dukkhā hi kāmā kaṭukā mahabbhayā, nibbānamaivābhimanau carissaṃ||

1126.

 ‘‘nāhaṃ alakkhyā ahirik‍katāya vā, na cittahaitū na ca dūrakantanā|

ājīvahaitū ca ahaṃ na nikkhamiṃ, katau ca tai citta paṭissavau mayā||

1127.

 ‘‘‘appicchatā sappurisaihi vaṇṇitā, makkhappahānaṃ vūpasamau dukhassa’|

itissu  maṃ citta tadā niyuñ‍jasi, idāni tvaṃ gacchasi pubbaciṇṇaṃ||

1128.

 ‘‘taṇhā avij‍jā ca piyāpiyañ‍ca, subhāni rūpāni sukhā ca vaidanā|

manāpiyā kāmaguṇā ca vantā, vantai ahaṃ āvamituṃ na ussahai||

1129.

 ‘‘sabbattha  tai citta vacau kataṃ mayā, bahūsu jātīsu na maisi kaupitau|

ajjhattasambhavau katañ‍ñutāya tai, dukkhai ciraṃ saṃsaritaṃ tayā katai||

1130.

 ‘‘tvañ‍ñaiva nau citta karausi brāhmaṇau brāhmaṇai (sī॰), brāhmaṇaṃ (?) bhāvalaupa-tappadhānatā gahaitabbā, tvaṃ khattiyau rājadasī rājadisī (syā॰ ka॰) karausi|

vaissā ca suddā ca bhavāma aikadā, daivattanaṃ vāpi tavaiva vāhasā||

1131.

 ‘‘tavaiva haitū asurā bhavāmasai, tvaṃmūlakaṃ nairayikā bhavāmasai|

athau tiracchānagatāpi aikadā, paitattanaṃ vāpi tavaiva vāhasā||

1132.

 ‘‘nanu dubbhissasi maṃ punappunaṃ, muhuṃ muhuṃ cāraṇikaṃva dassayaṃ|

ummattakainaiva mayā palaubhasi, kiñ‍cāpi tai citta virādhitaṃ mayā||

1133.

 ‘‘idaṃ purai cittamacāri cārikaṃ, yainicchakaṃ yatthakāmaṃ yathāsukhaṃ|

tadaj‍jahaṃ niggahaissāmi yaunisau, hatthippabhin‍naṃ  viya aṅkusaggahau||

1134.

 ‘‘satthā  ca mai laukamimaṃ adhiṭṭhahi, anic‍catau addhuvatau asāratau|

pakkhanda maṃ citta jinassa sāsanai, tāraihi aughā mahatā suduttarā||

1135.

 ‘‘na tai idaṃ citta yathā purāṇakaṃ, nāhaṃ alaṃ tuyha vasai nivattituṃ vasaina vattituṃ (?)|

mahaisinau pabbajitaumhi sāsanai, na mādisā haunti vināsadhārinau||

1136.

 ‘‘nagā  samuddā saritā vasundharā, disā catassau vidisā adhau divā|

sabbai anic‍cā tibhavā upaddutā, kuhiṃ gatau citta sukhaṃ ramissasi||

1137.

 ‘‘dhitipparaṃ kiṃ mama citta kāhisi, na tai alaṃ citta vasānuvattakau|

na jātu bhastaṃ ubhataumukhaṃ chupai, dhiratthu pūraṃ nava sautasandaniṃ||

1138.

 ‘‘varāhaaiṇaiyyavigāḷhasaivitai, pabbhārakuṭṭai pakataiva sundarai|

navambunā pāvusasitthakānanai, tahiṃ  guhāgaihagatau ramissasi||

1139.

 ‘‘sunīlagīvā susikhā supaikhunā, sucittapattacchadanā vihaṅgamā|

sumañ‍jughausatthanitābhigaj‍jinau, tai taṃ ramaissanti vanamhi jhāyinaṃ||

1140.

 ‘‘vuṭṭhamhi daivai caturaṅgulai tiṇai, saṃpupphitai maighanibhamhi kānanai|

nagantarai viṭapisamau sayissaṃ, taṃ mai mudū haihiti tūlasan‍nibhaṃ||

1141.

 ‘‘tathā tu kassāmi yathāpi issarau, yaṃ labbhati tainapi hautu mai alaṃ|

na tāhaṃ kassāmi yathā atanditau, biḷārabhastaṃva yathā sumadditaṃ||

1142.

 ‘‘tathā tu kassāmi yathāpi issarau, yaṃ labbhati tainapi hautu mai alaṃ|

vīriyaina taṃ mayha vasānayissaṃ, gajaṃva mattaṃ kusalaṅkusaggahau||

1143.

 ‘‘tayā  sudantaina avaṭṭhitaina hi, hayaina  yauggācariyauva uj‍junā|

pahaumi maggaṃ paṭipaj‍jituṃ sivaṃ, cittānurakkhīhi sadā nisaivitaṃ||

1144.

 ‘‘ārammaṇai  taṃ balasā nibandhisaṃ, nāgaṃva thambhamhi daḷhāya raj‍juyā|

taṃ mai suguttaṃ satiyā subhāvitaṃ, anissitaṃ sabbabhavaisu haihisi||

1145.

 ‘‘pañ‍ñāya chaitvā vipathānusārinaṃ, yaugaina niggayha pathai nivaisiya|

disvā samudayaṃ vibhavañ‍ca sambhavaṃ, dāyādakau haihisi aggavādinau||

1146.

 ‘‘catubbipal‍lāsavasaṃ adhiṭṭhitaṃ, gāmaṇḍalaṃva parinaisi citta maṃ|

nanu nūna (sī॰) saṃyaujanabandhanacchidaṃ, saṃsaivasai kāruṇikaṃ mahāmuniṃ||

1147.

 ‘‘migau yathā sairi sucittakānanai, rammaṃ giriṃ pāvusaabbhamāliniṃ māliṃ (?)|

anākulai tattha nagai ramissaṃ ramissasi (syā॰ ka॰), asaṃsayaṃ citta parā bhavissasi||

1148.

 ‘‘yai  tuyha chandaina vasaina vattinau, narā ca nārī ca anubhaunti yaṃ sukhaṃ|

aviddasū māravasānuvattinau, bhavābhinandī tava citta sāvakā’’ti||

… tālapuṭau thairau…|

pañ‍ñāsanipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

pañ‍ñāsamhi nipātamhi, aikau tālapuṭau suci|

gāthāyau tattha pañ‍ñāsa, puna pañ‍ca ca uttarīti||


20. saṭṭhinipātau

1. mahāmauggal‍lānatthairagāthā

1149.

 ‘‘ārañ‍ñikā  piṇḍapātikā, uñchāpattāgatai ratā|

dālaimu mac‍cunau sainaṃ, ajjhattaṃ susamāhitā||

1150.

 ‘‘ārañ‍ñikā piṇḍapātikā, uñchāpattāgatai ratā|

dhunāma  mac‍cunau sainaṃ, naḷāgāraṃva kuñ‍jarau||

1151.

 ‘‘rukkhamūlikā sātatikā, uñchāpattāgatai ratā|

dālaimu mac‍cunau sainaṃ, ajjhattaṃ susamāhitā||

1152.

 ‘‘rukkhamūlikā sātatikā, uñchāpattāgatai ratā|

dhunāma mac‍cunau sainaṃ, naḷāgāraṃva kuñ‍jarau||

1153.

 ‘‘aṭṭhikaṅkalakuṭikai, maṃsanhārupasibbitai|

dhiratthu purai duggandhai, paragattai mamāyasai||

1154.

 ‘‘gūthabhastai tacaunaddhai, uragaṇḍipisācini|

nava sautāni tai kāyai, yāni sandanti sabbadā||

1155.

 ‘‘tava sarīraṃ navasautaṃ, duggandhakaraṃ paribandhaṃ|

bhikkhu parivaj‍jayatai taṃ, mīḷhaṃ ca yathā sucikāmau||

1156.

 ‘‘aivañ‍cai taṃ janau jañ‍ñā, yathā jānāmi taṃ ahaṃ|

ārakā parivaj‍jaiyya, gūthaṭṭhānaṃva pāvusai’’||

1157.

 ‘‘aivamaitaṃ mahāvīra, yathā samaṇa bhāsasi|

aittha caikai visīdanti, paṅkamhiva jaraggavau||

1158.

 ‘‘ākāsamhi haliddiyā, yau mañ‍ñaitha rajaitavai|

añ‍ñaina vāpi raṅgaina, vighātudayamaiva taṃ||

1159.

 ‘‘tadākāsasamaṃ cittaṃ, ajjhattaṃ susamāhitaṃ|

mā pāpacittai āsādi, aggikhandhaṃva pakkhimā||

1160.

 ‘‘passa cittakataṃ bimbaṃ, arukāyaṃ samussitaṃ|

āturaṃ bahusaṅkappaṃ, yassa natthi dhuvaṃ ṭhiti||

1161.

 ‘‘passa  cittakataṃ rūpaṃ, maṇinā kuṇḍalaina ca|

aṭṭhiṃ tacaina aunaddhaṃ, saha vatthaihi saubhati||

1162.

 ‘‘alattakakatā  pādā, mukhaṃ cuṇṇakamakkhitaṃ|

alaṃ bālassa mauhāya, nau ca pāragavaisinau||

1163.

 ‘‘aṭṭhapadakatā kaisā, naittā añ‍janamakkhitā|

alaṃ bālassa mauhāya, nau ca pāragavaisinau||

1164.

 ‘‘añ‍janīva navā cittā, pūtikāyau alaṅkatau|

alaṃ bālassa mauhāya, nau ca pāragavaisinau||

1165.

 ‘‘audahi migavau pāsaṃ, nāsadā vāguraṃ migau|

bhutvā nivāpaṃ gacchāma, kaddantai migabandhakai||

1166.

 ‘‘chin‍nau pāsau migavassa, nāsadā vāguraṃ migau|

bhutvā nivāpaṃ gacchāma, saucantai migaluddakai||

1167.

 ‘‘tadāsi yaṃ bhiṃsanakaṃ, tadāsi laumahaṃsanaṃ|

anaikākārasampan‍nai, sāriputtamhi nibbutai||

1168.

 dī॰ ni॰ 2.221, 272; saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.186; 2.143; apa॰ thaira 1.2.115; jā॰ 1.1.95 ‘‘anic‍cā  vata saṅkhārā uppādavaya dhamminau||

upaj‍jitvā nirujjhanti, taisaṃ vūpasamau sukhau||

1169.

 ‘‘sukhumaṃ tai paṭivijjhanti, vālaggaṃ usunā yathā|

yai pañ‍cakkhandhai passanti, paratau nau ca attatau||

1170.

 ‘‘yai ca passanti saṅkhārai, paratau nau ca attatau|

pac‍cabyādhiṃsu nipuṇaṃ, vālaggaṃ usunā yathā||

1171.

 saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.21, 97 ‘‘sattiyā viya aumaṭṭhau, ḍayhamānauva matthakai|

kāmarāgappahānāya, satau bhikkhu paribbajai||

1172.

 saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.21, 97‘‘sattiyā viya aumaṭṭhau, ḍayhamānauva matthakai|

bhavarāgappahānāya, satau bhikkhu paribbajai’’||

1173.

 ‘‘cauditau bhāvitattaina, sarīrantimadhārinā|

migāramātupāsādaṃ, pādaṅguṭṭhaina kampayiṃ||

1174.

 ‘‘nayidaṃ sithilamārabbha, nayidaṃ appaina thāmasā|

nibbānamadhigantabbaṃ, sabbagantha-pamaucanaṃ||

1175.

 ‘‘ayañ‍ca  daharau bhikkhu, ayamuttamapaurisau|

dhāraiti antimaṃ daihaṃ, jaitvā māraṃ savāhiniṃ savāhanaṃ (ka॰)||

1176.

 ‘‘vivaramanupabhanti  vij‍jutā, vaibhārassa ca paṇḍavassa ca|

nagavivaragatau jhāyati, puttau appaṭimassa tādinau||

1177.

 ‘‘upasantau uparatau, pantasaināsanau muni|

dāyādau  buddhasaiṭṭhassa, brahmunā abhivanditau||

1178.

 ‘‘upasantaṃ uparataṃ, pantasaināsanaṃ muniṃ|

dāyādaṃ buddhasaiṭṭhassa, vanda brāhmaṇa kassapaṃ||

1179.

 ‘‘yau ca jātisataṃ gacchai, sabbā brāhmaṇajātiyau|

sauttiyau vaidasampan‍nau, manussaisu punappunaṃ||

1180.

 ‘‘ajjhāyakaupi cai assa, tiṇṇaṃ vaidāna pāragū|

aitassa vandanāyaitaṃ, kalaṃ nāgghati sauḷasiṃ||

1181.

 ‘‘yau sau aṭṭha vimaukkhāni, puraibhattaṃ aphassayi apassayi (sī॰ ka॰), aphussayi (syā॰)|

anulaumaṃ paṭilaumaṃ, tatau piṇḍāya gacchati||

1182.

 ‘‘tādisaṃ bhikkhuṃ māsādi mā hani (sī॰), māttānaṃ khaṇi brāhmaṇa|

abhippasādaihi manaṃ, arahantamhi tādinai|

khippaṃ pañ‍jalikau vanda, mā tai vijaṭi matthakaṃ||

1183.

 ‘‘naisau passati saddhammaṃ, saṃsāraina purakkhatau|

adhaugamaṃ jimhapathaṃ, kummaggamanudhāvati||

1184.

 ‘‘kimīva mīḷhasal‍littau, saṅkhārai adhimucchitau|

pagāḷhau lābhasak‍kārai, tucchau gacchati pauṭṭhilau||

1185.

 ‘‘imañ‍ca passa āyantaṃ, sāriputtaṃ sudassanaṃ|

vimuttaṃ ubhataubhāgai, ajjhattaṃ susamāhitaṃ||

1186.

 ‘‘visal‍laṃ  khīṇasaṃyaugaṃ, taivij‍jaṃ mac‍cuhāyinaṃ|

dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ  manussānaṃ, puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ anuttaraṃ||

1187.

 ‘‘aitai sambahulā daivā, iddhimantau yasassinau|

dasa daivasahassāni, sabbai brahmapurauhitā|

mauggal‍lānaṃ namassantā, tiṭṭhanti pañ‍jalīkatā||

1188.

 ‘‘‘namau  tai purisājañ‍ña, namau tai purisuttama|

yassa tai āsavā khīṇā, dakkhiṇaiyyausi mārisa’||

1189.

 ‘‘pūjitau  naradaivaina, uppan‍nau maraṇābhibhū|

puṇḍarīkaṃva tauyaina, saṅkhārainupalippati||

1190.

 ‘‘yassa muhuttaina sahassadhā laukau, saṃviditau sabrahmakappau vasi|

iddhiguṇai cutupapātai kālai, passati daivatā sa bhikkhu||

1191.

 ‘‘sāriputtauva pañ‍ñāya, sīlaina upasamaina ca|

yaupi pāraṅgatau bhikkhu, aitāvaparamau siyā||

1192.

 ‘‘kauṭisatasahassassa, attabhāvaṃ khaṇaina nimminai|

ahaṃ vikubbanāsu kusalau, vasībhūtaumhi iddhiyā||

1193.

 ‘‘samādhivij‍jāvasipāramīgatau, mauggal‍lānagauttau asitassa sāsanai|

dhīrau samucchindi samāhitindriyau, nāgau  yathā pūtilataṃva bandhanaṃ||

1194.

 ‘‘pariciṇṇau mayā satthā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ|

auhitau garukau bhārau, bhavanaitti samūhatā||

1195.

 ‘‘yassa catthāya pabbajitau, agārasmānagāriyaṃ|

sau mai atthau anuppattau, sabbasaṃyaujanakkhayau||

1196.

 ma॰ ni॰ 1.513 ‘‘kīdisau nirayau āsi, yattha dussī apac‍catha|

vidhuraṃ sāvakamāsaj‍ja, kakusandhañ‍ca brāhmaṇaṃ||

1197.

 ‘‘sataṃ āsi ayausaṅkū, sabbai pac‍cattavaidanā|

īdisau nirayau āsi, yattha dussī apac‍catha|

vidhuraṃ sāvakamāsaj‍ja, kakusandhañ‍ca brāhmaṇaṃ||

1198.

 ‘‘yau aitamabhijānāti, bhikkhu buddhassa sāvakau|

tādisaṃ bhikkhumāsaj‍ja, kaṇha dukkhaṃ nigacchasi||

1199.

 ‘‘majjhaisarasmiṃ  sarassa (sī॰), sāgarasmiṃ (ka॰) tiṭṭhanti, vimānā kappaṭhāyinau|

vaiḷuriyavaṇṇā rucirā, ac‍cimantau pabhassarā|

accharā tattha nac‍canti, puthu nānattavaṇṇiyau||

1200.

 ‘‘yau  aitamabhijānāti…pai॰… kaṇha dukkhaṃ nigacchasi||

1201.

 ‘‘yau vai buddhaina cauditau, bhikkhusaṅghassa paikkhatau|

migāramātupāsādaṃ, pādaṅguṭṭhaina kampayi||

1202.

 ‘‘yau aitamabhijānāti…pai॰… kaṇha dukkhaṃ nigacchasi||

1203.

 ‘‘yau  vaijayantapāsādaṃ, pādaṅguṭṭhaina kampayi|

iddhibalainupatthaddhau, saṃvaijaisi ca daivatā||

1204.

 ‘‘yau aitamabhijānāti…pai॰… kaṇha dukkhaṃ nigacchasi||

1204.

 ‘‘yau aitamabhijānāti…pai॰… kaṇha dukkhaṃ nigacchasi||

1205.

 ‘‘yau vaijayantapāsādai, sak‍kaṃ sau paripucchati|

api āvusau jānāsi, taṇhakkhayavimuttiyau|

tassa sak‍kau viyākāsi, pañhaṃ puṭṭhau yathātathaṃ||

1206.

 ‘‘yau aitamabhijānāti…pai॰… kaṇha dukkhaṃ nigacchasi||

1207.

 ‘‘yau brahmānaṃ paripucchati, sudhammāyaṃ ṭhitau sudhammāyā’bhitau (syā॰) sabhaṃ|

aj‍jāpi tyāvusau sā diṭṭhi, yā tai diṭṭhi purai ahu|

passasi vītivattantaṃ, brahmalaukai pabhassaraṃ||

1208.

 ‘‘tassa brahmā viyākāsi, pañhaṃ puṭṭhau yathātathaṃ|

na mai mārisa sā diṭṭhi, yā mai diṭṭhi purai ahu||

1209.

 ‘‘passāmi vītivattantaṃ, brahmalaukai pabhassaraṃ|

sauhaṃ aj‍ja kathaṃ vaj‍jaṃ, ahaṃ nic‍caumhi sassatau||

1210.

 ‘‘yau aitamabhijānāti…pai॰… kaṇha dukkhaṃ nigacchasi||

1211.

 ‘‘yau mahānairunau kūṭaṃ, vimaukkhaina aphassayi apassayi (sī॰ ka॰)|

vanaṃ pubbavidaihānaṃ, yai ca bhūmisayā narā||

1212.

 ‘‘yau aitamabhijānāti, bhikkhu buddhassa sāvakau|

tādisaṃ bhikkhumāsaj‍ja, kaṇha dukkhaṃ nigacchasi||

1213.

 ‘‘na vai aggi caitayati, ahaṃ bālaṃ ḍahāmīti|

bālauva jalitaṃ aggiṃ, āsaj‍ja naṃ paḍayhati||

1214.

 ‘‘aivamaiva tuvaṃ māra, āsaj‍ja naṃ tathāgataṃ|

sayaṃ ḍahissasi attānaṃ, bālau aggiṃva samphusaṃ||

1215.

 ‘‘apuñ‍ñaṃ  pasavī mārau, āsaj‍ja naṃ tathāgataṃ|

kiṃ nu mañ‍ñasi pāpima, na mai pāpaṃ vipac‍cati||

1216.

 ‘‘karatau tai cīyatai miyyatai (sabbattha) ma॰ ni॰ 1.513 passitabbaṃ pāpaṃ, cirarattāya antaka|

māra nibbinda buddhamhā, āsaṃ mākāsi bhikkhusu||

1217.

 ‘‘iti  māraṃ ataj‍jaisi, bhikkhu bhaisakaḷāvanai|

tatau sau dummanau yakkhau, tatthaivantaradhāyathā’’ti||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau mahāmauggalānau (ka॰) thairau gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

saṭṭhinipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

saṭṭhikamhi nipātamhi, mauggal‍lānau mahiddhikau|

aikauva thairagāthāyau, aṭṭhasaṭṭhi bhavanti tāti||


21. mahānipātau

1. vaṅgīsatthairagāthā

1218.

 ‘‘nikkhantaṃ  vata maṃ santaṃ, agārasmānagāriyaṃ|

vitak‍kā upadhāvanti, pagabbhā kaṇhatau imai||

1219.

 ‘‘uggaputtā mahissāsā, sikkhitā daḷhadhamminau daḷhadhanvinau (sī॰ aṭṭha॰)|

samantā parikiraiyyuṃ, sahassaṃ apalāyinaṃ||

1220.

 ‘‘sacaipi aittakā aitatau (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.209) bhiyyau, āgamissanti itthiyau|

naiva maṃ byādhayissanti byāthayissanti (?), dhammai samhi dhammaisvamhi (syā॰ ka॰) patiṭṭhitau||

1221.

 ‘‘sakkhī  hi mai sutaṃ aitaṃ, buddhassādic‍cabandhunau|

nibbānagamanaṃ maggaṃ, tattha mai niratau manau||

1222.

 ‘‘aivaṃ cai maṃ viharantaṃ, pāpima upagacchasi|

tathā mac‍cu karissāmi, na mai maggampi dakkhasi||

1223.

 ‘‘aratiñ‍ca aratiṃ (bahūsu) ratiñ‍ca pahāya, sabbasau gaihasitañ‍ca vitak‍kaṃ|

vanathaṃ na karaiyya kuhiñ‍ci, nibbanathau avanathau sa nibbanathau aratau sa hi (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.210) bhikkhu||

1224.

 ‘‘yamidha pathaviñ‍ca vaihāsaṃ, rūpagataṃ jagataugadhaṃ kiñ‍ci|

parijīyati sabbamanic‍caṃ, aivaṃ samaic‍ca caranti mutattā||

1225.

 ‘‘upadhīsu janā gadhitāsai, diṭṭhasutai diṭṭhai sutai (sī॰) paṭighai ca mutai ca|

aittha vinaudaya chandamanaijau, yau haittha na limpati muni tamāhu taṃ munimāhu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.210)||

1226.

 ‘‘atha  saṭṭhisitā savitak‍kā, puthuj‍janatāya puthū janatāya (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.210) adhammā niviṭṭhā|

na ca vaggagatassa kuhiñ‍ci, nau pana duṭṭhul‍lagāhī duṭṭhul‍labhāṇī (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.210) sa bhikkhu||

1227.

 ‘‘dabbau  cirarattasamāhitau, akuhakau nipakau apihālu|

santaṃ padaṃ ajjhagamā muni, paṭic‍ca parinibbutau kaṅkhati kālaṃ||

1228.

 ‘‘mānaṃ pajahassu gautama, mānapathañ‍ca jahassu asaisaṃ|

mānapathamhi sa mucchitau, vippaṭisārīhuvā cirarattaṃ||

1229.

 ‘‘makkhaina makkhitā pajā, mānahatā nirayaṃ papatanti|

saucanti janā cirarattaṃ, mānahatā nirayaṃ upapan‍nā||

1230.

 ‘‘na  hi saucati bhikkhu kadāci, maggajinau sammā paṭipan‍nau|

kittiñ‍ca  sukhañ‍cānubhauti, dhammadasauti tamāhu tathattaṃ||

1231.

 ‘‘tasmā akhilau idha akhilau (sī॰), akhilaudha (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.211) padhānavā, nīvaraṇāni pahāya visuddhau|

mānañ‍ca pahāya asaisaṃ, vij‍jāyantakarau samitāvī||

1232.

 ‘‘kāmarāgaina ḍayhāmi, cittaṃ mai pariḍayhati|

sādhu nibbāpanaṃ brūhi, anukampāya gautama||

1233.

 ‘‘sañ‍ñāya  vipariyaisā, cittaṃ tai pariḍayhati|

nimittaṃ parivaj‍jaihi, subhaṃ rāgūpasaṃhitaṃ ( ) (saṅkhārai paratau passa, dukkhatau mā ca attatau| nibbāpaihi mahārāgaṃ, mā dayhitthau punappunaṃ|) (sī॰ saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.212) uddānagāthāyaṃ aikasattatītisaṅkhyā ca, thairagāthāṭṭhakathā ca passitabbā||

1234.

 ‘‘asubhāya cittaṃ bhāvaihi, aikaggaṃ susamāhitaṃ|

sati kāyagatā tyatthu, nibbidābahulau bhava||

1235.

 ‘‘animittañ‍ca bhāvaihi, mānānusayamuj‍jaha|

tatau mānābhisamayā, upasantau carissasi||

1236.

 ‘‘tamaiva vācaṃ bhāsaiyya, yāyattānaṃ na tāpayai|

parai ca na vihiṃsaiyya, sā vai vācā subhāsitā||

1237.

 ‘‘piyavācamaiva bhāsaiyya, yā vācā paṭinanditā|

yaṃ anādāya pāpāni, paraisaṃ bhāsatai piyaṃ||

1238.

 ‘‘sac‍caṃ vai amatā vācā, aisa dhammau sanantanau|

sac‍cai atthai ca dhammai ca, āhu santau patiṭṭhitā||

1239.

 ‘‘yaṃ buddhau bhāsati vācaṃ, khaimaṃ nibbānapattiyā|

dukkhassantakiriyāya, sā vai vācānamuttamā||

1240.

 ‘‘gambhīrapañ‍ñau maidhāvī, maggāmaggassa kauvidau|

sāriputtau  mahāpañ‍ñau, dhammaṃ daisaiti bhikkhunaṃ||

1241.

 ‘‘saṅkhittainapi daisaiti, vitthārainapi bhāsati|

sālikāyiva nigghausau, paṭibhānaṃ udiyyati udīrayi (sī॰), udīyyati (syā॰), udayyati (?) uṭṭhahatīti taṃsaṃvaṇṇanā||

1242.

 ‘‘tassa  taṃ daisayantassa, suṇanti madhuraṃ giraṃ|

saraina rajanīyaina, savanīyaina vaggunā|

udaggacittā muditā, sautaṃ audhainti bhikkhavau||

1243.

 ‘‘aj‍ja pan‍narasai visuddhiyā, bhikkhū pañ‍casatā samāgatā|

saṃyaujanabandhanacchidā, anīghā khīṇapunabbhavā isī||

1244.

 ‘‘cak‍kavattī  yathā rājā, amac‍caparivāritau|

samantā anupariyaiti, sāgarantaṃ mahiṃ imaṃ||

1245.

 ‘‘aivaṃ  vijitasaṅgāmaṃ, satthavāhaṃ anuttaraṃ|

sāvakā payirupāsanti, taivij‍jā mac‍cuhāyinau||

1246.

 ‘‘sabbai bhagavatau puttā, palāpaittha na vij‍jati|

taṇhāsal‍lassa hantāraṃ, vandai ādic‍cabandhunaṃ||

1247.

 ‘‘parausahassaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, sugataṃ payirupāsati|

daisaintaṃ virajaṃ dhammaṃ, nibbānaṃ akutaubhayaṃ||

1248.

 ‘‘suṇanti dhammaṃ vimalaṃ, sammāsambuddhadaisitaṃ|

saubhati vata sambuddhau, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhatau||

1249.

 ‘‘‘nāganāmau’si bhagavā, isīnaṃ isisattamau|

mahāmaighauva hutvāna, sāvakai abhivassasi||

1250.

 ‘‘divā  vihārā nikkhamma, satthudassanakamyatā|

sāvakau tai mahāvīra, pādai vandati vaṅgisau||

1251.

 ‘‘ummaggapathaṃ mārassa, abhibhuyya carati pabhij‍ja khīlāni|

taṃ passatha bandhapamuñ‍cakaraṃ, asitaṃva bhāgasau pavibhaj‍ja||

1252.

 ‘‘aughassa hi nitaraṇatthaṃ, anaikavihitaṃ maggaṃ akkhāsi|

tasmiñ‍ca amatai akkhātai, dhammadasā ṭhitā asaṃhīrā||

1253.

 ‘‘paj‍jautakarau ativijjha ativijjha dhammaṃ (sī॰), sabbaṭhitīnaṃ atik‍kamamaddasa atik‍kamamadda (sī॰ ka॰)|

ñatvā ca sacchikatvā ca, aggaṃ sau daisayi dasaddhānaṃ||

1254.

 ‘‘aivaṃ sudaisitai dhammai, kau pamādau vijānataṃ dhammaṃ|

tasmā hi tassa bhagavatau sāsanai, appamattau sadā namassamanusikkhai||

1255.

 ‘‘buddhānubuddhau yau thairau, kauṇḍañ‍ñau tibbanik‍kamau|

lābhī sukhavihārānaṃ, vivaikānaṃ abhiṇhasau||

1256.

 ‘‘yaṃ  sāvakaina pattabbaṃ, satthu sāsanakārinā|

sabbassa taṃ anuppattaṃ, appamattassa sikkhatau||

1257.

 ‘‘mahānubhāvau taivij‍jau, caitaupariyakauvidau|

kauṇḍañ‍ñau buddhadāyādau, pādai vandati satthunau||

1258.

 ‘‘nagassa  passai āsīnaṃ, muniṃ dukkhassa pāraguṃ|

sāvakā payirupāsanti, taivij‍jā mac‍cuhāyinau||

1259.

 ‘‘caitasā tai caitasā (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.218) anupariyaiti, mauggal‍lānau mahiddhikau|

cittaṃ  naisaṃ samanvaisaṃ saman‍naisaṃ (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.218), vippamuttaṃ nirūpadhiṃ||

1260.

 ‘‘aivaṃ sabbaṅgasampan‍naṃ, muniṃ dukkhassa pāraguṃ|

anaikākārasampan‍naṃ, payirupāsanti gautamaṃ||

1261.

 ‘‘candau yathā vigatavalāhakai nabhai, viraucati vītamalauva bhāṇumā|

aivampi aṅgīrasa tvaṃ mahāmuni, atiraucasi yasasā sabbalaukaṃ||

1262.

 ‘‘kāvaiyyamattā vicarimha pubbai, gāmā gāmaṃ purā puraṃ|

athaddasāma sambuddhaṃ, sabbadhammāna pāraguṃ||

1263.

 ‘‘sau mai dhammamadaisaisi, muni dukkhassa pāragū|

dhammaṃ sutvā pasīdimha, saddhā addhā (sī॰ aṭṭha॰) nau udapaj‍jatha||

1264.

 ‘‘tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā, khandhai āyatanāni ca|

dhātuyau ca viditvāna, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ||

1265.

 ‘‘bahūnaṃ vata atthāya, uppaj‍janti tathāgatā|

itthīnaṃ purisānañ‍ca, yai tai sāsanakārakā||

1266.

 ‘‘taisaṃ khau vata atthāya, baudhimajjhagamā muni|

bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnañ‍ca, yai nirāmagataddasā||

1267.

 ‘‘sudaisitā cakkhumatā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

cattāri ariyasac‍cāni, anukampāya pāṇinaṃ||

1268.

 ‘‘dukkhaṃ  dukkhasamuppādaṃ, dukkhassa ca atik‍kamaṃ|

ariyaṃ  caṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ, dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ||

1269.

 ‘‘aivamaitai tathā vuttā, diṭṭhā mai tai yathā tathā|

sadatthau mai anuppattau, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

1270.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi, mama buddhassa santikai|

suvibhattaisu savibhattaisu (sī॰ ka॰) dhammaisu, yaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ tadupāgamiṃ||

1271.

 ‘‘abhiñ‍ñāpāramippattau, sautadhātu visaudhitā|

taivij‍jau iddhipattaumhi, caitaupariyakauvidau||

1272.

 ‘‘pucchāmi  satthāramanaumapañ‍ñaṃ, diṭṭhaiva dhammai yau vicikicchānaṃ chaittā|

aggāḷavai kālamakāsi bhikkhu, ñātau yasassī abhinibbutattau||

1273.

 ‘‘nigraudhakappau  iti tassa nāmaṃ, tayā kataṃ bhagavā brāhmaṇassa|

sau taṃ namassaṃ acari mutyapaikhau, āraddhavīriyau daḷhadhammadassī||

1274.

 ‘‘taṃ sāvakaṃ sak‍ka mayampi sabbai, añ‍ñātumicchāma samantacakkhu|

samavaṭṭhitā nau savanāya sautā haituṃ (sī॰ syā॰) suttanipātaṭṭhakathā passitabbā, tuvaṃ nau satthā tvamanuttarausi’’||

1275.

 chinda  nau vicikicchaṃ brūhi maitaṃ, parinibbutaṃ vaidaya bhūripañ‍ña|

majjhaiva nau bhāsa samantacakkhu, sak‍kauva daivāna sahassanaittau||

1276.

 ‘‘yai kaici ganthā idha mauhamaggā, añ‍ñāṇapakkhā vicikicchaṭhānā|

tathāgataṃ patvā na tai bhavanti, cakkhuñhi aitaṃ paramaṃ narānaṃ||

1277.

 ‘‘nau  cai hi jātu purisau kilaisai, vātau yathā abbhaghanaṃ vihānai|

tamauvassa nivutau sabbalaukau, jautimantaupi na pabhāsaiyyuṃ na jautimantaupi narā tapaiyyuṃ (su॰ ni॰ 350)||

1278.

 ‘‘dhīrā ca paj‍jautakarā bhavanti, taṃ taṃ ahaṃ vīra tathaiva mañ‍ñai|

vipassinaṃ jānamupāgamimha, parisāsu nau āvikarauhi kappaṃ||

1279.

 ‘‘khippaṃ giraṃ airaya vaggu vagguṃ, haṃsauva paggayha saṇikaṃ nikūja|

bindussaraina  suvikappitaina, sabbaiva tai uj‍jugatā suṇauma||

1280.

 ‘‘pahīnajātimaraṇaṃ  asaisaṃ, niggayha dhaunaṃ vadaissāmi paṭivaidiyāmi (sī॰ ka॰) dhammaṃ|

na kāmakārau hi hauti (sī॰ ka॰) puthuj‍janānaṃ, saṅkhaiyyakārau ca va (bahūsu) tathāgatānaṃ||

1281.

 ‘‘sampan‍navaiyyākaraṇaṃ tavaidaṃ, samuj‍jupañ‍ñassa samuggahītaṃ|

ayamañ‍jali pacchimau suppaṇāmitau, mā mauhayī jānamanaumapañ‍ña||

1282.

 ‘‘parauparaṃ ariyadhammaṃ viditvā, mā mauhayī jānamanaumavīriya|

vāriṃ yathā ghammani ghammatattau, vācābhikaṅkhāmi sutaṃ pavassa||

1283.

 ‘‘yadatthikaṃ brahmacariyaṃ acarī, kappāyanau kac‍cissataṃ amaughaṃ|

nibbāyi sau ādu saupādisaisau anupādisaisā (sī॰), anupādisaisau (ka॰), yathā vimuttau ahu taṃ suṇauma||

1284.

 ‘‘‘acchaicchi  taṇhaṃ idha nāmarūpai,

(iti bhagavā) kaṇhassa sautaṃ dīgharattānusayitaṃ|

atāri jātiṃ maraṇaṃ asaisaṃ’, ic‍cabravi  bhagavā pañ‍casaiṭṭhau||

1285.

 ‘‘aisa sutvā pasīdāmi, vacau tai isisattama|

amaughaṃ kira mai puṭṭhaṃ, na maṃ vañ‍caisi brāhmaṇau||

1286.

 ‘‘yathā vādī tathā kārī, ahu buddhassa sāvakau|

acchaicchi mac‍cunau jālaṃ, tataṃ māyāvinau daḷhaṃ||

1287.

 ‘‘addasa  bhagavā ādiṃ, upādānassa kappiyau|

ac‍cagā  vata kappānau, mac‍cudhaiyyaṃ suduttaraṃ||

1288.

 ‘‘taṃ daivadaivaṃ vandāmi, puttaṃ tai dvipaduttama|

anujātaṃ mahāvīraṃ, nāgaṃ nāgassa aurasa’’nti||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vaṅgīsau thairau gāthāyau

abhāsitthāti|

mahānipātau niṭṭhitau|


tatruddānaṃ –

sattatimhi nipātamhi, vaṅgīsau paṭibhāṇavā|

aikauva thairau natthañ‍ñau, gāthāyau aikasattatīti||

niṭṭhitā thairagāthāyau|

tatruddānaṃ –

sahassaṃ haunti tā gāthā, tīṇi saṭṭhisatāni ca|

thairā ca dvai satā saṭṭhi, cattārau ca pakāsitā||

sīhanādaṃ naditvāna, buddhaputtā anāsavā|

khaimantaṃ pāpuṇitvāna, aggikhandhāva nibbutāti||

thairagāthāpāḷi niṭṭhitā|
